package main

//必须用main包
import (
        "bytes"
        "flag"
        "fmt"
        "io"
        "log"
        "net/http"
        "os"
        "os/exec"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "hahacking/utils"
)

const (
        serverUser  = "root"            // 修改为服务器用户名
        serverHost  = "api.noso.cafe"   // 修改为服务器IP
        remotePath  = "/home2/noso-api" // 服务器上应用的路径
        serviceName = "nosoapi"         // systemd 服务名
        // API 检查配置
        apiBaseURL = "https://api.noso.cafe"
        apiTimeout = 10 * time.Second
)

/**
 * 使用方法：
 * 部署新版本：go run deploy/deploy.go
 * 回滚到上一个版本：go run deploy/deploy.go -r 1
 *  ** -r 0 表示回滚到当前最新版本
 * 显示日志：go run deploy/deploy.go -l
 * 运行测试：go run deploy/deploy.go -t
 * 构建应用：go run deploy/deploy.go -b
 * 冒烟测试：go run deploy/deploy.go -st
 *
 * 注意：日志轮转和清理现在完全自动化，无需手动干预
 * - 自动轮转：每小时检查文件大小和时间
 * - 自动清理：每天凌晨2点清理旧日志
 * - 配置：通过 config/env.json 中的 log 部分配置
 */
var ReleaseFile string
var ReleaseNo string      //build no
var ReleaseVersion string //版本号

func main() {
        ReleaseFile = serviceName

        // 解析命令行参数
        rollback := flag.Int("r", -1, "回滚到第几个版本（1表示上一个版本,0表示当前最新版本）")
        runtestsOnly := flag.Bool("t", false, "是否只运行测试")
        runtests := flag.String("tc", "all", "是否运行测试(all|model|service|common|utils|scheduler|middleware)")
        runbuild := flag.Bool("b", false, "是否运行构建")
        smokeTest := flag.Bool("st", false, "是否运行冒烟测试")
        showlog := flag.Bool("l", false, "是否显示日志")
        releaseFlag := flag.String("release", "patch", "是否发布版本(patch|minor|major)")
        flag.Parse()

        if *rollback >= 0 {
                Rollback(*rollback)
                return
        }

        if *runtestsOnly {
                RunTests(*runtests)
                return
        }

        if *runbuild {
                BuildApp()
                return
        }
        if *smokeTest {
                SmokeTest()
                return
        }
        if *showlog {
                CheckLog()
                return
        }
        // 获取版本号
        vm := utils.NewVersionManager()
        ReleaseVersion = vm.GetVersion()

        if *releaseFlag != "" {
                newVersion, err := vm.IncrementVersion(*releaseFlag)
                if err != nil {
                        fmt.Printf("❌ 版本增加失败: %v\n", err)
                        os.Exit(1)
                }
                ReleaseVersion = newVersion

                // 保存新版本号到文件
                if err := vm.SetVersion(ReleaseVersion); err != nil {
                        fmt.Printf("⚠️  保存版本号失败: %v\n", err)
                }

                // 创建 Git 标签
                if err := vm.CreateGitTag(ReleaseVersion); err != nil {
                        fmt.Printf("⚠️  创建 Git 标签失败: %v\n", err)
                } else {
                        fmt.Printf("🏷️  Git 标签已创建: v%s\n", ReleaseVersion)
                }

                fmt.Printf("📦 版本已更新为: %s\n", ReleaseVersion)
        }
        fmt.Println("🚀 开始构建、上传并重启 Go Web App...")
        var sshCommand string
        var output string
        var err error

        //step 0: 运行测试
        if err := RunTests(*runtests); err != nil {
                // fmt.Println("❌ 测试失败:", err)
                return
        }

        // Step 1: 编译 Go Web 应用
        if err := BuildApp(); err != nil {
                // fmt.Println("❌ 构建失败:", err)
                return
        }

        // Step 2: 上传到服务器
        UploadBinary()
        // 上传 config 目录下的所有文件
        configPath := "config/*"
        remoteConfigPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s@%s:%s/config/", serverUser, serverHost, remotePath)
        fmt.Println("📤 正在上传 config 目录下的所有文件到服务器:", remoteConfigPath)
        if err := runCommand(fmt.Sprintf("scp -r %s %s", configPath, remoteConfigPath)); err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 上传 config 文件失败:", err)
                return
        }
        fmt.Println("✅ 上传成功!")

        // 2.1 建立软链接
        releasePath := fmt.Sprintf("%s/releases", remotePath)
        sshCommand = fmt.Sprintf(
                "sudo ln -sf %s/%s %s/%s",
                releasePath, ReleaseFile, remotePath, serviceName,
        )
        fmt.Println("🔗 正在创建软链接:", sshCommand)
        if output, err = runRemoteCommand(sshCommand); err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 创建软链接失败:", err)
                return
        }
        fmt.Println(output)
        fmt.Println("✅ 软链接创建成功!")

        sshCommand = fmt.Sprintf("sudo systemctl stop %s", serviceName)
        if output, err = runRemoteCommand(sshCommand); err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 服务停止失败:", err)
                return
        }
        fmt.Println(output)
        fmt.Println("🔄 服务停止成功!")

        // Step 3: 远程重启服务
        fmt.Println("🔄 正在重启服务器上的服务:", serviceName)

        reloadCmd := fmt.Sprintf(
                "chmod +x %s/%s && sudo systemctl restart %s && sudo systemctl status %s --no-pager",
                releasePath, ReleaseFile, serviceName, serviceName,
        )
        if output, err = runRemoteCommand(reloadCmd); err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 服务重启失败:", err)
                return
        }

        fmt.Println(output)
        fmt.Println("✅ 远程服务已成功重启!")

        // Step 4: 清理旧版本文件
        CleanupOldVersions()
        //清理本地的旧版本文件
        CleanupOldLocalVersions()

        fmt.Println("🎉 部署完成!")

        fmt.Println("⏳ 等待服务启动...")
        time.Sleep(5 * time.Second)
        CheckLog()
        if err := SmokeTest(); err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 冒烟测试失败:", err)
                return
        }

}
func CheckLog() {
        fmt.Println("🔨 正在检查日志...")
        cmd := fmt.Sprintf("tail -n 20 %s/logs/app.log", remotePath)
        // cmd := fmt.Sprintf("less %s/logs/app.log ", remotePath)
        output, err := runRemoteCommand(cmd)
        if err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 检查日志失败:", err)
                return
        }
        fmt.Println(output)
        fmt.Println("✅ 日志检查完成!")
}

func SmokeTest() error {
        fmt.Println("🔨 正在运行冒烟测试...")
        if err := checkHTTPAPI(); err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ HTTP API 检查失败:", err)
                return err
        }
        fmt.Println("✅ HTTP API 检查通过!")
        fmt.Println("🎉 冒烟测试完成!")
        return nil
}

// checkHTTPAPI 检查 HTTP API 是否正常工作
func checkHTTPAPI() error {
        // 定义要检查的 API 端点
        endpoints := []struct {
                name   string
                url    string
                method string
        }{
                {"健康检查", apiBaseURL + "/ping", "GET"},
                // 可以根据需要添加更多端点
        }

        client := &http.Client{
                Timeout: apiTimeout,
        }

        for _, endpoint := range endpoints {
                fmt.Printf("🔍 检查 %s: %s\n", endpoint.name, endpoint.url)

                req, err := http.NewRequest(endpoint.method, endpoint.url, nil)
                if err != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("创建请求失败 %s: %v", endpoint.name, err)
                }

                // 添加必要的请求头
                req.Header.Set("User-Agent", "Deploy-SmokeTest/1.0")
                req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/json")

                resp, err := client.Do(req)
                if err != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("请求失败 %s: %v", endpoint.name, err)
                }
                defer resp.Body.Close()

                // 读取响应体
                body, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
                if err != nil {
                        return fmt.Errorf("读取响应失败 %s: %v", endpoint.name, err)
                }

                // 检查状态码
                if resp.StatusCode < 200 || resp.StatusCode >= 300 {
                        return fmt.Errorf("%s 返回错误状态码: %d, 响应: %s",
                                endpoint.name, resp.StatusCode, string(body))
                }

                fmt.Printf("✅ %s 检查通过 (状态码: %d)\n", endpoint.name, resp.StatusCode)

                // 打印响应内容（限制长度）
                responseStr := string(body)
                if len(responseStr) > 200 {
                        responseStr = responseStr[:200] + "..."
                }
                fmt.Printf("📄 响应内容: %s\n", responseStr)
                if ReleaseNo != "" {
                        if strings.Contains(responseStr, ReleaseNo) {
                                fmt.Printf("✅ %s 检查通过 (包含版本号: %s)\n", endpoint.name, ReleaseNo)
                        } else {
                                return fmt.Errorf("%s 不包含版本号: %s", endpoint.name, ReleaseNo)
                        }
                }
        }

        return nil
}

func CleanupOldLocalVersions() {
        fmt.Println("🧹 正在清理本地旧版本文件...")

        // 获取所有以服务名开头的文件，按时间排序
        listCmd := fmt.Sprintf("ls -t %s* | tail -n +6", serviceName)
        cmd := exec.Command("bash", "-c", listCmd)
        output, err := cmd.Output()
        if err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 获取本地文件列表失败:", err)
                return
        }

        // 计算要删除的文件
        filesToDelete := strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), "\n")
        if len(filesToDelete) == 0 || (len(filesToDelete) == 1 && filesToDelete[0] == "") {
                fmt.Println("✅ 没有需要清理的本地旧文件")
                return
        }

        // 删除旧文件
        for _, file := range filesToDelete {
                if err := os.Remove(file); err != nil {
                        fmt.Printf("❌ 删除文件 %s 失败: %v\n", file, err)
                        continue
                }
        }

        fmt.Printf("✅ 成功清理了 %d 个本地旧版本文件\n", len(filesToDelete))
}

func UploadBinary() {
        releasePath := fmt.Sprintf("%s/releases", remotePath)
        //创建releases目录
        runCommand(fmt.Sprintf("ssh %s@%s 'mkdir -p %s'", serverUser, serverHost, releasePath))
        remoteFile := fmt.Sprintf("%s@%s:%s/%s", serverUser, serverHost, releasePath, ReleaseFile)
        fmt.Println("📤 正在上传到服务器:", remoteFile)
        if err := runCommand(fmt.Sprintf("scp %s %s", ReleaseFile, remoteFile)); err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 上传失败:", err)
                return
        }
        fmt.Println("✅ 上传成功!")
}

func BuildApp() error {
        ReleaseNo = time.Now().Format("20060102150405")
        ReleaseFile += ReleaseNo
        ldflags := "-s -w" //-s: 移除符号表（symbol table），这会减小可执行文件的大小，但会使调试变得困难。-w: 移除DWARF调试信息，进一步减小可执行文件的大小。
        gitCommit := "$(git rev-parse --short HEAD)"

        // 使用全局版本号变量
        version := ReleaseVersion

        ldflags += fmt.Sprintf(" -X 'hahacking/version.BuildTime=%s' -X 'hahacking/version.Version=%s' -X 'hahacking/version.GitCommit=%s'", ReleaseNo, version, gitCommit)
        cmd := fmt.Sprintf("CGO_ENABLED=0 GOOS=linux GOARCH=amd64 go build -trimpath -ldflags=\"%s\" -buildvcs=false -o %s", ldflags, ReleaseFile)
        fmt.Println("🔨 正在编译应用:", cmd)
        if err := runCommand(cmd); err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 构建失败:", err)
                return err
        }
        fmt.Println("✅ 构建完成!")
        return nil
}

func RunTests(tests string) error {
        fmt.Println("🔨 正在运行测试...")
        // 跳过 cmd 目录，避免编译错误
        if tests == "all" {
                // tests = "./models/... ./service/... ./common/... ./utils/... ./scheduler/... ./middleware/..."
                tests = "./..."
        }
        cmd := "go test -coverprofile=coverage.out " + tests
        if err := runCommand(cmd); err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 测试失败:", err)
                return err
        }
        fmt.Println("✅ 测试完成!")
        fmt.Println("📊 正在生成覆盖率报告...")
        cmd = "go tool cover -html=coverage.out -o coverage.html"
        if err := runCommand(cmd); err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 生成覆盖率报表失败:", err)
                return err
        }

        cmd = "go tool cover -func=coverage.out | grep total: | awk '{print $3}'"
        var outBuf, errBuf bytes.Buffer
        runCommandOutput(cmd, &outBuf, &errBuf)
        fmt.Println("测试覆盖率:", outBuf.String())
        return nil
}
func runRemoteCommand(cmd string) (string, error) {
        remoteCmd := "ssh %s@%s '%s'"
        remoteCmd = fmt.Sprintf(remoteCmd, serverUser, serverHost, cmd)
        log.Println("🔄 正在执行远程命令:", remoteCmd)
        execCmd := exec.Command("bash", "-c", remoteCmd)
        output, err := execCmd.Output()
        return string(output), err
}

// CleanupOldVersions 清理远程服务器上的旧版本文件，只保留最近3个文件
func CleanupOldVersions() {
        fmt.Println("🧹 正在清理旧版本文件...")
        listCmd := "cd %s && ls -t %s* | tail -n +4"
        releasePath := fmt.Sprintf("%s/releases", remotePath)
        listCmd = fmt.Sprintf(listCmd, releasePath, serviceName)
        output, err := runRemoteCommand(listCmd)
        if err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 获取文件列表失败:", err)
                return
        }

        // 计算要删除的文件数量
        filesToDelete := strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), "\n")
        if len(filesToDelete) == 0 || (len(filesToDelete) == 1 && filesToDelete[0] == "") {
                fmt.Println("✅ 没有需要清理的旧文件")
                return
        }

        // 执行删除命令
        cleanupCmd := fmt.Sprintf("%s | xargs -r rm -f", listCmd)
        _, err = runRemoteCommand(cleanupCmd)
        if err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 清理旧版本文件失败:", err)
                return
        }

        fmt.Printf("✅ 成功清理了 %d 个旧版本文件\n", len(filesToDelete))
}

// Rollback 回滚到指定版本
func Rollback(version int) {
        fmt.Printf("🔄 正在回滚到第 %d 个版本...\n", version)
        releasePath := fmt.Sprintf("%s/releases", remotePath)
        // 获取远程服务器上的版本列表
        listCmd := fmt.Sprintf(
                "ssh %s@%s 'cd %s && ls -t %s*'",
                serverUser, serverHost, releasePath, serviceName,
        )

        cmd := exec.Command("bash", "-c", listCmd)
        output, err := cmd.Output()
        if err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 获取版本列表失败:", err)
                return
        }

        // 解析版本列表
        versions := strings.Split(strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), "\n")
        if len(versions) == 0 || (len(versions) == 1 && versions[0] == "") {
                fmt.Println("❌ 没有找到可回滚的版本")
                return
        }

        // 检查版本号是否有效
        if version > len(versions) {
                fmt.Printf("❌ 版本号无效，当前共有 %d 个版本\n", len(versions))
                return
        }

        // 获取目标版本文件名
        targetVersion := versions[version]

        // 停止当前服务
        stopCmd := fmt.Sprintf(
                "ssh %s@%s 'sudo systemctl stop %s'",
                serverUser, serverHost, serviceName,
        )
        if err := runCommand(stopCmd); err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 停止服务失败:", err)
                return
        }

        // 创建软链接到目标版本
        linkCmd := fmt.Sprintf(
                "ssh %s@%s 'sudo ln -sf %s/%s %s/%s'",
                serverUser, serverHost, releasePath, targetVersion, releasePath, serviceName,
        )
        if err := runCommand(linkCmd); err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 创建软链接失败:", err)
                return
        }

        // 重启服务
        restartCmd := fmt.Sprintf(
                "ssh %s@%s 'chmod +x %s/%s && sudo systemctl restart %s && sudo systemctl status %s --no-pager'",
                serverUser, serverHost, releasePath, targetVersion, serviceName, serviceName,
        )
        if err := runCommand(restartCmd); err != nil {
                fmt.Println("❌ 重启服务失败:", err)
                return
        }

        fmt.Printf("✅ 已成功回滚到版本: %s\n", targetVersion)
}

func runCommand(cmd string) error {
        return runCommandOutput(cmd, nil, nil)
}

// 运行 shell 命令
func runCommandOutput(cmd string, output *bytes.Buffer, errout *bytes.Buffer) error {
        // fmt.Println("▶️ 执行命令:", cmd)
        command := exec.Command("bash", "-c", cmd)
        if output == nil {
                command.Stdout = os.Stdout
                command.Stderr = os.Stderr
        } else {
                command.Stdout = output
                command.Stderr = errout
        }
        return command.Run()
}

package app

import (
        "hahacking/common/ret"
        "net/http"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

type Gin struct {
        Cxt *gin.Context
}

type Response struct {
        Code int    `json:"code"`
        Msg  string `json:"message"`
        Data any    `json:"data"`
}

// Response setting gin.JSON
func (g *Gin) Response(httpCode, errCode int, data any) {
        g.Cxt.JSON(httpCode, Response{
                Code: errCode,
                Msg:  ret.GetMsg(errCode),
                Data: data,
        })
}

func (g *Gin) Success(msg string, data any) {
        g.Cxt.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{
                "code":    ret.SUCCESS,
                "message": msg,
                "data":    data,
        })
}

func (g *Gin) SuccessWithNoData(msg string) {
        g.Cxt.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{
                "code":    ret.SUCCESS,
                "message": msg,
        })
}

func (g *Gin) Failed(code int, msg string) {
        g.Cxt.AbortWithStatusJSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{
                "code":    code,
                "message": msg,
        })
}

func (g *Gin) Error(code int, msg string, data interface{}) {
        g.Cxt.AbortWithStatusJSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{
                "code":    code,
                "message": msg,
                "data":    data,
        })
}

func (g *Gin) UnAuthorized() {
        g.Cxt.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{
                "code":    ret.TOKEN_INVALID,
                "message": ret.GetMsg(ret.TOKEN_INVALID),
        })
        g.Cxt.Abort()
}
func (g *Gin) NotFound(message string) {
        g.Cxt.JSON(http.StatusNotFound, gin.H{
                // "code":    ret.TOKEN_INVALID,
                "message": message,
        })
        g.Cxt.Abort()
}

package file

import (
        "fmt"
        "io/ioutil"
        "mime/multipart"
        "os"
        "path"
)

// GetSize get the file size
func GetSize(f multipart.File) (int, error) {
        content, err := ioutil.ReadAll(f)

        return len(content), err
}

// GetExt get the file ext
func GetExt(fileName string) string {
        return path.Ext(fileName)
}

// CheckNotExist check if the file exists
func CheckNotExist(src string) bool {
        _, err := os.Stat(src)

        return os.IsNotExist(err)
}

// CheckPermission check if the file has permission
func CheckPermission(src string) bool {
        _, err := os.Stat(src)

        return os.IsPermission(err)
}

// IsNotExistMkDir create a directory if it does not exist
func IsNotExistMkDir(src string) error {
        if notExist := CheckNotExist(src); notExist == true {
                if err := MkDir(src); err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }

        return nil
}

// MkDir create a directory
func MkDir(src string) error {
        err := os.MkdirAll(src, os.ModePerm)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }

        return nil
}

// Open a file according to a specific mode
func Open(name string, flag int, perm os.FileMode) (*os.File, error) {
        f, err := os.OpenFile(name, flag, perm)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }

        return f, nil
}

// MustOpen maximize trying to open the file
func MustOpen(fileName, filePath string) (*os.File, error) {
        dir, err := os.Getwd()
        if err != nil {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("os.Getwd err: %v", err)
        }

        src := dir + "/" + filePath
        perm := CheckPermission(src)
        if perm == true {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("file.CheckPermission Permission denied src: %s", src)
        }

        err = IsNotExistMkDir(src)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("file.IsNotExistMkDir src: %s, err: %v", src, err)
        }

        f, err := Open(src+fileName, os.O_APPEND|os.O_CREATE|os.O_RDWR, 0644)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("Fail to OpenFile :%v", err)
        }

        return f, nil
}

package gredis

import (
        "encoding/json"
        setting "hahacking/config"
        "time"

        "github.com/gomodule/redigo/redis"
)

var RedisConn *redis.Pool

// Setup Initialize the Redis instance
func Setup() error {
        RedisConn = &redis.Pool{
                MaxIdle:     setting.C.RedisSetting.MaxIdle,
                MaxActive:   setting.C.RedisSetting.MaxActive,
                IdleTimeout: setting.C.RedisSetting.IdleTimeout,
                Dial: func() (redis.Conn, error) {
                        c, err := redis.Dial("tcp", setting.C.RedisSetting.Host)
                        if err != nil {
                                return nil, err
                        }
                        if setting.C.RedisSetting.Password != "" {
                                if _, err := c.Do("AUTH", setting.C.RedisSetting.Password); err != nil {
                                        c.Close()
                                        return nil, err
                                }
                        }
                        return c, err
                },
                TestOnBorrow: func(c redis.Conn, t time.Time) error {
                        _, err := c.Do("PING")
                        return err
                },
        }

        return nil
}

// Set a key/value
func Set(key string, data interface{}, time int) error {
        conn := RedisConn.Get()
        defer conn.Close()

        value, err := json.Marshal(data)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }

        _, err = conn.Do("SET", key, value)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }

        _, err = conn.Do("EXPIRE", key, time)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }

        return nil
}

// Exists check a key
func Exists(key string) bool {
        conn := RedisConn.Get()
        defer conn.Close()

        exists, err := redis.Bool(conn.Do("EXISTS", key))
        if err != nil {
                return false
        }

        return exists
}

// Get get a key
func Get(key string) ([]byte, error) {
        conn := RedisConn.Get()
        defer conn.Close()

        reply, err := redis.Bytes(conn.Do("GET", key))
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }

        return reply, nil
}

func GetString(key string) (string, error) {
        conn := RedisConn.Get()
        defer conn.Close()

        reply, err := redis.String(conn.Do("GET", key))
        if err != nil {
                return "", err
        }

        return reply, nil
}

// Delete delete a kye
func Delete(key string) (bool, error) {
        conn := RedisConn.Get()
        defer conn.Close()

        return redis.Bool(conn.Do("DEL", key))
}

// LikeDeletes batch delete
func LikeDeletes(key string) error {
        conn := RedisConn.Get()
        defer conn.Close()

        keys, err := redis.Strings(conn.Do("KEYS", "*"+key+"*"))
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }

        for _, key := range keys {
                _, err = Delete(key)
                if err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }

        return nil
}

func LPush(key string, data interface{}) (string, error) {
        conn := RedisConn.Get()
        defer conn.Close()

        reply, err := redis.String(conn.Do("LPUSH", key, data))
        if err != nil {
                return "", err
        }

        return reply, nil
}

func RPop(key string) (string, error) {
        conn := RedisConn.Get()
        defer conn.Close()

        reply, err := redis.String(conn.Do("RPOP", key))
        if err != nil {
                return "", err
        }

        return reply, nil
}

package job

import (

        cron "github.com/robfig/cron/v3"
)

func Setup() {
        c := cron.New()
        //run per minute
        c.AddFunc("0 */1 * * * ?", func() {
                log.Println("Check if order is expired...")
        })
        c.Start()
}

package logger

import (
        "context"
        "fmt"
        "sync"
        "time"
)

// AutoRotationManager 自动日志轮转管理器
type AutoRotationManager struct {
        config         LogRotationConfig
        filePath       string
        fileName       string
        ctx            context.Context
        cancel         context.CancelFunc
        wg             sync.WaitGroup
        rotationTicker *time.Ticker
        cleanupTicker  *time.Ticker
}

// NewAutoRotationManager 创建自动轮转管理器
func NewAutoRotationManager() *AutoRotationManager {
        ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())

        return &AutoRotationManager{
                config: getLogRotationConfig(),
                ctx:    ctx,
                cancel: cancel,
        }
}

// Start 启动自动轮转管理器
func (arm *AutoRotationManager) Start() error {
        // 设置文件路径
        arm.filePath = getLogFilePath()
        arm.fileName = getLogFileName()

        // 启动轮转检查（每小时检查一次）
        arm.rotationTicker = time.NewTicker(1 * time.Hour)
        arm.wg.Add(1)
        go arm.rotationWorker()

        // 启动清理检查（每天凌晨2点执行）
        arm.cleanupTicker = time.NewTicker(24 * time.Hour)
        arm.wg.Add(1)
        go arm.cleanupWorker()

        Info("自动日志轮转管理器已启动")
        return nil
}

// Stop 停止自动轮转管理器
func (arm *AutoRotationManager) Stop() {
        if arm.cancel != nil {
                arm.cancel()
        }

        if arm.rotationTicker != nil {
                arm.rotationTicker.Stop()
        }

        if arm.cleanupTicker != nil {
                arm.cleanupTicker.Stop()
        }

        arm.wg.Wait()
        Info("自动日志轮转管理器已停止")
}

// rotationWorker 轮转工作协程
func (arm *AutoRotationManager) rotationWorker() {
        defer arm.wg.Done()

        for {
                select {
                case <-arm.ctx.Done():
                        return
                case <-arm.rotationTicker.C:
                        arm.checkAndRotate()
                }
        }
}

// cleanupWorker 清理工作协程
func (arm *AutoRotationManager) cleanupWorker() {
        defer arm.wg.Done()

        // 等待到下一个凌晨2点
        now := time.Now()
        nextCleanup := time.Date(now.Year(), now.Month(), now.Day(), 2, 0, 0, 0, now.Location())
        if nextCleanup.Before(now) {
                nextCleanup = nextCleanup.Add(24 * time.Hour)
        }

        // 等待到清理时间
        time.Sleep(time.Until(nextCleanup))

        for {
                select {
                case <-arm.ctx.Done():
                        return
                case <-arm.cleanupTicker.C:
                        arm.performCleanup()
                }
        }
}

// checkAndRotate 检查并执行轮转
func (arm *AutoRotationManager) checkAndRotate() {
        if logRotator == nil {
                return
        }

        // 检查是否需要轮转
        if logRotator.shouldRotate() {
                Info("检测到需要轮转日志文件")
                if err := logRotator.rotate(); err != nil {
                        Error("日志轮转失败: " + err.Error())
                } else {
                        Info("日志轮转完成")
                }
        }
}

// performCleanup 执行清理操作
func (arm *AutoRotationManager) performCleanup() {
        Info("开始执行自动日志清理")

        // 创建临时轮转器用于清理
        tempRotator := NewLogRotator(arm.config)
        tempRotator.SetFilePath(arm.filePath, arm.fileName)

        // 执行清理
        if err := tempRotator.cleanup(); err != nil {
                Error("自动日志清理失败: " + err.Error())
        } else {
                Info("自动日志清理完成")
        }
}

// GetRotationStatus 获取轮转状态信息
func (arm *AutoRotationManager) GetRotationStatus() map[string]interface{} {
        status := map[string]interface{}{
                "enabled":       arm.rotationTicker != nil,
                "config":        arm.config,
                "file_path":     arm.filePath,
                "file_name":     arm.fileName,
                "next_rotation": "每小时检查",
                "next_cleanup":  "每天凌晨2点",
        }

        if logRotator != nil {
                status["current_file_size"] = logRotator.fileSize
                status["last_rotate"] = logRotator.lastRotate
        }

        return status
}

// 全局自动轮转管理器
var autoRotationManager *AutoRotationManager

// StartAutoRotation 启动自动轮转
func StartAutoRotation() error {
        if autoRotationManager != nil {
                return fmt.Errorf("自动轮转管理器已经在运行")
        }

        autoRotationManager = NewAutoRotationManager()
        return autoRotationManager.Start()
}

// StopAutoRotation 停止自动轮转
func StopAutoRotation() {
        if autoRotationManager != nil {
                autoRotationManager.Stop()
                autoRotationManager = nil
        }
}

// GetAutoRotationStatus 获取自动轮转状态
func GetAutoRotationStatus() map[string]interface{} {
        if autoRotationManager == nil {
                return map[string]interface{}{
                        "enabled": false,
                        "message": "自动轮转未启动",
                }
        }

        return autoRotationManager.GetRotationStatus()
}

package logger

import (
        "hahacking/common/file"
        "io"
        "log"
        "os"
)

// LogConfig 日志配置结构
type LogConfig struct {
        // 输出目标配置
        OutputToFile    bool // 是否输出到文件
        OutputToConsole bool // 是否输出到控制台

        // 文件配置
        LogFilePath string // 日志文件路径
        LogFileName string // 日志文件名

        // 控制台配置
        EnableColors bool // 是否启用颜色

        // 日志级别配置
        MinLevel Level // 最小日志级别
}

// DefaultConfig 默认配置
func DefaultConfig() *LogConfig {
        return &LogConfig{
                OutputToFile:    true,
                OutputToConsole: true,
                LogFilePath:     "runtime/logs/",
                LogFileName:     "app.log",
                EnableColors:    true,
                MinLevel:        DEBUG,
        }
}

// SetupWithConfig 使用配置初始化日志系统
func SetupWithConfig(config *LogConfig) error {
        var writers []io.Writer

        // 设置日志级别
        SetLogLevel(config.MinLevel)

        // 添加控制台输出
        if config.OutputToConsole {
                writers = append(writers, os.Stdout)
        }

        // 添加文件输出
        if config.OutputToFile {
                filePath := config.LogFilePath
                fileName := config.LogFileName

                F, err := file.MustOpen(fileName, filePath)
                if err != nil {
                        return err
                }

                writers = append(writers, F)
        }

        // 创建多输出writer
        if len(writers) == 0 {
                // 如果没有配置任何输出，默认输出到控制台
                writers = append(writers, os.Stdout)
        }

        multiWriter = io.MultiWriter(writers...)
        logger = log.New(multiWriter, DefaultPrefix, log.LstdFlags)

        return nil
}

package logger

import (
        "fmt"
        "hahacking/config"
        "path/filepath"
        "strings"
        "time"
)

// getLogFilePath get the log file save path
func getLogFilePath() string {
        // 优先从 env.json 读取配置
        logConfig := config.Get("log")
        if logConfig != nil {
                if filename, ok := logConfig["filename"].(string); ok && filename != "" {
                        // 如果配置了完整的文件路径，提取目录部分
                        dir := filepath.Dir(filename)
                        if dir != "." && dir != "" {
                                return fmt.Sprintf("%s%s%s/", config.C.BasePath, config.C.AppSetting.RuntimeRootPath, dir)
                        }
                }
        }
        // 回退到 app.ini 配置
        return fmt.Sprintf("%s%s%s", config.C.BasePath, config.C.AppSetting.RuntimeRootPath, config.C.AppSetting.LogSavePath)
}

// getLogFileName get the save name of the log file
func getLogFileName() string {
        // 在生产环境，优先使用配置文件中的 filename
        if config.ENV == "prd" {
                logConfig := config.Get("log")
                if logConfig != nil {
                        if filename, ok := logConfig["filename"].(string); ok && filename != "" {
                                // 如果配置的是完整路径（包含目录），只提取文件名
                                // 例如 "logs/app.log" -> "app.log"
                                if idx := strings.LastIndex(filename, "/"); idx >= 0 {
                                        return filename[idx+1:]
                                }
                                return filename
                        }
                }
                // 如果没有配置 filename，使用固定的 app.log
                return "app.log"
        }

        // 开发环境使用时间戳文件名
        return fmt.Sprintf("%s%s.%s",
                config.C.AppSetting.LogSaveName,
                time.Now().Format(config.C.AppSetting.TimeFormat),
                config.C.AppSetting.LogFileExt,
        )
}

package logger

import (
        "context"
        "log/slog"
        "os"
        "path/filepath"
        "sync"
        "time"

        "github.com/natefinch/lumberjack"
)

type LevelFileHandler struct {
        handlers map[slog.Level]slog.Handler
        mu       sync.Mutex
}

func NewLevelFileHandler() (*LevelFileHandler, error) {
        h := &LevelFileHandler{
                handlers: make(map[slog.Level]slog.Handler),
        }

        // 初始化不同级别的日志文件
        logDir := "logs"
        if err := os.MkdirAll(logDir, 0755); err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }

        // 配置 INFO 级别日志（10MB，保留7天）
        infoLogger := &lumberjack.Logger{
                Filename:   filepath.Join(logDir, "info.log"),
                MaxSize:    10,   // MB
                MaxAge:     7,    // 天
                MaxBackups: 3,    // 备份文件数
                Compress:   true, // 压缩旧日志
        }
        // h.handlers[slog.LevelInfo] = slog.NewTextHandler(infoLogger, &slog.HandlerOptions{
        //         Level: slog.LevelInfo,
        // })
        h.handlers[slog.LevelInfo] = slog.NewJSONHandler(infoLogger, &slog.HandlerOptions{
                Level: slog.LevelInfo,
        })

        // 配置 ERROR 级别日志（10MB，保留7天）
        errorLogger := &lumberjack.Logger{
                Filename:   filepath.Join(logDir, "error.log"),
                MaxSize:    10,   // MB
                MaxAge:     7,    // 天
                MaxBackups: 3,    // 备份文件数
                Compress:   true, // 压缩旧日志
        }
        h.handlers[slog.LevelError] = slog.NewTextHandler(errorLogger, &slog.HandlerOptions{
                Level: slog.LevelError,
        })
        // h.handlers[slog.LevelError] = slog.NewJSONHandler(errorLogger, &slog.HandlerOptions{
        //         Level: slog.LevelError,
        // })
        return h, nil
}

func (h *LevelFileHandler) Enabled(ctx context.Context, level slog.Level) bool {
        for l := range h.handlers {
                if level >= l {
                        return true
                }
        }
        return false
}

func (h *LevelFileHandler) Handle(ctx context.Context, r slog.Record) error {
        h.mu.Lock()
        defer h.mu.Unlock()

        for level, handler := range h.handlers {
                if r.Level >= level {
                        return handler.Handle(ctx, r)
                }
        }
        return nil
}

func (h *LevelFileHandler) WithAttrs(attrs []slog.Attr) slog.Handler {
        h.mu.Lock()
        defer h.mu.Unlock()

        newHandlers := make(map[slog.Level]slog.Handler, len(h.handlers))
        for level, handler := range h.handlers {
                newHandlers[level] = handler.WithAttrs(attrs)
        }
        return &LevelFileHandler{handlers: newHandlers, mu: h.mu}
}

func (h *LevelFileHandler) WithGroup(name string) slog.Handler {
        h.mu.Lock()
        defer h.mu.Unlock()

        newHandlers := make(map[slog.Level]slog.Handler, len(h.handlers))
        for level, handler := range h.handlers {
                newHandlers[level] = handler.WithGroup(name)
        }
        return &LevelFileHandler{handlers: newHandlers, mu: h.mu}
}

func main0() {
        // 初始化日志管理器
        handler, err := NewLevelFileHandler()
        if err != nil {
                panic(err)
        }
        logger := slog.New(handler)

        // 示例使用
        logger.Info("This is an info message", "user", "alice", "time", time.Now())
        logger.Error("This is an error message", "error", "timeout", "time", time.Now())

        // 模拟业务逻辑
        for i := 0; i < 5; i++ {
                if i%2 == 0 {
                        logger.Info("Processing item", "item", i)
                } else {
                        logger.Error("Failed to process item", "item", i, "reason", "network error")
                }
                time.Sleep(time.Second)
        }
}

package logger

import (
        "fmt"
        "io"
        "log"
        "os"
        "os/signal"
        "path/filepath"
        "runtime"
        "strings"
        "sync"
        "syscall"

        "hahacking/config"
)

type Level int

var (
        F *os.File

        DefaultPrefix      = ""
        DefaultCallerDepth = 2

        logger     *log.Logger
        logPrefix  = ""
        levelFlags = []string{"DEBUG", "INFO", "WARN", "ERROR", "FATAL"}

        // 添加多输出支持
        multiWriter io.Writer

        // 全局日志级别
        currentLevel Level = DEBUG
        levelMutex   sync.RWMutex

        // 日志轮转器
        logRotator *LogRotator
)

const (
        DEBUG Level = iota
        INFO
        WARNING
        ERROR
        FATAL
)

// Setup initialize the log instance
func Setup() {
        filePath := getLogFilePath()
        fileName := getLogFileName()

        // 从配置文件读取日志级别
        level := getLogLevelFromConfig()
        SetLogLevel(level)

        // 创建多输出writer，同时输出到文件和控制台
        var writers []io.Writer
        writers = append(writers, os.Stdout)

        if config.ENV == "prd" {
                // 生产环境启用日志轮转
                setupLogRotation()
                if logRotator != nil && logRotator.file != nil {
                        // 将日志轮转器的文件句柄加入到 writers
                        writers = append(writers, logRotator)
                }
                setupGracefulShutdown()
        }

        multiWriter = io.MultiWriter(writers...)
        logger = log.New(multiWriter, DefaultPrefix, log.LstdFlags)

        // 输出初始化信息
        logger.Println("日志系统初始化完成，文件路径：", filePath+fileName, "日志级别：", levelFlags[level])

        // 如果启用了日志轮转，输出轮转配置信息并启动自动轮转管理器
        if config.ENV == "prd" && logRotator != nil {
                rotationConfig := getLogRotationConfig()
                logger.Printf("日志轮转配置：最大大小=%dMB，最大保留天数=%d天，最大备份数=%d个",
                        rotationConfig.MaxSize, rotationConfig.MaxAge, rotationConfig.MaxBackups)

                // 启动自动轮转管理器
                if err := StartAutoRotation(); err != nil {
                        logger.Printf("启动自动轮转失败: %v", err)
                } else {
                        logger.Println("自动日志轮转已启动")
                }
        }
}
func setupLogRotation() {
        filePath := getLogFilePath()
        fileName := getLogFileName()

        // 获取日志轮转配置
        rotationConfig := getLogRotationConfig()

        // 创建日志轮转器
        logRotator = NewLogRotator(rotationConfig)
        logRotator.SetFilePath(filePath, fileName)

        // 创建初始日志文件
        if err := logRotator.createNewFile(); err != nil {
                log.Fatalf("logging.Setup err: %v", err)
        }

        // 获取文件句柄用于兼容性
        F = logRotator.file
        // 注意：此时 logger 还未创建，日志信息会在 Setup() 函数中输出
}

// GetWriter 获取日志writer，供其他组件使用
func GetWriter() io.Writer {
        return multiWriter
}

// GetFileWriter 获取文件writer
func GetFileWriter() io.Writer {
        return F
}

// GetLogRotationInfo 获取日志轮转信息
func GetLogRotationInfo() map[string]interface{} {
        if logRotator == nil {
                return nil
        }

        info := make(map[string]interface{})
        info["max_size"] = logRotator.config.MaxSize
        info["max_age"] = logRotator.config.MaxAge
        info["max_backups"] = logRotator.config.MaxBackups
        info["current_file_size"] = formatFileSize(logRotator.fileSize)
        info["file_path"] = logRotator.filePath
        info["file_name"] = logRotator.fileName

        return info
}

// SetupWithLevel 使用指定日志级别初始化日志系统
func SetupWithLevel(level Level) {
        SetLogLevel(level)
        Setup()
}

// SetLogLevel 设置日志级别
func SetLogLevel(level Level) {
        levelMutex.Lock()
        defer levelMutex.Unlock()
        currentLevel = level
}

// GetLogLevel 获取当前日志级别
func GetLogLevel() Level {
        levelMutex.RLock()
        defer levelMutex.RUnlock()
        return currentLevel
}

// IsLevelEnabled 检查指定级别是否启用
func IsLevelEnabled(level Level) bool {
        levelMutex.RLock()
        defer levelMutex.RUnlock()
        return level >= currentLevel
}

// Debug output logs at debug level
func Debug(v ...interface{}) {
        if !IsLevelEnabled(DEBUG) {
                return
        }
        setPrefix(DEBUG)
        logger.Println(v...)
}
func Debugf(format string, v ...interface{}) {
        if !IsLevelEnabled(DEBUG) {
                return
        }
        setPrefix(DEBUG)
        logger.Printf(format, v...)
}

// Info output logs at info level
func Info(v ...interface{}) {
        if !IsLevelEnabled(INFO) {
                return
        }
        setPrefix(INFO)
        logger.Println(v...)
}
func Infof(format string, v ...interface{}) {
        if !IsLevelEnabled(INFO) {
                return
        }
        setPrefix(INFO)
        logger.Printf(format, v...)
}

// Warn output logs at warn level
func Warn(v ...interface{}) {
        if !IsLevelEnabled(WARNING) {
                return
        }
        setPrefix(WARNING)
        logger.Println(v...)
}
func Warnf(format string, v ...interface{}) {
        if !IsLevelEnabled(WARNING) {
                return
        }
        setPrefix(WARNING)
        logger.Printf(format, v...)
}

// Error output logs at error level
func Error(v ...interface{}) {
        if !IsLevelEnabled(ERROR) {
                return
        }
        setPrefix(ERROR)
        logger.Println(v...)
}
func Errorf(format string, v ...interface{}) {
        if !IsLevelEnabled(ERROR) {
                return
        }
        setPrefix(ERROR)
        logger.Printf(format, v...)
}

// Fatal output logs at fatal level
func Fatal(v ...interface{}) {
        if !IsLevelEnabled(FATAL) {
                return
        }
        setPrefix(FATAL)
        logger.Println(v...)
}
func Fatalf(format string, v ...interface{}) {
        if !IsLevelEnabled(FATAL) {
                return
        }
        setPrefix(FATAL)
        logger.Printf(format, v...)
}

// setPrefix set the prefix of the log output
func setPrefix(level Level) {
        _, file, line, ok := runtime.Caller(DefaultCallerDepth)
        if ok {
                logPrefix = fmt.Sprintf("[%s][%s:%d]", levelFlags[level], filepath.Base(file), line)
        } else {
                logPrefix = fmt.Sprintf("[%s]", levelFlags[level])
        }

        logger.SetPrefix(logPrefix)
}

// getLogLevelFromConfig 从配置文件读取日志级别
func getLogLevelFromConfig() Level {
        logConfig := config.Get("log")
        if logConfig == nil {
                return DEBUG // 默认DEBUG级别
        }

        levelStr, ok := logConfig["level"].(string)
        if !ok {
                return DEBUG // 默认DEBUG级别
        }

        levelStr = strings.ToLower(levelStr)
        switch levelStr {
        case "debug":
                return DEBUG
        case "info":
                return INFO
        case "warn", "warning":
                return WARNING
        case "error":
                return ERROR
        case "fatal":
                return FATAL
        default:
                return DEBUG // 默认DEBUG级别
        }
}

// setupGracefulShutdown 设置优雅关闭
func setupGracefulShutdown() {

        c := make(chan os.Signal, 1)
        signal.Notify(c, os.Interrupt, syscall.SIGTERM)

        go func() {
                <-c
                Info("收到关闭信号，正在优雅关闭日志系统...")
                StopAutoRotation()
                if logRotator != nil {
                        logRotator.Close()
                }
                Info("日志系统已关闭")
                os.Exit(0)
        }()
}

package logger

import (
        "fmt"
        "os"
        "path/filepath"
        "sort"
        "strconv"
        "strings"
        "sync"
        "time"

        "hahacking/config"
)

// LogRotationConfig 日志轮转配置
type LogRotationConfig struct {
        MaxSize    int // 单个日志文件的最大大小（MB）
        MaxAge     int // 日志文件的最大保留天数
        MaxBackups int // 保留的日志文件备份数量
}

// LogRotator 日志轮转器
type LogRotator struct {
        config     LogRotationConfig
        file       *os.File
        filePath   string
        fileName   string
        fileSize   int64
        mutex      sync.Mutex
        lastRotate time.Time
}

// NewLogRotator 创建新的日志轮转器
func NewLogRotator(config LogRotationConfig) *LogRotator {
        return &LogRotator{
                config:     config,
                lastRotate: time.Now(),
        }
}

// Write 实现io.Writer接口，支持日志轮转
func (lr *LogRotator) Write(p []byte) (n int, err error) {
        lr.mutex.Lock()
        defer lr.mutex.Unlock()

        // 检查是否需要轮转
        if lr.shouldRotate() {
                if err := lr.rotate(); err != nil {
                        return 0, err
                }
        }

        // 写入日志
        if lr.file != nil {
                n, err = lr.file.Write(p)
                if err == nil {
                        lr.fileSize += int64(n)
                }
                return n, err
        }

        return 0, fmt.Errorf("log file not opened")
}

// shouldRotate 检查是否需要轮转
func (lr *LogRotator) shouldRotate() bool {
        if lr.file == nil {
                return false
        }

        // 检查文件大小
        if lr.config.MaxSize > 0 && lr.fileSize >= int64(lr.config.MaxSize*1024*1024) {
                return true
        }

        // 检查时间（每天轮转一次）
        now := time.Now()
        if now.Day() != lr.lastRotate.Day() || now.Month() != lr.lastRotate.Month() || now.Year() != lr.lastRotate.Year() {
                return true
        }

        return false
}

// rotate 执行日志轮转
func (lr *LogRotator) rotate() error {
        if lr.file != nil {
                lr.file.Close()
        }

        // 重命名当前日志文件
        if lr.file != nil {
                oldPath := filepath.Join(lr.filePath, lr.fileName)
                if _, err := os.Stat(oldPath); err == nil {
                        // 生成带时间戳的备份文件名
                        timestamp := time.Now().Format("20060102-150405")
                        ext := filepath.Ext(lr.fileName)
                        name := strings.TrimSuffix(lr.fileName, ext)
                        backupName := fmt.Sprintf("%s.%s%s", name, timestamp, ext)
                        backupPath := filepath.Join(lr.filePath, backupName)

                        if err := os.Rename(oldPath, backupPath); err != nil {
                                return fmt.Errorf("failed to rename log file: %v", err)
                        }
                }
        }

        // 清理旧文件
        if err := lr.cleanup(); err != nil {
                return fmt.Errorf("failed to cleanup old log files: %v", err)
        }

        // 创建新的日志文件
        if err := lr.createNewFile(); err != nil {
                return fmt.Errorf("failed to create new log file: %v", err)
        }

        lr.lastRotate = time.Now()
        return nil
}

// cleanup 清理旧的日志文件
func (lr *LogRotator) cleanup() error {
        if lr.config.MaxAge <= 0 && lr.config.MaxBackups <= 0 {
                return nil
        }

        // 获取所有日志文件
        files, err := lr.getLogFiles()
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }

        // 按修改时间排序（最新的在前）
        sort.Slice(files, func(i, j int) bool {
                return files[i].ModTime().After(files[j].ModTime())
        })

        // 删除超过最大保留天数的文件
        if lr.config.MaxAge > 0 {
                cutoff := time.Now().AddDate(0, 0, -lr.config.MaxAge)
                for i := len(files) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
                        file := files[i]
                        if file.ModTime().Before(cutoff) {
                                filePath := filepath.Join(lr.filePath, file.Name())
                                if err := os.Remove(filePath); err != nil {
                                        // 如果文件不存在，忽略错误
                                        if !os.IsNotExist(err) {
                                                return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove old log file %s: %v", filePath, err)
                                        }
                                }
                                // 从切片中移除已删除的文件
                                files = append(files[:i], files[i+1:]...)
                        }
                }
        }

        // 删除超过最大备份数量的文件
        if lr.config.MaxBackups > 0 && len(files) > lr.config.MaxBackups {
                for i := lr.config.MaxBackups; i < len(files); i++ {
                        filePath := filepath.Join(lr.filePath, files[i].Name())
                        if err := os.Remove(filePath); err != nil {
                                // 如果文件不存在，忽略错误
                                if !os.IsNotExist(err) {
                                        return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove excess log file %s: %v", filePath, err)
                                }
                        }
                }
        }

        return nil
}

// getLogFiles 获取所有日志文件
func (lr *LogRotator) getLogFiles() ([]os.FileInfo, error) {
        var files []os.FileInfo

        entries, err := os.ReadDir(lr.filePath)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }

        // 获取当前日志文件的基础名称（不含扩展名）
        baseName := strings.TrimSuffix(lr.fileName, filepath.Ext(lr.fileName))

        for _, entry := range entries {
                if entry.IsDir() {
                        continue
                }

                // 检查是否是日志文件（以基础名称开头）
                if strings.HasPrefix(entry.Name(), baseName) {
                        info, err := entry.Info()
                        if err != nil {
                                continue
                        }
                        files = append(files, info)
                }
        }

        return files, nil
}

// createNewFile 创建新的日志文件
func (lr *LogRotator) createNewFile() error {
        // 确保目录存在
        if err := os.MkdirAll(lr.filePath, 0755); err != nil {
                return fmt.Errorf("failed to create log directory: %v", err)
        }

        // 创建新文件
        filePath := filepath.Join(lr.filePath, lr.fileName)
        file, err := os.OpenFile(filePath, os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY|os.O_APPEND, 0644)
        if err != nil {
                return fmt.Errorf("failed to create log file: %v", err)
        }

        lr.file = file
        lr.fileSize = 0

        // 获取文件大小
        if stat, err := file.Stat(); err == nil {
                lr.fileSize = stat.Size()
        }

        return nil
}

// Close 关闭日志文件
func (lr *LogRotator) Close() error {
        lr.mutex.Lock()
        defer lr.mutex.Unlock()

        if lr.file != nil {
                return lr.file.Close()
        }
        return nil
}

// SetFilePath 设置文件路径
func (lr *LogRotator) SetFilePath(filePath, fileName string) {
        lr.mutex.Lock()
        defer lr.mutex.Unlock()

        lr.filePath = filePath
        lr.fileName = fileName
}

// getLogRotationConfig 从配置文件获取日志轮转配置
func getLogRotationConfig() LogRotationConfig {
        logConfig := config.Get("log")
        rotationConfig := LogRotationConfig{
                MaxSize:    100, // 默认100MB
                MaxAge:     7,   // 默认7天
                MaxBackups: 10,  // 默认10个备份
        }

        if logConfig == nil {
                return rotationConfig
        }

        // 读取maxsize
        if maxsize, ok := logConfig["maxsize"].(float64); ok {
                rotationConfig.MaxSize = int(maxsize)
        } else if maxsize, ok := logConfig["maxsize"].(int); ok {
                rotationConfig.MaxSize = maxsize
        }

        // 读取maxage
        if maxage, ok := logConfig["maxage"].(float64); ok {
                rotationConfig.MaxAge = int(maxage)
        } else if maxage, ok := logConfig["maxage"].(int); ok {
                rotationConfig.MaxAge = maxage
        }

        // 读取maxbackups
        if maxbackups, ok := logConfig["maxbackups"].(float64); ok {
                rotationConfig.MaxBackups = int(maxbackups)
        } else if maxbackups, ok := logConfig["maxbackups"].(int); ok {
                rotationConfig.MaxBackups = maxbackups
        }

        return rotationConfig
}

// formatFileSize 格式化文件大小
func formatFileSize(size int64) string {
        const unit = 1024
        if size < unit {
                return strconv.FormatInt(size, 10) + " B"
        }
        div, exp := int64(unit), 0
        for n := size / unit; n >= unit; n /= unit {
                div *= unit
                exp++
        }
        return fmt.Sprintf("%.1f %cB", float64(size)/float64(div), "KMGTPE"[exp])
}

        Sugar     *zap.SugaredLogger

        Zaplogger, err := zap.NewDevelopment()
        if err != nil {
                panic("Failed to initialize zap logger: " + err.Error())
        }
        Sugar = Zaplogger.Sugar()
        defer Zaplogger.Sync() // 确保日志在程序退出前写入
}

package mailer

import (
        "crypto/tls"

        "gopkg.in/gomail.v2"
)

type Mailer struct {
        Host     string
        Port     int
        Username string
        Password string
        From     string
        UseTLS   bool
}

func NewMailer(host string, port int, username, password, from string, useTLS bool) *Mailer {
        return &Mailer{
                Host:     host,
                Port:     port,
                Username: username,
                Password: password,
                From:     from,
                UseTLS:   useTLS,
        }
}

func (m *Mailer) dialer() *gomail.Dialer {
        d := gomail.NewDialer(m.Host, m.Port, m.Username, m.Password)

        if m.UseTLS {
                d.TLSConfig = &tls.Config{InsecureSkipVerify: true}
        }

        return d
}

// 发送消息
func (m *Mailer) Send(msg *Message) error {
        d := m.dialer()
        return d.DialAndSend(msg.m)
}

package mailer

import (
        "os"

        "gopkg.in/gomail.v2"
)

type Message struct {
        m *gomail.Message
}

// NewMessage 创建新邮件
func NewMessage() *Message {
        return &Message{
                m: gomail.NewMessage(),
        }
}

// SetFrom 设置发件人
func (m *Message) SetFrom(addr string) {
        m.m.SetHeader("From", addr)
}

// SetTo 设置收件人
func (m *Message) SetTo(addrs ...string) {
        m.m.SetHeader("To", addrs...)
}

// SetSubject 设置主题
func (m *Message) SetSubject(subject string) {
        m.m.SetHeader("Subject", subject)
}

// SetBody 设置纯文本与 HTML
func (m *Message) SetBody(plain string, html string) {
        m.m.SetBody("text/plain", plain)
        m.m.AddAlternative("text/html", html)
}

func (m *Message) SetBodyFromTemplate(templatename string, data interface{}) error {
        body, err := RenderTemplate("templates/"+templatename+".html", data)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        m.SetBody("text/html", body)
        return nil
}

// AddInline 添加 CID 内联图片（从文件路径）
// path: 图片文件路径
// cid: Content-ID，对应模板中的 cid:xxx
// 示例：msg.AddInline("assets/logo.png", "logo")
// 模板中使用：<img src="cid:logo">
func (m *Message) AddInline(path, cid string) {
        m.m.Embed(path, gomail.SetHeader(map[string][]string{
                "Content-ID": {"<" + cid + ">"},
        }))
}

// AddInlineFromBytes 从字节数组添加内联图片
// 注意：gomail 的 Embed 方法只支持文件路径，不支持直接传入字节数组
// 如果需要从内存数据添加图片，请先保存到临时文件，然后使用 AddInline
// data: 图片的字节数据（暂不支持，请使用文件路径方式）
// cid: Content-ID，对应模板中的 cid:xxx
// filename: 临时文件名（用于确定Content-Type），如 "logo.png"
// 示例：先保存图片到文件，然后使用 msg.AddInline("path/to/logo.png", "logo")
func (m *Message) AddInlineFromBytes(data []byte, cid, filename string) error {
        // 创建临时文件
        tmpFile, err := os.CreateTemp("", "mail_*.png")
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        defer os.Remove(tmpFile.Name()) // 清理临时文件

        if _, err := tmpFile.Write(data); err != nil {
                tmpFile.Close()
                return err
        }
        tmpFile.Close()

        // 使用临时文件路径添加内联图片
        m.AddInline(tmpFile.Name(), cid)
        return nil
}

// AddAttachment 添加附件（正常文件）
func (m *Message) AddAttachment(path string) {
        m.m.Attach(path)
}

package mailer

import (
        "bytes"
        "html/template"
        "path/filepath"
)

func RenderTemplate(filePath string, data interface{}) (string, error) {
        tpl, err := template.New("").Funcs(template.FuncMap{
                "safe":     func(s string) template.HTML { return template.HTML(s) },
                "notEmpty": func(s string) bool { return s != "" },
        }).ParseFiles(filePath)

        if err != nil {
                return "", err
        }

        // ParseFiles registers templates by their base filename, not the full path
        templateName := filepath.Base(filePath)
        var buf bytes.Buffer
        if err := tpl.ExecuteTemplate(&buf, templateName, data); err != nil {
                return "", err
        }

        return buf.String(), nil
}

package redis

import (
        "context"
        "hahacking/config"
        "log"

        "github.com/go-redis/redis/v8"
)

var Client *redis.Client

func NewClient() *redis.Client {
        redisInfo := config.Get("redis")
        return redis.NewClient(&redis.Options{
                Addr:     redisInfo["host"].(string) + ":" + redisInfo["port"].(string),
                Password: redisInfo["password"].(string),
                DB:       int(redisInfo["db"].(float64)),
        })
}

func Setup() {
        // 初始化Redis客户端
        Client = NewClient()
        pong, err := Client.Ping(context.Background()).Result()
        if err != nil {
                log.Fatalf("Failed to connect to Redis: %v", err)
        }
        log.Println("Connected to Redis - " + pong)
}

package ret

var MsgFlags = map[int]string{
        SUCCESS:                        "ok",
        ERROR:                          "fail",
        INVALID_PARAMS:                 "请求参数错误",
        ERROR_AUTH_CHECK_TOKEN_FAIL:    "Token鉴权失败",
        ERROR_AUTH_CHECK_TOKEN_TIMEOUT: "Token已超时",
        ERROR_AUTH_TOKEN:               "Token生成失败",
        ERROR_AUTH:                     "Token错误",
        TOKEN_INVALID:                  "登陆失效",
}

func GetMsg(code int) string {
        msg, ok := MsgFlags[code]
        if ok {
                return msg
        }

        return MsgFlags[ERROR]
}

package util

import (
        "crypto/md5"
        "crypto/sha1"
        "encoding/hex"
        "fmt"
        "io"
)

func EncodeMD5(value string) string {
        m := md5.New()
        m.Write([]byte(value))

        return hex.EncodeToString(m.Sum(nil))
}

func EncodeSHA1(data string) string {
        t := sha1.New()
        io.WriteString(t, data)
        return fmt.Sprintf("%x", t.Sum(nil))
}

package util

import (
        "fmt"
        "math/rand"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

func GenCode() string {
        rand1 := rand.New(rand.NewSource(time.Now().UnixNano()))
        str := fmt.Sprintf("%s%s%d", "MT", time.Now().Format("20060102"), rand1.Int())
        return str[:18]
}

func RandomString(length int) string {
        rand1 := rand.New(rand.NewSource(time.Now().UnixNano()))
        str := fmt.Sprintf("%d", rand1.Int())
        return str[:length]
}

func GetQueryParams(c *gin.Context) map[string]interface{} {
        params := make(map[string]interface{})

        for key, values := range c.Request.URL.Query() {
                if len(values) > 0 {
                        if len(values) == 1 {
                                params[key] = values[0]
                        } else {
                                params[key] = values
                        }
                }
        }
        return params
}

func ParseDateTime(s string, loc *time.Location, format string) (*time.Time, error) {
        s = strings.TrimSpace(s)
        if s == "" {
                return nil, nil
        }
        if loc == nil {
                loc = time.Local
        }
        if format == "" {
                format = "2006-01-02 15:04"
        }
        t, err := time.ParseInLocation(format, s, loc)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &t, nil
}

package config

import (
        "flag"
        "fmt"
        "log"
        "sync"
        "time"

        "github.com/fsnotify/fsnotify"
        "github.com/spf13/pflag"
        "github.com/spf13/viper"
        "gopkg.in/ini.v1"
)

type Config struct {
        BasePath        string
        AppSetting      *App
        ServerSetting   *Server
        DatabaseSetting *Database
        RedisSetting    *Redis
        WxappSetting    *WxApp
        OssSetting      *OssConfig
}

var C Config

// viperConfig 全局配置变量
var viperConfig *viper.Viper
var V *viper.Viper
var ENV string

// setupOnce 确保 Setup 只被调用一次
var setupOnce sync.Once

const (
        ENV_DEV  = "dev"
        ENV_TEST = "test"
        ENV_PRD  = "prd"
)

func Setup(basePath string, environment string) (any, error) {
        var result any
        var setupErr error

        setupOnce.Do(func() {
                ENV = environment

                // 只在第一次调用时定义 flag，避免重复定义
                if !flag.Parsed() {
                        flag.String("env", "dev", "可选的参数为:dev,test,prod")
                        pflag.CommandLine.AddGoFlagSet(flag.CommandLine)
                        pflag.Parse()
                        viper.BindPFlags(pflag.CommandLine)
                }

                //path , _ := os.Getwd()

                result = InitViper(environment, basePath)

                C.BasePath = basePath
                C.AppSetting = &App{}
                C.ServerSetting = &Server{}
                C.DatabaseSetting = &Database{}
                C.OssSetting = &OssConfig{}
                C.WxappSetting = &WxApp{}
                C.RedisSetting = &Redis{}
                initializeSetting(basePath) //这里放了logger的配置，后续移出到config里
        })

        return result, setupErr
}

func Get(name string) map[string]any {
        return viper.GetStringMap(name)
}

func InitViper(env string, basePath string) interface{} {
        viper.AddConfigPath(basePath)             //执行test会用到这里
        viper.AddConfigPath(basePath + "/config") //gin启动会读这里
        viper.SetConfigType("json")

        // 1. 先读入基础配置
        viper.SetConfigName("config/env.json")
        if err := viper.ReadInConfig(); err != nil {
                panic(err)
        }

        // 2. 读入环境覆盖配置
        viper.SetConfigName(fmt.Sprintf("config/env_%s.json", env))
        if err := viper.MergeInConfig(); err != nil {
                panic(err)
        }

        // viper.SetConfigName("env_" + env)
        // error := viper.ReadInConfig()
        // if error != nil {
        //         panic(error)
        // }

        //监听配置文件修改
        viper.WatchConfig()
        viper.OnConfigChange(func(e fsnotify.Event) {
                // 配置文件发生变更之后会调用的回调函数
                // logger.Warnf("Config file changed: %s", e.Name)
        })
        viperConfig = viper.GetViper()
        c := viper.AllSettings() //获取所有配置
        return c
}

// GetConfig 获取全局配置
func GetConfig() *viper.Viper {
        return viperConfig
}

// 获取数据库配置信息
func GetDatabaseInfo() map[string]interface{} {
        return viper.GetStringMap("db")
}

// 获取数据库配置信息
func GetDatabaseInfo2() map[string]interface{} {
        return viper.GetStringMap("db2")
}

// 获取环境变量
func GetEnvInfo(env string) string {
        viper.AutomaticEnv()
        return viper.GetString(env)
}

var cfg *ini.File

// Setup initialize the configuration instance
func initializeSetting(base string) {
        var err error
        if base == "" {
                base = "."
        }
        cfg, err = ini.Load(base + "/config/app.ini")
        if err != nil {
                log.Fatalf("setting.Setup, fail to parse 'conf/app.ini': %v", err)
        }

        mapTo("app", C.AppSetting)
        mapTo("server", C.ServerSetting)
        mapTo("database", C.DatabaseSetting)
        mapTo("redis", C.RedisSetting)
        mapTo("wxapp", C.WxappSetting)
        mapTo("oss", C.OssSetting)

        C.ServerSetting.ReadTimeout = C.ServerSetting.ReadTimeout * time.Second
        C.ServerSetting.WriteTimeout = C.ServerSetting.WriteTimeout * time.Second
        C.RedisSetting.IdleTimeout = C.RedisSetting.IdleTimeout * time.Second
}

// mapTo map section
func mapTo(section string, v interface{}) {
        err := cfg.Section(section).MapTo(v)
        if err != nil {
                log.Fatalf("Cfg.MapTo %s err: %v", section, err)
        }
}

// IsTesting 检测是否在测试环境中运行
// 通过检查 Go 测试框架的标志来判断是否在测试环境中运行
func IsTesting() bool {
        return flag.Lookup("test.v") != nil
}

package database

import (
        "fmt"
        "hahacking/config"
        configuration "hahacking/config"
        "log"
        "os"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "gorm.io/driver/mysql"
        "gorm.io/driver/sqlite"
        "gorm.io/gorm"
        "gorm.io/gorm/logger"
        "gorm.io/gorm/schema"
)

type Config struct {
        Driver   string // 可选: "postgres", "mysql", "sqlite"
        DSN      string // 连接字符串
        LogLevel int    // GORM 日志级别
}

var (
        Conn     *gorm.DB
        LogLevel = logger.Info // 默认日志级别
)

// SetLogLevel 设置 GORM 日志级别
func SetLogLevel(level logger.LogLevel) {
        LogLevel = level
}

func Setup() {
        if Conn == nil {
                initDB()
        }
}
func GetConn() *gorm.DB {
        return Conn
}

func initDB() (*gorm.DB, error) {
        serverSetting := config.Get("server")
        mode := serverSetting["mode"].(string)
        if mode == "prd" {
                SetLogLevel(logger.Silent)
        }
        dbConfig := configuration.GetDatabaseInfo()
        newLogger := logger.New(
                log.New(os.Stdout, "\r\n", log.LstdFlags), // io writer（日志输出的目标，前缀和日志包含的内容——译者注）
                logger.Config{
                        SlowThreshold:             time.Second, // 慢 SQL 阈值
                        LogLevel:                  LogLevel,
                        IgnoreRecordNotFoundError: true,  // 忽略ErrRecordNotFound（记录未找到）错误
                        Colorful:                  false, // 禁用彩色打印
                },
        )
        // log.Println("xxxxxxxxxxxxdbConfig", dbConfig)
        var dialector gorm.Dialector
        driver := dbConfig["driver"].(string)
        // dsn := dbConfig["dsn"].(string)
        // 选择数据库驱动并初始化
        var err error
        var db *gorm.DB
        // log.Printf("db driver %v", driver)
        switch driver {
        // case "postgres":
        //         dialector = postgres.Open(dsn)
        //         db, err = gorm.Open(dialector, &gorm.Config{})
        case "mysql":
                dsn := fmt.Sprintf("%s:%s@tcp(%s:%s)/%s?charset=utf8mb4&parseTime=True&loc=Local",
                        dbConfig["user"].(string),
                        dbConfig["password"].(string),
                        dbConfig["host"].(string),
                        dbConfig["port"].(string), dbConfig["database"].(string))
                mysqlConfig := mysql.Config{
                        DSN:                       dsn,
                        DefaultStringSize:         256,
                        DisableDatetimePrecision:  true,
                        DontSupportRenameIndex:    true,
                        DontSupportRenameColumn:   true,
                        SkipInitializeWithVersion: false,
                }
                dialector = mysql.New(mysqlConfig)
                db, err = gorm.Open(dialector, &gorm.Config{
                        NamingStrategy: schema.NamingStrategy{
                                TablePrefix:   "t_",                              // 表名前缀，`User`表为`t_users`
                                SingularTable: true,                              // 使用单数表名
                                NameReplacer:  strings.NewReplacer("CID", "Cid"), // 在转为数据库名称之前，使用NameReplacer更改结构/字段名称。
                        },
                        Logger: newLogger,
                        // 禁用迁移时自动创建物理外键约束
                        DisableForeignKeyConstraintWhenMigrating: true,
                })
                // 获取通用数据库对象 sql.DB，然后使用其配置连接池
                sqlDB, err := db.DB()
                if err != nil {
                        fmt.Printf("get sql.DB error %v", err)
                }

                // SetMaxIdleConns 设置空闲连接池中连接的最大数量
                sqlDB.SetMaxIdleConns(10)
                // SetMaxOpenConns 设置打开数据库连接的最大数量
                sqlDB.SetMaxOpenConns(100)
                // SetConnMaxLifetime 设置了连接可复用的最大时间
                sqlDB.SetConnMaxLifetime(time.Hour)
        case "sqlite":
                // log.Println("xxxxxxxxxxxxdb sqlite", dsn)
                dsn := dbConfig["dsn"].(string)
                dialector = sqlite.Open(dsn)
                db, err = gorm.Open(dialector, &gorm.Config{
                        NamingStrategy: schema.NamingStrategy{
                                TablePrefix:   "t_",                              // 表名前缀，`User`表为`t_users`
                                SingularTable: true,                              // 使用单数表名
                                NameReplacer:  strings.NewReplacer("CID", "Cid"), // 在转为数据库名称之前，使用NameReplacer更改结构/字段名称。
                        },
                        Logger: newLogger,
                })
                // sqlDB, err := db.DB()
                // if err != nil {
                //         panic("failed to get sql.DB")
                // }
                // sqlDB.SetMaxOpenConns(1) // SQLite typically works best with a single connection
                // sqlDB.SetMaxIdleConns(1)
        default:
                log.Fatal("Unsupported database driver:", driver)
        }
        // 连接数据库
        if err != nil {
                log.Fatal("Failed to connect to database:", err)
        }

        Conn = db

        return db, err
}

package datatypes

import (
        "database/sql/driver"
        "encoding/json"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "strings"

        "gorm.io/gorm"
        "gorm.io/gorm/clause"
        "gorm.io/gorm/schema"
)

// JSON defined JSON data type, need to implements driver.Valuer, sql.Scanner interface
type JSON json.RawMessage

// Value return json value, implement driver.Valuer interface
func (j JSON) Value() (driver.Value, error) {
        if len(j) == 0 {
                return nil, nil
        }
        bytes, err := json.RawMessage(j).MarshalJSON()
        return string(bytes), err
}

// Scan scan value into Jsonb, implements sql.Scanner interface
func (j *JSON) Scan(value interface{}) error {
        if value == nil {
                *j = JSON("null")
                return nil
        }
        var bytes []byte
        switch v := value.(type) {
        case []byte:
                bytes = v
        case string:
                bytes = []byte(v)
        default:
                return errors.New(fmt.Sprint("Failed to unmarshal JSONB value:", value))
        }

        result := json.RawMessage{}
        err := json.Unmarshal(bytes, &result)
        *j = JSON(result)
        return err
}

// MarshalJSON to output non base64 encoded []byte
func (j JSON) MarshalJSON() ([]byte, error) {
        return json.RawMessage(j).MarshalJSON()
}

// UnmarshalJSON to deserialize []byte
func (j *JSON) UnmarshalJSON(b []byte) error {
        result := json.RawMessage{}
        err := result.UnmarshalJSON(b)
        *j = JSON(result)
        return err
}

func (j JSON) String() string {
        return string(j)
}

// GormDataType gorm common data type
func (JSON) GormDataType() string {
        return "json"
}

// GormDBDataType gorm db data type
func (JSON) GormDBDataType(db *gorm.DB, field *schema.Field) string {
        switch db.Dialector.Name() {
        case "sqlite":
                return "JSON"
        case "mysql":
                return "JSON"
        case "postgres":
                return "JSONB"
        }
        return ""
}

// JSONQueryExpression json query expression, implements clause.Expression interface to use as querier
type JSONQueryExpression struct {
        column      string
        keys        []string
        hasKeys     bool
        equals      bool
        equalsValue any
}

// JSONQuery query column as json
func JSONQuery(column string) *JSONQueryExpression {
        return &JSONQueryExpression{column: column}
}

// HasKey returns clause.Expression
func (jsonQuery *JSONQueryExpression) HasKey(keys ...string) *JSONQueryExpression {
        jsonQuery.keys = keys
        jsonQuery.hasKeys = true
        return jsonQuery
}

// Keys returns clause.Expression
func (jsonQuery *JSONQueryExpression) Equals(value interface{}, keys ...string) *JSONQueryExpression {
        jsonQuery.keys = keys
        jsonQuery.equals = true
        jsonQuery.equalsValue = value
        return jsonQuery
}

// Build implements clause.Expression
func (jsonQuery *JSONQueryExpression) Build(builder clause.Builder) {
        if stmt, ok := builder.(*gorm.Statement); ok {
                switch stmt.Dialector.Name() {
                case "mysql", "sqlite":
                        switch {
                        case jsonQuery.hasKeys:
                                if len(jsonQuery.keys) > 0 {
                                        builder.WriteString("JSON_EXTRACT(" + stmt.Quote(jsonQuery.column) + ",")
                                        builder.AddVar(stmt, "$."+strings.Join(jsonQuery.keys, "."))
                                        builder.WriteString(") IS NOT NULL")
                                }
                        case jsonQuery.equals:
                                if len(jsonQuery.keys) > 0 {
                                        builder.WriteString("JSON_EXTRACT(" + stmt.Quote(jsonQuery.column) + ",")
                                        builder.AddVar(stmt, "$."+strings.Join(jsonQuery.keys, "."))
                                        builder.WriteString(") = ")
                                        stmt.AddVar(builder, jsonQuery.equalsValue)
                                }
                        }
                case "postgres":
                        switch {
                        case jsonQuery.hasKeys:
                                if len(jsonQuery.keys) > 0 {
                                        stmt.WriteQuoted(jsonQuery.column)
                                        stmt.WriteString("::jsonb")
                                        for _, key := range jsonQuery.keys[0 : len(jsonQuery.keys)-1] {
                                                stmt.WriteString(" -> ")
                                                stmt.AddVar(builder, key)
                                        }

                                        stmt.WriteString(" ? ")
                                        stmt.AddVar(builder, jsonQuery.keys[len(jsonQuery.keys)-1])
                                }
                        case jsonQuery.equals:
                                if len(jsonQuery.keys) > 0 {
                                        builder.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("json_extract_path_text(%v::json,", stmt.Quote(jsonQuery.column)))

                                        for idx, key := range jsonQuery.keys {
                                                if idx > 0 {
                                                        builder.WriteByte(',')
                                                }
                                                stmt.AddVar(builder, key)
                                        }
                                        builder.WriteString(") = ")

                                        if _, ok := jsonQuery.equalsValue.(string); ok {
                                                stmt.AddVar(builder, jsonQuery.equalsValue)
                                        } else {
                                                stmt.AddVar(builder, fmt.Sprint(jsonQuery.equalsValue))
                                        }
                                }
                        }
                }
        }
}
