package datatypes

import (
        "database/sql/driver"
        "encoding/json"
        "log"
        "strings"
)

type Slot []string

//第二种方式
// type Slot struct {
//         Slots []string
// }

// GormDataType -> sets type to binary(16)
func (my Slot) GormDataType() string {
        return "varchar(512)"
}

// Scan --> tells GORM how to receive from the database
func (str *Slot) Scan(value interface{}) error {

        log.Println("value", value)
        if value == nil {
                *str = []string{}
                return nil
        }
        bytes, _ := value.([]byte)
        strvalue := string(bytes)
        if len(strvalue) < 2 {
                *str = []string{}
                return nil
        }
        tmp := strvalue[1 : len(strvalue)-1]
        *str = strings.Split(tmp, "|")
        // *str = Slot{Slots: strings.Split(tmp, "|")}
        return nil
}

// Value -> tells GORM how to save into the database
func (s Slot) Value() (driver.Value, error) {
        if len(s) == 0 {
                return "||", nil
        }

        str := strings.Join(s, "|")
        return "|" + str + "|", nil
}

// UnmarshalJSON implements json.Unmarshaler interface
// This allows Slot to be unmarshaled from JSON array format
func (s *Slot) UnmarshalJSON(data []byte) error {
        // Handle null or empty JSON
        if len(data) == 0 || string(data) == "null" {
                *s = []string{}
                return nil
        }

        // Try to unmarshal as JSON array
        var arr []string
        if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &arr); err != nil {
                return err
        }
        *s = Slot(arr)
        return nil
}

// MarshalJSON implements json.Marshaler interface
// This allows Slot to be marshaled to JSON array format
func (s Slot) MarshalJSON() ([]byte, error) {
        if s == nil {
                return json.Marshal([]string{})
        }
        return json.Marshal([]string(s))
}

package datatypes

import (
        "database/sql/driver"
        "encoding/json"
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        // 示例使用 SQLite 数据库
)

// --- 自定义类型 StringSlice ---

// StringSlice 是 []string 的自定义类型，用于数据库的 JSON 存储
type StringSlice []string

// Value 方法将 StringSlice 转换为 JSON 字符串，以保存到数据库
func (s StringSlice) Value() (driver.Value, error) {
        if s == nil {
                return nil, nil // 如果切片为空，保存为 NULL
        }
        // 将 []string 编码为 JSON 字节数组
        jsonBytes, err := json.Marshal(s)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return string(jsonBytes), nil // 返回 JSON 字符串
}

// Scan 方法从数据库中读取 JSON 字符串，并反序列化回 StringSlice
func (s *StringSlice) Scan(value interface{}) error {
        if value == nil {
                *s = nil // 如果数据库值为 NULL，则将其设为空切片
                return nil
        }

        var bytes []byte
        switch v := value.(type) {
        case []byte:
                bytes = v
        case string:
                bytes = []byte(v)
        default:
                return errors.New(fmt.Sprintf("unsupported type for StringSlice scan: %T", value))
        }

        // 将 JSON 字节数组解码为 []string
        if len(bytes) == 0 { // 处理空字符串情况
                *s = nil
                return nil
        }

        err := json.Unmarshal(bytes, s)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return nil
}

package datatypes

import (
        "database/sql/driver"

        "github.com/google/uuid"
)

//需要mysql8支持 参考来源：https://medium.com/wesionary-team/implementation-of-uuid-and-binary-16-in-gorm-v2-1c329c352c91
//MYTYPE -> new datatype
type UUID uuid.UUID

// StringToMYTYPE -> parse string to MYTYPE
func StringToMYTYPE(s string) (UUID, error) {
        id, err := uuid.Parse(s)
        return UUID(id), err
}

//String -> String Representation of Binary16
func (my UUID) String() string {
        return uuid.UUID(my).String()
}

//GormDataType -> sets type to binary(16)
func (my UUID) GormDataType() string {
        return "binary(16)"
}

func (my UUID) MarshalJSON() ([]byte, error) {
        s := uuid.UUID(my)
        str := "\"" + s.String() + "\""
        return []byte(str), nil
}

func (my *UUID) UnmarshalJSON(by []byte) error {
        s, err := uuid.ParseBytes(by)
        *my = UUID(s)
        return err
}

// Scan --> tells GORM how to receive from the database
func (my *UUID) Scan(value interface{}) error {

        bytes, _ := value.([]byte)
        parseByte, err := uuid.FromBytes(bytes)
        *my = UUID(parseByte)
        return err
}

// Value -> tells GORM how to save into the database
func (my UUID) Value() (driver.Value, error) {
        return uuid.UUID(my).MarshalBinary()
}

package websocket

import (
        "fmt"
        "log"
        "net/http"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/gorilla/websocket"
)

var upgrader = websocket.Upgrader{
        CheckOrigin: func(r *http.Request) bool {
                return true
        },
}

func HandleConnections(c *gin.Context) {
        // 升级HTTP连接为WebSocket
        ws, err := upgrader.Upgrade(c.Writer, c.Request, nil)
        if err != nil {
                log.Printf("Upgrade error: %v", err)
                return
        }
        defer ws.Close()

        // 循环处理消息
        for {
                var msg string
                err := ws.ReadJSON(&msg)
                if err != nil {
                        log.Printf("Read error: %v", err)
                        break
                }

                log.Printf("Received: %s", msg)

                // 回传消息
                reply := fmt.Sprintf("Server received: %s", msg)
                err = ws.WriteJSON(reply)
                if err != nil {
                        log.Printf("Write error: %v", err)
                        break
                }
        }
}

package i18n

import (
        "encoding/json"
        "fmt"
        "hahacking/config"
        "os"
        "path/filepath"
        "strconv"
        "strings"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/nicksnyder/go-i18n/v2/i18n"
        "golang.org/x/text/language"
)

// 全局i18n Bundle
var bundle *i18n.Bundle
var C *gin.Context

// 初始化i18n
func Init() {
        // 创建Bundle，设置默认语言
        bundle = i18n.NewBundle(language.English)
        bundle.RegisterUnmarshalFunc("json", json.Unmarshal)

        // 加载翻译文件
        err := filepath.Walk(config.C.BasePath+"/config/locales", func(path string, info os.FileInfo, err error) error {
                if err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                if info.IsDir() || filepath.Ext(path) != ".json" {
                        return nil
                }
                _, err = bundle.LoadMessageFile(path)
                return err
        })
        if err != nil {
                panic(fmt.Sprintf("加载翻译文件失败: %v", err))
        }
}

// i18n中间件
func Middleware() gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                // 从Accept-Language头获取语言
                acceptLang := c.GetHeader("Accept-Language")
                lang, _ := language.Parse(acceptLang)
                if lang == language.Und { // 未定义语言时使用默认值
                        lang = language.English
                }
                // log.Println("lang", lang)
                // 创建Localizer
                SetupContext(c, lang.String())
                c.Next()
        }
}

// 增加这个方法便于单元测试
func SetupContext(c *gin.Context, lang string) {
        localizer := i18n.NewLocalizer(bundle, lang)
        // log.Println("localizerxxxxxxxxxxx", localizer)
        C = c
        c.Set("i18n", localizer)
}

func T(messageID string, args ...string) string {
        localizer := C.MustGet("i18n").(*i18n.Localizer)
        msg, _ := localizer.Localize(&i18n.LocalizeConfig{
                MessageID: messageID,
                // TemplateData: data,
        })
        if len(args) > 0 {
                for k, v := range args {
                        msg = strings.ReplaceAll(msg, "{"+strconv.Itoa(k)+"}", v)
                }
        }
        return msg
}

func T0(c *gin.Context) func(string, ...interface{}) string {
        localizer := c.MustGet("i18n").(*i18n.Localizer)
        return func(messageID string, data ...interface{}) string {
                var templateData map[string]interface{}
                if len(data) > 0 {
                        if m, ok := data[0].(map[string]interface{}); ok {
                                templateData = m
                        }
                }
                msg, err := localizer.Localize(&i18n.LocalizeConfig{
                        MessageID:    messageID,
                        TemplateData: templateData,
                })
                if err != nil {
                        return messageID // 翻译失败时返回ID
                }
                return msg
        }
}

package middleware

import (
        "hahacking/utils/acl"
        "hahacking/utils/cache"
        "hahacking/utils/response"
        "log"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

func ACL() gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                var userName = c.GetHeader("x-requested-with")
                if userName == "" {
                        response.Error(c, "header miss userName", nil)
                        c.Abort()
                        return
                }
                path := c.Request.URL.Path
                method := c.Request.Method
                cacheName := userName + "|" + path + "|" + method
                log.Println("cachename", cacheName)
                // 从缓存中读取&判断
                entry, err := cache.GlobalCache.Get(cacheName)
                log.Println("entry", entry)
                if err == nil && entry != nil {
                        if string(entry) == "true" {
                                c.Next()
                        } else {
                                response.Error(c, "access denied", nil)
                                c.Abort()
                                return
                        }
                } else {
                        // 从数据库中读取&判断
                        //记录日志
                        acl.Enforcer.EnableLog(true)
                        // 加载策略规则
                        err = acl.Enforcer.LoadPolicy()
                        if err != nil {
                                log.Println("loadPolicy error")
                                panic(err)
                        }
                        // 验证策略规则

                        result, err := acl.Enforcer.Enforce(userName, path, method)
                        log.Println("result", result)
                        if err != nil {
                                response.Error(c, "No permission found", nil)
                                c.Abort()
                                return
                        }
                        if !result {
                                // 添加到缓存中
                                cache.GlobalCache.Set(cacheName, []byte("false"))
                                response.Error(c, "access denied", nil)
                                c.Abort()
                                return
                        } else {
                                cache.GlobalCache.Set(cacheName, []byte("true"))
                        }
                        c.Next()
                }
        }
}

package middleware

import (
        "hahacking/service"
        "log"
        "net/http"
        "strconv"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

// 基础认证，支持给group配置后，限定到api一级的权限 - anyone, user, admin (参考userrole对象/表)
func BaseAuth(roles ...string) gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                if len(roles) == 0 {
                        c.Next()
                        return
                }

                // 判断roles中是否包含"anyone"
                for _, role := range roles {
                        if role == "anyone" {
                                c.Next()
                                return
                        }
                }

                userrequired := false
                adminrequired := false
                for _, role := range roles {
                        if role == "user" {
                                userrequired = true
                        }
                        if role == "admin" {
                                adminrequired = true
                        }
                }
                if userrequired {
                        uidStr := c.GetString("uid")
                        uid, _ := strconv.Atoi(uidStr)
                        if uid == 0 {
                                c.JSON(http.StatusUnauthorized, gin.H{"error": "请登录"})
                                c.Abort()
                                return
                        } else {
                                c.Next()
                                return
                        }
                }
                if adminrequired {
                        uidStr := c.GetString("uid")
                        uid, _ := strconv.Atoi(uidStr)
                        userService := service.NewUserService()
                        u, _ := userService.GetById(uid)
                        uroles := u.GetRoles()
                        log.Println("roles", uroles)
                        for _, role := range uroles {
                                if role.Code == "admin" {
                                        c.Next()
                                        return
                                }
                        }
                        c.JSON(http.StatusForbidden, gin.H{"error": "无权限"})
                        c.Abort()
                        return
                }
                c.Next()
        }
}

package middleware

import (
        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/nicksnyder/go-i18n/v2/i18n"
)
func I18n(bundle *i18n.Bundle) gin.HandlerFunc {
    return func(c *gin.Context) {
        lang := c.GetHeader("Accept-Language")
        if lang == "" {
            lang = "en"
        }
        localizer := i18n.NewLocalizer(bundle, lang)
        c.Set("localizer", localizer)
        c.Next()
    }
}
package middleware

import (
        "hahacking/service"
        "hahacking/utils/response"
        "net/http"
        "strconv"
        "strings"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

// JWT中间件，支持跳过公开路由
func JWTAuth(publicPaths ...string) gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                // // 检查当前路径是否在公开路径列表中 - 不需要了，改用BaseAuth。这里只检查jwt有效性及refresh
                currentPath := c.Request.URL.Path
                for _, path := range publicPaths {
                        if strings.HasPrefix(currentPath, path) {
                                c.Next() // 公开路由，直接放行
                                return
                        }
                }

                // 以下为受保护路由的JWT验证逻辑
                tokenString := c.GetHeader("Authorization")
                if tokenString == "" {
                        c.JSON(http.StatusUnauthorized, gin.H{"error": "缺少认证令牌"})
                        c.Abort()
                        return
                }

                if len(tokenString) > 7 && tokenString[:7] == "Bearer " {
                        tokenString = tokenString[7:]
                } else {
                        c.JSON(http.StatusUnauthorized, response.Response{
                                Code:    response.ErrorCodeTokenInvalid,
                                Message: "无效的令牌",
                                Data:    nil,
                        })
                        c.Abort()
                        return
                }
                userService := service.NewUserService()
                claims, err := userService.ParseJWT(tokenString)

                if err != nil {
                        c.JSON(http.StatusUnauthorized, response.Response{
                                Code:    response.ErrorCodeTokenExpired,
                                Message: "令牌已过期",
                                Data:    err.Error(),
                        })
                        c.Abort()
                        return
                }

                c.Set("username", claims.Username)
                c.Set("uid", claims.Uid)
                c.Next()
        }
}

func GetCurrentUser(c *gin.Context) (string, uint, bool) {
        username, ok1 := c.Get("username")
        uidStr, ok2 := c.Get("uid")
        if !ok1 || !ok2 {
                return "", 0, false
        }

        // Convert uid from string to uint
        uid, err := strconv.ParseUint(uidStr.(string), 10, 32)
        if err != nil {
                return "", 0, false
        }

        return username.(string), uint(uid), true
}

package middleware

import (
        "bytes"
        "fmt"
        "hahacking/common/logger"
        "io"
        "time"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

// CustomLogger 自定义Gin日志中间件，使用统一的日志系统
func CustomLogger() gin.HandlerFunc {
        return gin.LoggerWithFormatter(func(param gin.LogFormatterParams) string {
                // 构建日志消息
                var statusColor, methodColor, resetColor string
                if param.IsOutputColor() {
                        statusColor = param.StatusCodeColor()
                        methodColor = param.MethodColor()
                        resetColor = param.ResetColor()
                }

                if param.Latency > time.Minute {
                        param.Latency = param.Latency.Truncate(time.Second)
                }

                // 使用统一的日志格式
                logMessage := fmt.Sprintf("[GIN] %v | %3d %s | %13v | %15s | %s %s %s %s",
                        // param.TimeStamp.Format("2006/01/02 - 15:04:05"),
                        statusColor, param.StatusCode, resetColor,
                        param.Latency,
                        param.ClientIP,
                        methodColor, param.Method, resetColor,
                        param.Path,
                )

                // 如果有错误，添加到日志中
                if param.ErrorMessage != "" {
                        logMessage += " | " + param.ErrorMessage
                }

                // 使用统一的日志系统记录
                logger.Info(logMessage)
                return ""
        })
}

// CustomLoggerWithBody 带请求体的日志中间件
func CustomLoggerWithBody() gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                // 读取请求体
                var bodyBytes []byte
                if c.Request.Body != nil {
                        bodyBytes, _ = io.ReadAll(c.Request.Body)
                        // 重新设置请求体，因为读取后需要重置
                        c.Request.Body = io.NopCloser(bytes.NewBuffer(bodyBytes))
                }

                // 记录请求开始
                start := time.Now()
                path := c.Request.URL.Path
                raw := c.Request.URL.RawQuery

                // 处理请求
                c.Next()

                // 记录请求结束
                latency := time.Since(start)
                clientIP := c.ClientIP()
                method := c.Request.Method
                statusCode := c.Writer.Status()
                errorMessage := c.Errors.ByType(gin.ErrorTypePrivate).String()

                // 构建日志消息
                // logMessage := fmt.Sprintf("[GIN] %v | %3d | %13v | %15s | %s %s",
                // time.Now().Format("2006/01/02 - 15:04:05"),
                logMessage := fmt.Sprintf("[GIN] | %3d | %13v | %15s | %s %s",
                        statusCode,
                        latency,
                        clientIP,
                        method,
                        path,
                )

                if raw != "" {
                        logMessage += "?" + raw
                }

                // 如果有请求体，添加到日志中（限制长度）
                if len(bodyBytes) > 0 && len(bodyBytes) < 1024 {
                        logMessage += " | Body: " + string(bodyBytes)
                }

                // 如果有错误，添加到日志中
                if errorMessage != "" {
                        logMessage += " | Error: " + errorMessage
                }

                // 根据状态码选择日志级别
                if statusCode >= 500 {
                        logger.Error(logMessage)
                } else if statusCode >= 400 {
                        logger.Warn(logMessage)
                } else {
                        logger.Info(logMessage)
                }
        }
}

package middleware

import (
        "net/url"
        "strings"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

// QueryArrayCompatibilityMiddleware 查询数组兼容性中间件
// 自动将 xxx[]=a&xxx[]=b 格式转换为 xxx=a&xxx=b 格式
func QueryArrayCompatibility() gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                // 获取原始查询参数
                rawQuery := c.Request.URL.RawQuery
                if rawQuery == "" {
                        c.Next()
                        return
                }

                // 解析查询参数
                values, err := url.ParseQuery(rawQuery)
                if err != nil {
                        c.Next()
                        return
                }

                // 处理数组参数兼容性
                normalizedValues := make(url.Values)
                for key, valueList := range values {
                        // 如果key以[]结尾，去掉[]并合并到普通key
                        if strings.HasSuffix(key, "[]") {
                                normalKey := strings.TrimSuffix(key, "[]")
                                // 如果普通key已经存在，合并值
                                if existing, exists := normalizedValues[normalKey]; exists {
                                        normalizedValues[normalKey] = append(existing, valueList...)
                                } else {
                                        normalizedValues[normalKey] = valueList
                                }
                        } else {
                                // 普通参数直接复制
                                normalizedValues[key] = valueList
                        }
                }

                // 重新构建查询字符串
                newQuery := normalizedValues.Encode()
                if newQuery != rawQuery {
                        // 更新请求URL
                        c.Request.URL.RawQuery = newQuery
                }

                c.Next()
        }
}

package middleware

import (
        "net/http"
        "time"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/juju/ratelimit"
)

func RateLimit(fillInterval time.Duration, cap, quantum int64) gin.HandlerFunc {
        bucket := ratelimit.NewBucketWithQuantum(fillInterval, cap, quantum)
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                if bucket.TakeAvailable(1) < 1 {
                        c.String(http.StatusForbidden, "rate limit...")
                        c.Abort()
                        return
                }
                c.Next()
        }
}

package audit

import "reflect"

type Auditable interface {
        IsAuditable()
}

func IsAuditable(dest any) bool {
        // 处理指针和非指针
        if _, ok := dest.(Auditable); ok {
                return true
        }
        // 如果是指针，尝试解引用
        val := reflect.ValueOf(dest)
        if val.Kind() == reflect.Ptr && !val.IsNil() {
                if _, ok := val.Elem().Interface().(Auditable); ok {
                        return true
                }
        }
        return false
}

package audit

/*
TODO auditlog应该用另一个database，这样不会影响主数据库的性能，也方便定期归档
TODO 测试环境，需要把auditlog的表删除，然后重新运行测试
*/

type AuditLog struct {
        ID         uint    `gorm:"primaryKey"`
        TableName  string  `gorm:"type:varchar(255);not null"`
        ObjectName string  `gorm:"type:varchar(255);not null"`
        RecordID   uint    `gorm:"not null"`
        Action     string  `gorm:"type:varchar(50);not null"`
        OldData    *string `gorm:"type:json;null"`
        NewData    string  `gorm:"type:json"`
        ChangedBy  string  `gorm:"type:varchar(100)"`
        ChangedAt  int64   `gorm:"autoCreateTime"`
        IPAddress  string  `gorm:"type:varchar(45)"`
}

type SearchForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        TableName  string `form:"table_name"`
        ObjectName string `form:"object_name"`
        RecordID   uint   `form:"record_id"`
        Action     string `form:"action"`
}

func Search(q SearchForm) ([]AuditLog, int) {
        db := database.Conn
        var os []AuditLog
        count := int64(0)
        stmt := db.Model(&AuditLog{})
        if q.TableName != "" {
                stmt = stmt.Where("table_name = ?", q.TableName)
        }
        if q.ObjectName != "" {
                stmt = stmt.Where("object_name = ?", q.ObjectName)
        }
        if q.RecordID != 0 {
                stmt = stmt.Where("record_id = ?", q.RecordID)
        }
        if q.Action != "" {
                stmt = stmt.Where("action = ?", q.Action)
        }
        stmt.Count(&count).Find(&os)
        return os, int(count)
}

func pushToRedis(auditLog AuditLog) {
        database.Conn.Create(&auditLog) //同步处理先
}

/*
*
弃用！！由于更新时候情况比较复杂，无法拿到更新的id（主要是类似statement.Where("id = ?", id)这种），所以无法记录更新前的数据
@deprecated
*/
func RegisterAuditHooks(db *gorm.DB) {
        // 先移除已存在的钩子，避免重复注册
        db.Callback().Create().Remove("audit_create")
        db.Callback().Update().Remove("audit_update")
        db.Callback().Delete().Remove("audit_delete")

        //如果是auditlog的操作，跳过
        db.Callback().Create().After("gorm:after_create").Register("audit_create", func(db *gorm.DB) {
                if !IsAuditable(db.Statement.Dest) {
                        log.Printf("object %v is not auditable", db.Statement.Dest)
                        return
                }
                //获取对象名称而不是表名
                objid, objname := getRecordID(db)
                if objname == "AuditLog" {
                        return
                }
                log.Printf("auditing object create name:%s, table:%s", objname, db.Statement.Table)

                auditLog := AuditLog{
                        TableName:  db.Statement.Table,
                        ObjectName: objname,
                        RecordID:   objid,
                        Action:     "CREATE",
                        ChangedBy:  getUser(db),
                        IPAddress:  getIP(db),
                }
                newData, _ := json.Marshal(db.Statement.Dest)
                auditLog.NewData = string(newData)
                pushToRedis(auditLog)
        })

        db.Callback().Update().Before("gorm:before_update").Register("audit_update", func(db *gorm.DB) {
                if !IsAuditable(db.Statement.Dest) {
                        log.Printf("object %v is not auditable", db.Statement.Dest)
                        return
                }

                objid, objname, tablename, ok := getUpdateID(db)
                log.Printf("object update id:%d, name:%s, table:%s, ok:%v", objid, objname, tablename, ok)
                if !ok {
                        return
                }

                oldData, err := getRowAsMap(db, tablename, objid)
                if err != nil {
                        // 处理未查到或报错
                }
                log.Println("oldData", oldData)
                auditLog := AuditLog{
                        TableName:  tablename,
                        ObjectName: objname,
                        RecordID:   objid,
                        Action:     "UPDATE",
                        ChangedBy:  getUser(db),
                        IPAddress:  getIP(db),
                }
                oldJSON, _ := json.Marshal(oldData)
                newData, _ := json.Marshal(db.Statement.Dest)
                oldJSONStr := string(oldJSON)
                auditLog.OldData = &oldJSONStr
                auditLog.NewData = string(newData)
                pushToRedis(auditLog)
        })

        db.Callback().Delete().Before("gorm:before_delete").Register("audit_delete", func(db *gorm.DB) {
                if !IsAuditable(db.Statement.Dest) {
                        log.Printf("object %v is not auditable", db.Statement.Dest)
                        return
                }

                objid, objname := getRecordID(db)
                if objname == "AuditLog" {
                        return
                }
                log.Printf("auditing object create name:%s, table:%s", objname, db.Statement.Table)
                oldData := map[string]interface{}{}
                db.Statement.ConnPool.QueryRowContext(context.Background(), "SELECT * FROM "+db.Statement.Table+" WHERE id = ?", db.Statement.Dest).Scan(oldData)

                auditLog := AuditLog{
                        TableName:  db.Statement.Table,
                        ObjectName: objname,
                        RecordID:   objid,
                        Action:     "DELETE",
                        ChangedBy:  getUser(db),
                        IPAddress:  getIP(db),
                }
                oldJSON, _ := json.Marshal(oldData)
                oldJSONStr := string(oldJSON)
                auditLog.OldData = &oldJSONStr
                pushToRedis(auditLog)
        })
}
func getRowAsMap(db *gorm.DB, tablename string, id uint) (map[string]interface{}, error) {
        query := fmt.Sprintf("SELECT * FROM %s WHERE id = ?", tablename)
        // row := db.Statement.ConnPool.QueryRowContext(context.Background(), query, id)
        // 获取列名
        cols, err := db.Statement.ConnPool.QueryContext(context.Background(), query, id)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer cols.Close()
        columns, err := cols.Columns()
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        values := make([]interface{}, len(columns))
        valuePtrs := make([]interface{}, len(columns))
        for i := range columns {
                valuePtrs[i] = &values[i]
        }
        if cols.Next() {
                if err := cols.Scan(valuePtrs...); err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                result := make(map[string]interface{})
                for i, col := range columns {
                        var v interface{}
                        val := values[i]
                        b, ok := val.([]byte)
                        if ok {
                                v = string(b)
                        } else {
                                v = val
                        }
                        result[col] = v
                }
                return result, nil
        }
        return nil, sql.ErrNoRows
}

/*
TODO 这里需要优化，因为Update传入的map，无法拿到id，所以无法记录更新前的数据
*/
func getUpdateID(db *gorm.DB) (uint, string, string, bool) {
        sql := db.Dialector.Explain(db.Statement.SQL.String(), db.Statement.Vars...)
        log.Printf("即将执行的SQL: %s", sql)
        log.Printf("SQL: %s", db.Statement.SQL.String())
        log.Printf("Vars: %v", db.Statement.Vars)
        // 1. struct 类型
        if db.Statement.Schema != nil && len(db.Statement.Schema.PrimaryFields) > 0 {
                for _, pf := range db.Statement.Schema.PrimaryFields {
                        val := reflect.ValueOf(db.Statement.Dest)
                        if val.Kind() == reflect.Ptr {
                                val = val.Elem()
                        }
                        if val.Kind() == reflect.Map {
                                idVal := val.MapIndex(reflect.ValueOf("ID"))
                                if idVal.IsValid() && idVal.Kind() == reflect.Uint {
                                        return uint(idVal.Uint()), "map", db.Statement.Table, true
                                }
                                idVal = val.MapIndex(reflect.ValueOf("id"))
                                if idVal.IsValid() && idVal.Kind() == reflect.Uint {
                                        return uint(idVal.Uint()), "map", db.Statement.Table, true
                                }
                        } else {
                                value, zero := pf.ValueOf(context.Background(), val)
                                if !zero {
                                        if id, ok := value.(uint); ok {
                                                return id, db.Statement.Schema.Name, db.Statement.Table, true
                                        }
                                }
                        }
                }
        }
        // 2. map 类型
        val := reflect.ValueOf(db.Statement.Dest)
        if val.Kind() == reflect.Map {
                idVal := val.MapIndex(reflect.ValueOf("ID"))
                if idVal.IsValid() && idVal.Kind() == reflect.Uint {
                        return uint(idVal.Uint()), "map", db.Statement.Table, true
                }
                idVal = val.MapIndex(reflect.ValueOf("id"))
                if idVal.IsValid() && idVal.Kind() == reflect.Uint {
                        return uint(idVal.Uint()), "map", db.Statement.Table, true
                }
        }
        // 3. WHERE 子句
        if clauseWhere, ok := db.Statement.Clauses["WHERE"]; ok {
                if expr, ok := clauseWhere.Expression.(clause.Expr); ok {
                        for _, v := range expr.Vars {
                                if id, ok := v.(uint); ok {
                                        // 对象名无法直接获得，返回空
                                        return id, "", db.Statement.Table, true
                                }
                        }
                }
        }
        return 0, "", db.Statement.Table, false
}
func getRecordID(db *gorm.DB) (uint, string) {
        if dest, ok := db.Statement.Dest.(interface{ GetID() uint }); ok {
                log.Println("dest.GetID()", dest.GetID())
                return dest.GetID(), ""
        }
        // 反射获取主键值
        // val := reflect.ValueOf(db.Statement.Dest).Elem()
        obj := db.Statement.Dest
        log.Println("obj", obj)
        var objname string

        val := reflect.ValueOf(obj)
        //这里可能是Update传入的map，val.Kind()是map - 比如order.AfterCreate() - 修改ordersn，或者单独修改batch或者bean的库存值
        log.Println("val.Kind()", val.Kind())
        if val.Kind() == reflect.Ptr {
                val = val.Elem()
                if val.Kind() == reflect.Ptr { //在来一次
                        val = val.Elem()
                }
                log.Println("val.Kind() struct", val.Kind())
                objname = val.Type().Name()
        } else {
                objname = reflect.TypeOf(obj).Name()
        }
        idField := val.FieldByName("ID")
        if idField.IsValid() && idField.Type().Kind() == reflect.Uint {
                return uint(idField.Uint()), objname
        }
        return 0, objname
}
func getUser(db *gorm.DB) string {
        if ctx, ok := db.Statement.Context.Value("user").(string); ok {
                return ctx
        }
        return "anonymous"
}

func getIP(db *gorm.DB) string {
        if ctx, ok := db.Statement.Context.(*gin.Context); ok {
                return ctx.ClientIP()
        }
        return "unknown"
}

        Moisture          float64       `json:"moisture"`
        Density           float64       `json:"density"` //堆积密度 单位：g/cm3
        Mesh              string        `json:"mesh"`    //目数 14/18/20/24/30/40/60/80/100/120/140/170/200/230/270/325/400/500/630/800/1000/1250/1500/2000
        RawStock          int           `json:"rawstock" gorm:"column:rawstock"`
        RawStockAlert     int           `json:"rawstockalert" gorm:"column:rawstockalert;default:2000"` //生豆库存预警值
        RoastedStock      int           `json:"roastedstock" gorm:"column:roastedstock"`
        RoastedStockAlert int           `json:"roastedstockalert" gorm:"column:roastedstockalert;default:500"` //熟豆库存预警值
        PurchasePrice     float64       `json:"purchaseprice" gorm:"column:purchaseprice;type:decimal(10,2)"`
        BPrice            float64       `json:"bprice" gorm:"column:bprice;type:decimal(10,2)"`
        CPrice            float64       `json:"cprice" gorm:"column:cprice;type:decimal(10,2)"`
        Owner             string        `json:"owner"`
        Description       string        `json:"description"`
        Prices            []price.Price `json:"prices" gorm:"-"`
        Tags              []tag.Tag     `json:"tags" gorm:"-"`
        Cover             string        `json:"cover"` //封面图片url

        //标签 始终报错
        // Taggings []tag.Tagging `gorm:"polymorphic:Target"`
        // Tags     []tag.Tag     `gorm:"many2many:t_taggings;joinForeignKey:TargetID;joinReferences:TagID;polymorphic:Target;polymorphicValue:bean"`
}

type BeanType string

var BeanTypes = map[BeanType]string{
        BeanTypeGreen:   "生豆",
        BeanTypeRoasted: "熟豆",
}

const (
        BeanTypeGreen   BeanType = "green"
        BeanTypeRoasted BeanType = "roasted"
        BeanTypeBlended BeanType = "blended"
        BeanTypeGround  BeanType = "ground"
        BeanTypeExtract BeanType = "extract"
        BeanTypeCapsule BeanType = "capsule"
        BeanTypePod     BeanType = "pod"
        BeanTypeOther   BeanType = "other"
)

type BeanStatus string

const (
        BeanStatusValid   BeanStatus = "valid"
        BeanStatusInvalid BeanStatus = "invalid"
)

var BeanStatuses = map[BeanStatus]string{
        BeanStatusValid:   "有效",
        BeanStatusInvalid: "无效",
}

func (b Bean) IsAuditable() {}

func Meta(name string) map[string]any {
        names := strings.Split(name, ",")
        result := make(map[string]any)
        for _, n := range names {
                if n == "BeanStatus" {
                        result[n] = models.ConvertMapToArray(BeanStatuses)
                }
                if n == "BeanType" {
                        result[n] = models.ConvertMapToArray(BeanTypes)
                }
                if n == "BatchStatus" {
                        result[n] = models.ConvertMapToArray(BatchStatuses)
                }
                if n == "BatchType" {
                        result[n] = models.ConvertMapToArray(BatchTypes)
                }
                if n == "StockLogType" {
                        result[n] = models.ConvertMapToArray(StockLogTypes)
                }
                if n == "StockLogInType" {
                        result[n] = models.ConvertMapToArray(StockLogInTypes)
                }
                if n == "StockLogOutType" {
                        result[n] = models.ConvertMapToArray(StockLogOutTypes)
                }
        }
        return result
}

func (s *Bean) AfterFind(tx *gorm.DB) (err error) {
        // s.StatusL = translateDeliveryType(s.Status)
        // s.ProcessL = translateDeliveryType(s.Process)
        return
}
func (s *Bean) AfterUpdate(tx *gorm.DB) (err error) {
        var exprovider supplier.Supplier
        err = tx.Where("name = ?", s.Provider).First(&exprovider).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound { //已经存在该名称的供应商记录
        } else {
                if exprovider.ID == 0 {
                        supplier.Update(&supplier.Supplier{
                                Name:        s.Provider,
                                Status:      supplier.StatusValid,
                                Type:        supplier.SupplierTypeBean,
                                Description: "生豆供应商（" + s.Name + ")",
                        })
                } else {
                        if exprovider.Status != supplier.StatusValid {
                                exprovider.Status = supplier.StatusValid
                                tx.Save(&exprovider)
                        }
                }
        }
        return nil
}
func translateDeliveryType(deliveryType string) string {
        switch deliveryType {
        case "express":
                return "快递"
        case "pickup":
                return "自提"
        default:
                return deliveryType
        }
}

// 获取当前 豆子种类数量、生豆数量/价值、熟豆数量价值
func GetTotal() (map[string]any, error) {
        db := database.Conn

        // 定义结果结构体
        type TotalResult struct {
                Count              int64   `json:"count"`
                GreenTotal         int64   `json:"green_total"`
                RoastedTotal       int64   `json:"roasted_total"`
                GreenTotalAmount   float64 `json:"green_total_amount"`
                RoastedTotalAmount float64 `json:"roasted_total_amount"`
                GreenCount         int64   `json:"green_count"`   // rawstock > 0 的个数
                RoastedCount       int64   `json:"roasted_count"` // roastedstock > 0 的个数
        }

        var result TotalResult

        // 使用单个SQL查询获取所有统计数据
        err := db.Model(&Bean{}).
                Where("status = ?", BeanStatusValid).
                Select(`
                        COUNT(*) as count,
                        COALESCE(SUM(IFNULL(rawstock, 0)), 0) as green_total,
                        COALESCE(SUM(IFNULL(roastedstock, 0)), 0) as roasted_total,
                        COALESCE(SUM(IFNULL(purchaseprice, 0) * IFNULL(rawstock, 0) /1000), 0) as green_total_amount,
                        COALESCE(SUM(IFNULL(cprice, 0) * IFNULL(roastedstock, 0) /1000), 0) as roasted_total_amount,
                        COUNT(CASE WHEN IFNULL(rawstock, 0) > 0 THEN 1 END) as green_count,
                        COUNT(CASE WHEN IFNULL(roastedstock, 0) > 0 THEN 1 END) as roasted_count
                `).
                Scan(&result).Error

        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }

        return map[string]any{
                "total":              result.Count,
                "greenTotal":         result.GreenTotal,
                "roastedTotal":       result.RoastedTotal,
                "greenTotalAmount":   result.GreenTotalAmount,
                "roastedTotalAmount": result.RoastedTotalAmount,
                "greenCount":         result.GreenCount,
                "roastedCount":       result.RoastedCount,
        }, nil
}
func GetById(id uint) (*Bean, error) {
        var o Bean
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("id = ?", id).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}
func GetByCode(code string) (*Bean, error) {
        var o Bean
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("code = ?", code).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}

type SearchForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        Name   string `form:"name"`
        Code   string `form:"code"`
        Status string `form:"status"`
        Owner  string `form:"owner"`
}

func Search(q SearchForm) ([]Bean, int) {
        db := database.Conn
        var os []Bean

        var statement = db.Model(&Bean{})
        if q.Name != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("name LIKE ?", "%"+q.Name+"%")
        }
        if q.Code != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("code LIKE ?", "%"+q.Code+"%")
        }
        if q.Status != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", q.Status)
        } else {
                statement = statement.Where("status != ?", "invalid")
        }
        if q.Owner != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("owner = ?", q.Owner)
        }
        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()
        var count int64
        statement.
                Count(&count).
                Order(sort).
                Offset(start).
                Limit(pagesize).
                Find(&os)
        return os, int(count)
}

func Update(o *Bean) (*Bean, error) {
        db := database.Conn
        //TODO 重构为form并validate
        if o.ID != 0 {
                if err := db.Model(&Bean{}).Where("id = ?", o.ID).Updates(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
        } else {
                if err := db.Create(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
        }
        o.GeneratePrices(false)
        return o, nil
}

func (bean Bean) GeneratePrices(overwrite bool) (err error) {
        db := database.Conn
        f := price.SearchLevelForm{Status: "valid"}
        allLevels, _ := price.SearchLevel(f)
        for _, level := range allLevels {
                base := bean.CPrice //统一为零售基础价为基底
                priceValue := base * level.Discount
                //取整
                priceValue = math.Round(priceValue)
                newprice := price.Price{BeanID: bean.ID, PriceLevelID: level.ID, Price: priceValue, Status: "valid"}
                var existingPrice price.Price
                if err := db.Where("bean_id = ? AND pricelevel_id = ?", bean.ID, level.ID).First(&existingPrice).Error; err == nil {
                        // Update the existing price
                        if overwrite {
                                existingPrice.Price = priceValue
                                if err := db.Save(&existingPrice).Error; err != nil {
                                        return err
                                }
                        }
                        continue
                } else if err := db.Create(&newprice).Error; err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }

        return nil
}
func GetAllPriceList(leveltype price.LevelType) ([]price.Price, error) {
        var prices []price.Price
        form := price.SearchForm{
                Status: "valid",
        }
        if leveltype != "" {
                form.LevelType = string(leveltype)
        }
        prices, _ = price.Search(form)
        return prices, nil
}
func (bean Bean) GetPriceList(leveltype price.LevelType) ([]price.Price, error) {
        var prices []price.Price
        form := price.SearchForm{
                Status: "valid",
                BeanID: bean.ID,
        }
        if leveltype != "" {
                form.LevelType = string(leveltype)
        }
        prices, _ = price.Search(form)
        return prices, nil
}

func GetPrices(ids []uint, levelid uint) (map[uint]float64, error) {
        var prices []price.Price
        db := database.Conn
        var statement = db.Model(&price.Price{})
        statement.Where("bean_id IN (?)", ids)
        statement.Where("pricelevel_id", levelid)
        err := statement.Find(&prices).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        var mapping = make(map[uint]float64)
        for _, price := range prices {
                mapping[price.BeanID] = price.Price
        }
        return mapping, nil
}

func (bean Bean) Delete() error {
        db := database.Conn
        if err := db.Delete(&Bean{}, bean.ID).Error; err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return nil
}
func (bean Bean) Remove() error {
        db := database.Conn
        if err := db.Model(&Bean{}).Where("id = ?", bean.ID).Updates(map[string]any{"status": "invalid"}).Error; err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return nil
}

func contains(slice []string, item string) bool {
        for _, v := range slice {
                if v == item {
                        return true
                }
        }
        return false
}

package bean

import (
        "errors"
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/database/datatypes"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/utils"
        "strconv"
)

type BeanBatch struct {
        models.Model
        BeanID     uint                  `json:"bean_id"`
        Bean       *Bean                 `json:"bean" gorm:"foreignKey:BeanID;references:ID"`
        Seq        int                   `json:"seq"`
        Date       string                `json:"date"`
        BeanType   BeanType              `json:"bean_type"`
        Status     BatchStatus           `json:"status"`
        StatusL    string                `json:"statusL" gorm:"-"`
        TotalPrice float64               `json:"totalprice" gorm:"column:totalprice;type:decimal(10,2)"`
        TotalQty   int                   `json:"totalqty" gorm:"column:totalqty;default:0;not null;type:int"`
        CurrentQty int                   `json:"currentqty" gorm:"column:currentqty;default:0;not null;type:int"` //批次在更新前的数量
        Memo       string                `json:"memo"`
        Type       StockLogType          `json:"type"`
        Receipts   datatypes.StringSlice `json:"receipts" gorm:"type:text"` //采购凭证图片
}

// 记录批次数量变化：采购生/熟豆+、烘焙生豆-、销售熟豆订单-、撤销销售熟豆订单+
type BeanBatchLog struct {
        models.Model
        BeanBatchID uint       `json:"beanbatch_id" gorm:"column:beanbatch_id"`
        BeanBatch   *BeanBatch `json:"beanbatch" gorm:"foreignKey:BeanBatchID;references:ID"`
        BeanID      uint       `json:"bean_id" gorm:"column:bean_id"`
        Bean        *Bean      `json:"bean" gorm:"foreignKey:BeanID;references:ID"`
        BeanType    BeanType   `json:"bean_type" gorm:"column:bean_type"`
        Qty         int        `json:"qty" gorm:"column:qty"`
        RefType     string     `json:"ref_type"` //来源类型
        RefID       string     `json:"ref_id"`   //来源ID
}

type BatchStatus string

const (
        BatchStatusInvalid BatchStatus = "invalid" // 无效
        BatchStatusValid   BatchStatus = "valid"   // 有效
)

var BatchStatuses = map[BatchStatus]string{
        BatchStatusInvalid: "无效",
        BatchStatusValid:   "有效",
}

type BatchType string

const (
        BatchTypePurchase BatchType = "purchase"
        BatchTypeProduce  BatchType = "produce"
        BatchTypeSale     BatchType = "sale"
        BatchTypeReturn   BatchType = "return"
        BatchTypeTransfer BatchType = "transfer"
)

var BatchTypes = map[BatchType]string{
        BatchTypePurchase: "采购",
        BatchTypeProduce:  "生产",
        BatchTypeSale:     "销售",
        BatchTypeReturn:   "退货",
        BatchTypeTransfer: "转移",
}

type SearchBatchForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        BeanID   int       `form:"bean_id"`
        Date     string    `form:"date"`
        Status   string    `form:"status"`
        Type     BatchType `form:"type"`
        BeanType string    `form:"bean_type"`
}

func GetBatchById(id uint) (*BeanBatch, error) {
        db := database.Conn
        var o BeanBatch
        if err := db.Model(&BeanBatch{}).Where("id = ?", id).First(&o).Error; err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}

func SearchBatch(q SearchBatchForm) ([]BeanBatch, int) {
        db := database.Conn
        var os []BeanBatch
        var statement = db.Model(&BeanBatch{}).Preload("Bean")
        if q.Date != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("date LIKE ?", "%"+q.Date+"%")
        }
        if q.BeanID != 0 {
                statement = statement.Where("bean_id = ?", q.BeanID)
        }
        if q.Type != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("type = ?", q.Type)
        }
        if q.BeanType != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("bean_type = ?", q.BeanType)
        }
        if q.Status != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", q.Status)
        }
        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()

        var count int64
        statement.
                Count(&count).
                Order(sort).
                Offset(start).
                Limit(pagesize).
                Find(&os)
        return os, int(count)
}

func UpdateBatch(o *BeanBatch) (*BeanBatch, error) {
        db := database.Conn

        if o.ID != 0 {
                if err := db.Model(&BeanBatch{}).Where("id = ?", o.ID).Updates(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
        } else {
                o.Status = BatchStatusValid
                o.CurrentQty = o.TotalQty
                if err := db.Create(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
        }

        bean, err := GetById(o.BeanID)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        delta := o.TotalQty
        memo := "批次入库"
        if contains(StockOutTypes(), string(o.Type)) {
                delta = -delta
                memo = "批次出库"
        }

        bean.Stocking(delta, o.BeanType, o.Type, "beanbatch", strconv.Itoa(int(o.ID)), memo)
        return o, nil
}

// 撤销批次 - 需要修改库存回去并且记录日志
func (batch *BeanBatch) Undo() error {
        db := database.Conn
        bean, err := GetById(batch.BeanID)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        bean.Stocking(-batch.TotalQty, batch.BeanType, StockLogTypeUndo, "beanbatch", strconv.Itoa(int(batch.ID)), "批次撤销"+strconv.Itoa(int(batch.ID)))
        return db.Model(&BeanBatch{}).Where("id = ?", batch.ID).Update("status", BatchStatusInvalid).Error
}

func (batch *BeanBatch) Log(qty int, refType string, refID string, memo string) error {
        db := database.Conn
        // TODO 应该增加个字段
        if refType != "user" { //用户操作不检查重复 - 因为同一个人可能会多次操作入库
                var exist BeanBatchLog
                db.Model(&BeanBatchLog{}).
                        Where("ref_type = ? AND ref_id = ? AND beanbatch_id = ? AND bean_type = ?",
                                refType, refID, batch.ID, batch.BeanType).First(&exist)
                if exist.ID > 0 {
                        return errors.New("重复记录")
                }
        }
        db.Create(&BeanBatchLog{
                BeanBatchID: batch.ID,
                BeanType:    batch.BeanType,
                BeanID:      batch.BeanID,
                Qty:         qty,
                RefType:     refType,
                RefID:       refID,
        })
        batch.CurrentQty += qty
        db.Save(batch)
        return nil
}

type SearchBatchLogForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        BeanBatchID uint     `form:"beanbatch_id"`
        BeanID      uint     `form:"bean_id"`
        BeanType    BeanType `form:"bean_type"`
        RefType     string   `form:"ref_type"`
        RefID       string   `form:"ref_id"`
}

func SearchBatchLog(q SearchBatchLogForm) ([]BeanBatchLog, int) {
        db := database.Conn
        var os []BeanBatchLog
        statement := db.Model(&BeanBatchLog{}).Preload("BeanBatch").Preload("Bean")
        if q.BeanBatchID != 0 {
                statement = statement.Where("beanbatch_id = ?", q.BeanBatchID)
        }
        if q.BeanID != 0 {
                statement = statement.Where("bean_id = ?", q.BeanID)
        }
        if q.BeanType != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("bean_type = ?", q.BeanType)
        }
        if q.RefType != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("ref_type = ?", q.RefType)
        }
        if q.RefID != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("ref_id = ?", q.RefID)
        }
        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()
        statement.
                Order(sort).
                Offset(start).
                Limit(pagesize).Find(&os)
        var count int64
        statement.Count(&count)
        return os, int(count)
}

package bean

import (
        "errors"
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/models"

        "hahacking/utils"
)

const (
        FieldRawStock     = "RawStock"
        FieldRoastedStock = "RoastedStock"
)

type BeanStockLog struct {
        models.Model
        ID          uint           `json:"id" form:"id" gorm:"primaryKey"`
        BeanID      uint           `json:"bean_id"`
        Bean        *Bean          `json:"bean" gorm:"foreignKey:BeanID;references:ID"`
        BeanBatchID uint           `json:"beanbatch_id"` //如果是减库存（销售熟豆，烘焙生豆），必须要有batch信息
        BeanType    BeanType       `json:"bean_type" gorm:"column:bean_type"`
        Qty         int            `json:"qty"`
        FromQty     int            `json:"fromqty" gorm:"column:fromqty"`
        ToQty       int            `json:"toqty" gorm:"column:toqty"`
        Type        StockLogType   `json:"type"`
        Status      StockLogStatus `json:"status"`
        StatusL     string         `json:"statusL" gorm:"-"`
        SrcType     string         `json:"src_type"`
        SrcID       string         `json:"src_id"`
        Memo        string         `json:"memo"`
}
type StockLogStatus string

const (
        StockLogStatusValid   StockLogStatus = "valid"
        StockLogStatusInvalid StockLogStatus = "invalid"
)

type StockLogType string

const (
        StockLogTypeProduce     StockLogType = "produce"
        StockLogTypePurchase    StockLogType = "purchase"
        StockLogTypeSale        StockLogType = "sale"
        StockLogTypeReturn      StockLogType = "return"
        StockLogTypeTransferIn  StockLogType = "transferin"
        StockLogTypeTransferOut StockLogType = "transferout"
        StockLogTypeWaste       StockLogType = "waste"
        StockLogTypeUndo        StockLogType = "undo" //撤销
)

var StockLogTypes = map[StockLogType]string{
        StockLogTypeProduce:     "生产",
        StockLogTypePurchase:    "采购",
        StockLogTypeSale:        "销售",
        StockLogTypeReturn:      "退货",
        StockLogTypeTransferIn:  "转入",
        StockLogTypeTransferOut: "转出",
        StockLogTypeWaste:       "损耗",
        StockLogTypeUndo:        "撤销", //注意撤销可能是入库，也可能是出库
}
var StockLogInTypes = map[StockLogType]string{
        StockLogTypeProduce:    "生产",
        StockLogTypePurchase:   "采购",
        StockLogTypeReturn:     "退货",
        StockLogTypeTransferIn: "转入",
}
var StockLogOutTypes = map[StockLogType]string{
        StockLogTypeSale:        "销售",
        StockLogTypeTransferOut: "转出",
        StockLogTypeWaste:       "损耗",
}

func StockInTypes() []string {
        inTypes := []string{}
        for k := range StockLogInTypes {
                inTypes = append(inTypes, string(k))
        }
        return inTypes
}
func StockOutTypes() []string {
        outTypes := []string{}
        for k := range StockLogOutTypes {
                outTypes = append(outTypes, string(k))
        }
        return outTypes
}

type SearchStockLogForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        BeanID   int    `form:"bean_id"`
        BeanName string `form:"bean_name"`
        BeanType string `form:"bean_type"`
        Date     string `form:"date"`
        Status   string `form:"status"`
        Type     string `form:"type"`
        SrcType  string `form:"src_type"`
        SrcID    string `form:"src_id"`
}

func SearchStockLog(q SearchStockLogForm) ([]BeanStockLog, int) {
        db := database.Conn
        var os []BeanStockLog

        var statement = db.Model(&BeanStockLog{}).Preload("Bean")
        if q.Date != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("created_at LIKE ?", "%"+q.Date+"%")
        }
        if q.BeanID != 0 {
                statement = statement.Where("bean_id = ?", q.BeanID)
        }
        if q.BeanName != "" {
                statement = statement.Joins("Bean").Where("Bean.name LIKE ?", "%"+q.BeanName+"%")
        }
        if q.BeanType != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("bean_type = ?", q.BeanType)
        }
        if q.Type != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("type = ?", q.Type)
        }
        if q.Status != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", q.Status)
        }
        if q.SrcType != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("src_type = ?", q.SrcType)
        }
        if q.SrcID != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("src_id = ?", q.SrcID)
        }
        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()

        var count int64
        statement.
                Count(&count).
                Order(sort).
                Offset(start).
                Limit(pagesize).
                Find(&os)
        return os, int(count)
}

// qty的正负由外部逻辑决定
func (bean *Bean) Stocking(qty int, beanType BeanType, logType StockLogType, srcType string, srcID string, memo string) (*Bean, error) {
        db := database.Conn

        // 避免重复入库
        if srcType != "user" { //用户操作不检查重复
                var exist BeanStockLog
                db.Model(&BeanStockLog{}).Where("type = ? AND src_type = ? AND src_id = ? AND bean_id = ? AND bean_type = ?", logType, srcType, srcID, bean.ID, beanType).First(&exist)
                if exist.ID > 0 {
                        return nil, errors.New("重复库存修改记录")
                }
        }

        fromQty := 0
        toQty := 0

        // Get fresh data after locking
        var freshBean Bean
        if err := db.First(&freshBean, bean.ID).Error; err != nil {
                // tx.Rollback()
                return nil, err
        }

        switch beanType {
        case BeanTypeGreen:
                fromQty = freshBean.RawStock
                toQty = freshBean.RawStock + qty

                if err := db.Model(&bean).Update("rawstock", toQty).Error; err != nil {
                        // tx.Rollback()
                        return nil, err
                }
                // log.Printf("xxxxxxxxxxxxfromQty: %+v", fromQty)
        case BeanTypeRoasted:
                fromQty = freshBean.RoastedStock
                toQty = freshBean.RoastedStock + qty

                if err := db.Model(&bean).Update("roastedstock", toQty).Error; err != nil {
                        // tx.Rollback()
                        return nil, err
                }
        default:
                return nil, errors.New("不支持的豆子类型")
        }

        log := BeanStockLog{
                BeanID:   bean.ID,
                BeanType: beanType,
                Qty:      qty,
                FromQty:  fromQty,
                ToQty:    toQty,
                Type:     logType,
                Status:   StockLogStatusValid,
                SrcType:  srcType,
                SrcID:    srcID,
                Memo:     memo,
        }
        if err := db.Create(&log).Error; err != nil {
                // tx.Rollback()
                return nil, err
        }

        // if err := tx.Commit().Error; err != nil {
        //         tx.Rollback()
        //         return nil, err
        // }

        return bean, nil
}

package bean

import (
        "time"
)

func NewBean() (*Bean, *BeanBatch, error) {
        b := Bean{
                Name:    "test coffee bean" + time.Now().Format("2006-01-02 15:04"),
                Code:    "code",
                Country: "china"}
        Update(&b)

        batch, err := UpdateBatch(&BeanBatch{
                BeanID:     b.ID,
                Type:       StockLogTypePurchase,
                BeanType:   BeanTypeGreen,
                TotalQty:   100,
                TotalPrice: 100,
        })
        if err != nil {
                return nil, nil, err
        }
        return &b, batch, nil
}

package category

import (
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/utils"
        "strings"

        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type Category struct {
        models.Model
        Name     string     `json:"name"`
        Code     string     `json:"code"`
        Type     string     `json:"type"` //page,post,recipe,cupping
        Status   string     `json:"status"`
        ParentID *uint      `json:"parent_id" gorm:"null"`
        Parent   *Category  `json:"parent" gorm:"foreignKey:ParentID;references:ID"`
        Children []Category `json:"children" gorm:"-"`
}

func Meta(name string) map[string]any {
        names := strings.Split(name, ",")
        result := make(map[string]any)
        for _, n := range names {
                if n == "CategoryStatus" {
                        result[n] = models.ConvertMapToArray(models.CommonStatuses)
                }
        }
        return result
}

func GetById(id uint) (*Category, error) {
        var o Category
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("id = ?", id).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}

func (c *Category) Tree(depth int) ([]Category, error) {
        db := database.Conn
        var children []Category
        err := db.Where("parent_id = ?", c.ID).Find(&children).Error
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        // 递归
        if depth != 1 {
                for i := range children {
                        subChildren, err := children[i].Tree(depth - 1)
                        if err != nil {
                                return nil, err
                        }
                        children[i].Children = subChildren
                }
        }
        return children, nil
}

type SearchForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        Name   string `form:"name"`
        BeanID string `form:"beanid"`
        Status string `form:"status"`
}

func Search(q SearchForm) ([]Category, int) {
        db := database.Conn
        var os []Category
        var statement = db.Model(&Category{}).Preload("Parent")
        if q.Name != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("name LIKE ?", "%"+q.Name+"%")
        }
        if q.Status != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", q.Status)
        }
        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()
        var count int64
        statement.
                Count(&count).
                Order(sort).
                Offset(start).
                Limit(pagesize).
                Find(&os)
        return os, int(count)
}

type UpdateForm struct {
        ID          uint   `json:"id"`
        Name        string `json:"name"`
        Code        string `json:"code"`
        Status      string `json:"status"`
        Type        string `json:"type"`
        PublishDate string `json:"publish_date"`
        Content     string `json:"content"`
        Author      string `json:"author"`
        ParentID    *uint  `json:"parent_id"`
        utils.Pager
}

func (form *UpdateForm) Validate() map[string]string {
        var result = make(map[string]string)
        if form.Name == "" {
                result["name"] = "请填写名称"
        }
        if form.Code == "" {
                result["code"] = "请填写编码"
        }
        if form.ParentID == nil {
                result["parent_id"] = "请选择父分类"
        }
        if form.Status == "" {
                result["status"] = "请选择状态"
        }
        return result
}
func Update(o *Category) (*Category, error) {
        db := database.Conn
        tx := db.Begin()
        if o.ID != 0 {
                if err := tx.Model(&Category{}).Where("id = ?", o.ID).Updates(map[string]any{
                        "name":      o.Name,
                        "code":      o.Code,
                        "type":      o.Type,
                        "status":    o.Status,
                        "parent_id": o.ParentID,
                }).Error; err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return nil, err
                }
        } else {
                if err := tx.Create(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return nil, err
                }
        }

        tx.Commit()
        return o, nil
}

func Delete(id int) error {
        db := database.GetConn()
        tx := db.Begin()

        if err := tx.Delete(&Category{}, id).Error; err != nil {
                tx.Rollback()
                return err
        }

        tx.Commit()
        return nil
}

package cupping

import (
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/models/user"
        "hahacking/utils"
        "strings"

        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type Cupping struct {
        models.Model
        Description string           `json:"description"`
        Status      CuppingStatus    `json:"status"`
        StatusL     string           `json:"statusL" gorm:"-"`
        Type        string           `json:"type" gorm:"column:type"`
        Location    string           `json:"location" gorm:"column:location"`
        Method      CuppingMethod    `json:"method" gorm:"column:method"`
        Scores      *[]CuppingScore  `json:"scores" gorm:"foreignKey:CuppingID;references:ID"`
        Samples     *[]CuppingSample `json:"samples" gorm:"foreignKey:CuppingID;references:ID"`
        SampleCount int              `json:"samplecount" gorm:"-"`
        ScoreCount  int              `json:"scorecount" gorm:"-"`
        UserCount   int              `json:"usercount" gorm:"-"`
        UserID      *uint            `json:"user_id"` //创建人
        User        *user.User       `json:"user" gorm:"foreignKey:UserID;references:ID"`
}

func (o *Cupping) AfterFind(tx *gorm.DB) (err error) {
        var count int64
        tx.Model(&CuppingSample{}).Where("cupping_id = ?", o.ID).Count(&count)
        o.SampleCount = int(count)

        var scoreCount int64
        tx.Model(&CuppingScore{}).Where("cupping_id = ?", o.ID).Count(&scoreCount)
        o.ScoreCount = int(scoreCount)
        var userCount int64
        tx.Model(&CuppingScore{}).Where("cupping_id = ?", o.ID).Select("distinct user_id").Count(&userCount)
        o.UserCount = int(userCount)

        return
}

type CuppingStatus string // 杯测状态

const (
        CuppingStatusInvalid CuppingStatus = "invalid"
        CuppingStatusValid   CuppingStatus = "valid"
        CuppingStatusClosed  CuppingStatus = "closed"
)

var CuppingStatuses = map[CuppingStatus]string{
        CuppingStatusInvalid: "无效",
        CuppingStatusValid:   "有效",
        CuppingStatusClosed:  "关闭",
}

type CuppingMethod string // 杯测方法

const (
        CuppingMethodStandard CuppingMethod = "standard"
        CuppingMethodDrip     CuppingMethod = "drip"
        CuppingMethodEspresso CuppingMethod = "espresso"
        CuppingMethodFrench   CuppingMethod = "french"
        CuppingMethodOther    CuppingMethod = "other"
)

var CuppingMethods = map[CuppingMethod]string{
        CuppingMethodStandard: "标准",
        CuppingMethodDrip:     "滴滤",
        CuppingMethodEspresso: "意式",
        CuppingMethodFrench:   "法压",
        CuppingMethodOther:    "其他",
}

func Meta(name string) map[string]any {
        names := strings.Split(name, ",")
        result := make(map[string]any)
        for _, n := range names {
                if n == "CuppingMethod" {
                        result[n] = models.ConvertMapToArray(CuppingMethods)
                }
                if n == "CuppingStatus" {
                        result[n] = models.ConvertMapToArray(CuppingStatuses)
                }
                if n == "CuppingScoreStatus" {
                        result[n] = models.ConvertMapToArray(models.CommonStatuses)
                }
        }
        return result
}

func GetById(id uint) (*Cupping, error) {
        var o Cupping
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("id = ?", id).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}

type SearchForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        Name   string `form:"name"`
        BeanID string `form:"beanid"`
        Status string `form:"status"`
}

func Search(q SearchForm) ([]Cupping, int) {
        db := database.Conn
        var os []Cupping
        var statement = db.Model(&Cupping{}).Preload("User").Preload("Samples.Bean").Preload("Samples.BeanBatch").Preload("Samples").Preload("Scores")
        if q.Name != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("name LIKE ?", "%"+q.Name+"%")
        }
        if q.BeanID != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("bean_id = ?", q.BeanID)
        }
        if q.Status != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", q.Status)
        }
        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()
        var count int64
        statement.
                Count(&count).
                Order(sort).
                Offset(start).
                Limit(pagesize).
                Find(&os)
        return os, int(count)
}

func Update(o *Cupping) error {
        db := database.Conn
        tx := db.Begin()

        if o.ID != 0 {
                if err := tx.Model(&Cupping{}).Where("id = ?", o.ID).Updates(map[string]any{
                        "description": o.Description,
                        "status":      o.Status,
                        "type":        o.Type,
                        "location":    o.Location,
                        "method":      o.Method,
                }).Error; err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return err
                }
        } else {
                if err := tx.Create(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return err
                }
        }

        // Handle samples
        if o.Samples != nil {
                // Get existing samples
                var existingSamples []CuppingSample
                if err := tx.Where("cupping_id = ?", o.ID).Find(&existingSamples).Error; err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return err
                }

                // Create a map of existing samples by ID
                existingMap := make(map[uint]*CuppingSample)
                for i := range existingSamples {
                        existingMap[existingSamples[i].ID] = &existingSamples[i]
                }

                // Process each sample
                for i := range *o.Samples {
                        sample := &(*o.Samples)[i]
                        sample.CuppingID = o.ID

                        if sample.ID != 0 && existingMap[sample.ID] != nil {
                                // Update existing sample
                                if err := tx.Model(&CuppingSample{}).Where("id = ?", sample.ID).Updates(map[string]interface{}{
                                        "bean_id":         sample.BeanID,
                                        "beanbatch_id":    sample.BeanBatchID,
                                        "beanqty":         sample.BeanQty,
                                        "grindsize":       sample.GrindSize,
                                        "watertemp":       sample.WaterTemp,
                                        "extractionyield": sample.ExtractionYield,
                                        "method":          sample.Method,
                                        "triangle":        sample.Triangle,
                                }).Error; err != nil {
                                        tx.Rollback()
                                        return err
                                }
                                delete(existingMap, sample.ID)
                        } else {
                                // Create new sample
                                if err := tx.Create(sample).Error; err != nil {
                                        tx.Rollback()
                                        return err
                                }
                        }
                }

                // Delete samples that are no longer in the list
                for id := range existingMap {
                        if err := tx.Delete(&CuppingSample{}, id).Error; err != nil {
                                tx.Rollback()
                                return err
                        }
                }
        }

        tx.Commit()
        return nil
}

func Delete(id int) error {
        db := database.GetConn()
        tx := db.Begin()

        // Delete associated samples first
        if err := tx.Where("cupping_id = ?", id).Delete(&CuppingSample{}).Error; err != nil {
                tx.Rollback()
                return err
        }

        // Delete associated scores
        if err := tx.Where("cupping_id = ?", id).Delete(&CuppingScore{}).Error; err != nil {
                tx.Rollback()
                return err
        }

        // Delete the cupping record
        if err := tx.Delete(&Cupping{}, id).Error; err != nil {
                tx.Rollback()
                return err
        }

        tx.Commit()
        return nil
}

                Preload("Bean").

package cupping

import (
        "database/sql/driver"
        "encoding/json"
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/database/datatypes"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/models/bean"
        "hahacking/utils"

        "log"

        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type CuppingScore struct {
        models.Model
        UserID          uint       `json:"user_id"`
        BeanID          *uint      `json:"bean_id" gorm:"column:bean_id;null"`
        Bean            *bean.Bean `json:"bean" gorm:"foreignKey:BeanID;references:ID"`
        CuppingID       uint       `json:"cupping_id" gorm:"column:cupping_id"`
        Cupping         *Cupping   `json:"cupping" gorm:"foreignKey:CuppingID;references:ID"`
        CuppingSampleID uint       `json:"cuppingsample_id" gorm:"column:cuppingsample_id"`

        CuppingScoreItem

        Total        float64       `json:"total" gorm:"total"`                 // 总分 //获取汇总时候用到，其实不存储
        Count        int           `json:"count" gorm:"count"`                 // 评分次数 //获取汇总时候会用到，平时不用
        DefectsTotal float64       `json:"defects_total" gorm:"defects_total"` // 瑕疵总分
        Status       models.Status `json:"status"`
}
type CuppingScoreItem struct { //评分项
        Aroma         float64        `json:"aroma" gorm:"not null"`      // 干香
        Fragrance     float64        `json:"fragrance" gorm:"not null"`  // 湿香
        AromaTags     datatypes.Slot `json:"aroma_tags" gorm:"not null"` // 香气标签
        AromaNotes    string         `json:"aroma_notes"`                // 香气备注
        Flavor        float64        `json:"flavor" gorm:"not null"`     // 风味（强度）
        Aftertaste    float64        `json:"aftertaste" gorm:"not null"` // 回甘
        FlavorTags    datatypes.Slot `json:"flavor_tags" gorm:""`        // 风味标签
        FlavorNotes   string         `json:"flavor_notes"`               // 风味备注
        Acidity       float64        `json:"acidity" gorm:"not null"`    // 酸质
        Sweetness     float64        `json:"sweetness" gorm:"not null"`  // 甜度
        Body          float64        `json:"body" gorm:"not null"`       // 口感
        BodyTags      datatypes.Slot `json:"body_tags"`                  // 口感标签
        PrimaryTastes datatypes.Slot `json:"primary_tastes"`             // 主要风味
        // 以下3个字新的cva表里已经没有了
        // Balance    float64 `json:"balance" gorm:"not null"`    // 平衡感
        // Cleancup   float64 `json:"cleancup" gorm:"not null"`   // 干净度
        // Uniformity float64 `json:"uniformity" gorm:"not null"` // 一致性
        Overall float64  `json:"overall" gorm:"not null"`                      // 整体评分
        Defects *Defects `json:"defects" gorm:"column:defects;type:json;null"` // 瑕疵
        Notes   string   `json:"notes"`
}

type Defects []Defect
type Defect struct {
        Name  string `json:"name"`
        Level string `json:"level"`
        Desc  string `json:"desc"`
}

func (d Defects) Total() float64 {
        total := 0
        for _, defect := range d {
                if defect.Level == "quark" {
                        total += 2
                }
                if defect.Level == "full" {
                        total += 4
                }
        }
        return float64(total)
}

func (d *Defects) Scan(value interface{}) error {
        bytes, ok := value.([]byte)
        if !ok {
                log.Printf("Failed to unmarshal Defects value: %v", value)
                return nil

        }
        return json.Unmarshal(bytes, d)
}

func (d Defects) Value() (driver.Value, error) {
        return json.Marshal(d)
}
func (o *CuppingScore) BeforeSave(tx *gorm.DB) (err error) {
        o.Total = o.Aroma + o.Fragrance +
                o.Flavor + o.Acidity + o.Body + o.Aftertaste + o.Sweetness + o.Overall
        if o.Defects != nil {
                o.DefectsTotal = o.Defects.Total()
        }
        return
}

func (o *CuppingScore) AfterFind(tx *gorm.DB) (err error) {
        //改为在保存时更新 2025-05-14
        // o.Total = o.Fragrance + o.Flavor + o.Acidity + o.Body + o.Balance + o.Aftertaste + o.Overall + o.Cleancup + o.Uniformity + o.Sweetness
        // o.DefectsTotal = o.Defects.Total()

        return
}
