        utils.Pager
        UserID    uint          `form:"user_id"`
        CuppingID uint          `form:"cupping_id"`
        SampleID  uint          `form:"sample_id"`
        BeanID    uint          `form:"bean_id"`

// SearchScore searches for cupping scores based on the provided criteria
func SearchScore(q SearchScoreForm) ([]CuppingScore, int) {
        db := database.GetConn()
        var os []CuppingScore
        var statement = db.Model(&CuppingScore{}).Preload("Bean").Preload("Cupping")

        if q.UserID != 0 {
                statement = statement.Where("user_id = ?", q.UserID)
        }
        if q.CuppingID != 0 {
                statement = statement.Where("cupping_id = ?", q.CuppingID)
        }
        if q.SampleID != 0 {
                statement = statement.Where("cuppingsample_id = ?", q.SampleID)
        }
        if q.BeanID != 0 {
                statement = statement.Where("bean_id = ?", q.BeanID)
        }
        if q.Status != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", q.Status)
        } else {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", models.StatusValid)
        }

        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()
        var count int64
        statement.
                Count(&count).
                Order(sort).
                Offset(start).
                Limit(pagesize).
                Find(&os)

        return os, int(count)
}

// 获取样本的得分汇总
func GetScoresBySampleId(sampleId uint) ([]CuppingScore, error) {

        var scores []CuppingScore
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("cuppingsample_id = ?", sampleId).Find(&scores).Error
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return scores, nil
}

// UpdateScore updates or creates a cupping score
func UpdateScore(o *CuppingScore) error {
        db := database.GetConn()
        tx := db.Begin()

        if o.ID != 0 {
                // Update existing score
                if err := tx.Model(&CuppingScore{}).Where("id = ?", o.ID).Updates(map[string]any{
                        "user_id":          o.UserID,
                        "cupping_id":       o.CuppingID,
                        "cuppingsample_id": o.CuppingSampleID,
                        "aroma":            o.Aroma,
                        "aroma_tags":       o.AromaTags,
                        "aroma_notes":      o.AromaNotes,
                        "fragrance":        o.Fragrance,
                        "flavor":           o.Flavor,
                        "flavor_tags":      o.FlavorTags,
                        "flavor_notes":     o.FlavorNotes,
                        "acidity":          o.Acidity,
                        "body":             o.Body,
                        "body_tags":        o.BodyTags,
                        "aftertaste":       o.Aftertaste,
                        "overall":          o.Overall,
                        "sweetness":        o.Sweetness,
                        // "cleancup":         o.Cleancup,
                        // "uniformity":       o.Uniformity,
                        "defects": o.Defects,
                        "notes":   o.Notes,
                        "status":  o.Status,
                }).Error; err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return err
                }
        } else {
                // 创建新记录时，排除已废弃的字段（balance, cleancup, uniformity）
                if err := tx.Omit("balance", "cleancup", "uniformity").Create(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return err
                }
        }
        tx.Commit()
        return nil
}

// DeleteScore deletes a cupping score and updates the sample's average score
func DeleteScore(id uint) error {
        db := database.GetConn()
        tx := db.Begin()

        // Get the score first to know the sample ID
        var score CuppingScore
        if err := tx.First(&score, id).Error; err != nil {
                tx.Rollback()
                return err
        }

        // Delete the score
        if err := tx.Delete(&CuppingScore{}, id).Error; err != nil {
                tx.Rollback()
                return err
        }

        tx.Commit()
        return nil
}

type CuppingScoreSummary struct {
        CuppingScore
        Count int `json:"count"`
        // Total float64 `json:"total"`
        // Defects  int  `json:"defects"` //是否要汇总全部瑕疵（合并全部json）
        DefectsTotal float64 `json:"defects_total"`
}

func GetSampleScoreSummary(sampleID uint) (*CuppingScoreSummary, error) {
        db := database.GetConn()
        var summary CuppingScoreSummary
        rawSQL := getSummaryRawSQL("cuppingsample_id")
        err := db.Raw(rawSQL, sampleID).Scan(&summary).Error
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }

        return &summary, nil
}

func GetBeanScoreSummary(beanID uint) (*CuppingScoreSummary, error) {
        db := database.GetConn()
        var summary CuppingScoreSummary
        rawSQL := getSummaryRawSQL("bean_id")
        err := db.Raw(rawSQL, beanID).Scan(&summary).Error
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &summary, nil
}

func getSummaryRawSQL(groupBy string) string {
        sql := `SELECT 
                        ` + groupBy + `,
                        COUNT(*) as count,
                        ROUND(AVG(aroma), 1) as aroma,
                        ROUND(AVG(fragrance), 1) as fragrance,
                        ROUND(AVG(flavor), 1) as flavor,
                        ROUND(AVG(acidity), 1) as acidity,
                        ROUND(AVG(body), 1) as body,
                        ROUND(AVG(aftertaste), 1) as aftertaste,
                        ROUND(AVG(sweetness), 1) as sweetness,
                        ROUND(AVG(overall), 1) as overall,
                        ROUND(AVG(aroma + fragrance + flavor + acidity + body + 
                                         aftertaste + sweetness + overall), 1) as total,
                        ROUND(AVG(defects_total), 1) as defects_total
                FROM t_cupping_score 
                WHERE ` + groupBy + ` = ? GROUP BY ` + groupBy

        //         SELECT
        //                 cuppingsample_id ,
        //                 COUNT(*) as count,
        //                 ROUND(AVG(aroma), 1) as aroma,
        //                 ROUND(AVG(fragrance), 1) as fragrance,
        //                 ROUND(AVG(flavor), 1) as flavor,
        //                 ROUND(AVG(acidity), 1) as acidity,
        //                 ROUND(AVG(body), 1) as body,
        //                 ROUND(AVG(aftertaste), 1) as aftertaste,
        //                 ROUND(AVG(sweetness), 1) as sweetness,
        //                 ROUND(AVG(overall), 1) as overall,
        //                 ROUND(AVG(aroma + fragrance + flavor + acidity + body +
        //                                  aftertaste + sweetness + overall), 1) as total,
        //                 ROUND(AVG(defects_total), 1) as defects_total
        //         FROM t_cupping_score
        //         WHERE cuppingsample_id = ?
        //         GROUP BY cuppingsample_id
        return sql
}

package cupping

import (
        "hahacking/database/datatypes"
        "hahacking/models/bean"
        "hahacking/models/roast"
)

func NewCupping(b *bean.Bean, roastedBatch *bean.BeanBatch, ro *roast.Roast) (*Cupping, error) {
        cp := Cupping{
                Description: "test cupping",
                Location:    "test cuping location",
                Status:      CuppingStatusValid,
                Method:      CuppingMethodStandard,
                Samples: &[]CuppingSample{
                        {
                                BeanID:          b.ID,
                                BeanBatchID:     roastedBatch.ID,
                                RoastID:         ro.ID,
                                BeanQty:         10,
                                ExtractionYield: "0.5",
                                GrindSize:       "1",
                                WaterTemp:       20,
                                Method:          CuppingMethodStandard,
                        },
                },
        }
        err := Update(&cp)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &cp, nil
}
func NewCuppingSample(cp *Cupping, b *bean.Bean, roastedBatch *bean.BeanBatch, roasto *roast.Roast) (*CuppingSample, error) {
        sample := CuppingSample{
                CuppingID:       cp.ID,
                BeanID:          b.ID,
                BeanBatchID:     roastedBatch.ID,
                RoastID:         roasto.ID,
                BeanQty:         10,
                ExtractionYield: "0.5",
                GrindSize:       "1",
                WaterTemp:       20,
                Method:          CuppingMethodStandard,
        }
        err := UpdateSample(&sample)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &sample, nil
}
func NewCuppingScore(cp *Cupping, sample *CuppingSample, overrideScore *CuppingScore) (*CuppingScore, error) {
        jsonDefects := Defects{
                {
                        Name:  "霉味",
                        Level: "full",
                        Desc:  "霉味",
                },
        }
        var cuppingScore CuppingScore
        if overrideScore != nil {
                cuppingScore = *overrideScore
        } else {
                cuppingScore = CuppingScore{
                        CuppingID:       cp.ID,
                        CuppingSampleID: sample.ID,
                        CuppingScoreItem: CuppingScoreItem{
                                Acidity:       9,
                                Fragrance:     9,
                                FlavorTags:    datatypes.Slot{"test", "flavor", "tag1", "tag2"},
                                FlavorNotes:   "test flavor notes",
                                Body:          9,
                                BodyTags:      datatypes.Slot{"test", "body", "tag1", "tag2"},
                                PrimaryTastes: datatypes.Slot{"test", "primary", "tag1", "tag2"},
                                Aroma:         9,
                                Aftertaste:    9,
                                Sweetness:     9,
                                Overall:       9,
                                Defects:       &jsonDefects,
                                Notes:         "test notes",
                        },
                }
        }
        err := UpdateScore(&cuppingScore)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &cuppingScore, nil
}

package favorite

import (
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/models/category"
        "hahacking/models/item"
        "hahacking/models/report"
        "hahacking/models/user"
        "hahacking/utils"
        "strconv"

        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

/*
收藏内容
*/
type Favorite struct {
        models.Model
        ItemID string        `json:"item_id" gorm:"column:item_id"` // 目标ID
        Type   string        `json:"type"`                          // 类型
        Status models.Status `json:"status"`                        // 状态
        UserID *uint         `json:"user_id" gorm:"not null"`
        User   *user.User    `json:"user" gorm:"foreignKey:UserID;references:ID"`
}

func (o *Favorite) Target() string {
        return "todo"
}

func UserAdd(typ string, itemID string, userID uint) Favorite {
        db := database.Conn
        f := Favorite{
                ItemID: itemID,
                Type:   typ,
                Status: models.StatusValid,
                UserID: &userID,
        }
        db.FirstOrCreate(&f)
        return f
}
func UserRemove(typ string, itemID string, userID uint) error {
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Delete(&Favorite{}, "type = ? AND item_id = ? AND user_id = ?", typ, itemID, userID).Error
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return nil
}

func GetById(id uint) (*Favorite, error) {
        var o Favorite
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("id = ?", id).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}

type SearchForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        ItemID string `form:"item_id"`
        UserID string `form:"user_id"`
        Type   string `form:"type"`
        Status string `form:"status"`
}

func Search(q SearchForm) ([]interface{}, int) {
        db := database.Conn
        var os []Favorite
        var statement = db.Model(&Favorite{}).Preload("User")
        if q.ItemID != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("item_id = ?", q.ItemID)
        }
        if q.UserID != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("user_id = ?", q.UserID)
        }
        if q.Type != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("type = ?", q.Type)
        }
        if q.Status != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", q.Status)
        } else {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", models.StatusValid)
        }
        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()
        var count int64
        statement.
                Count(&count).
                Order(sort).
                Offset(start).
                Limit(pagesize).
                Find(&os)

        objs := make([]interface{}, len(os))
        for i, o := range os {
                switch o.Type {
                case "Report", "report":
                        id, _ := strconv.ParseUint(o.ItemID, 10, 64)
                        objs[i], _ = report.GetById(uint(id))

                case "Category", "category":
                        id, _ := strconv.ParseUint(o.ItemID, 10, 64)
                        objs[i], _ = category.GetById(uint(id))

                case "Item", "item":
                        id, _ := strconv.ParseUint(o.ItemID, 10, 64)
                        objs[i], _ = item.GetById(uint(id))

                default:
                        objs[i] = o
                }
        }
        return objs, int(count)
}

func Update(o *Favorite) error {
        db := database.Conn
        tx := db.Begin()

        if o.ID != 0 {
                if err := tx.Model(&Favorite{}).Where("id = ?", o.ID).Updates(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return err
                }
        } else {
                if err := tx.Create(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return err
                }
        }

        tx.Commit()
        return nil
}

func Delete(id int) error {
        db := database.Conn
        tx := db.Begin()
        if err := tx.Delete(&Favorite{}, id).Error; err != nil {
                tx.Rollback()
                return err
        }

        tx.Commit()
        return nil
}

package item

import (
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/database/datatypes"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/utils"

        "github.com/lib/pq"
        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type Item struct {
        models.Model
        Name         string         `json:"name"`
        Code         string         `json:"code"`
        CategoryPath string         `json:"categorypath" gorm:"column:categorypath"`
        CategoryID   string         `json:"category_id"`
        ParentID     string         `json:"parent_id"` //deprecated
        Price        float64        `json:"price" gorm:"type:decimal(10,2)"`
        Description  string         `json:"description" gorm:"column:description;type:text"`
        Status       ItemStatus     `json:"status"`
        ShopID       string         `json:"shop_id"`
        SKUAtt1      string         `gorm:"type:varchar(200);column:skuatt1" json:"skuatt1"`
        SKUAtt2      string         `gorm:"type:varchar(200);column:skuatt2" json:"skuatt2"`
        SKUAtt3      string         `gorm:"type:varchar(200);column:skuatt3" json:"skuatt3"`
        SKUAttValue1 string         `gorm:"type:varchar(1000);column:skuAttValue1" json:"skuattvalue1"`
        SKUAttValue2 string         `gorm:"type:varchar(1000);column:skuAttValue2" json:"skuattvalue2"`
        SKUAttValue3 string         `gorm:"type:varchar(1000);column:skuAttValue3" json:"skuattvalue3"`
        Pics         pq.StringArray `gorm:"type:text" json:"pics"`
        Tags         pq.StringArray `gorm:"type:text" json:"tags"`
        Sequence     string         `json:"sequence"`
        Stockable    ItemStockable  `json:"stockable"`                                  //是否限制库存
        MenuTags     datatypes.Slot `gorm:"type:text;column:menuTags" json:"menu_tags"` //门店菜单标签

        //以下字段原本在attribute中
        EnName  string `json:"enname" gorm:"column:enname"`
        Country string `json:"country" gorm:"column:country"` //国家iso code

}

type ItemStatus string

const (
        ItemStatusCreate  ItemStatus = "create"
        ItemStatusPublish ItemStatus = "publish"
        ItemStatusPending ItemStatus = "pending"
)

var ItemStatuses = map[ItemStatus]string{
        ItemStatusCreate:  "创建",
        ItemStatusPublish: "上架",
        ItemStatusPending: "下架",
}

type ItemStockable string

const (
        ItemStockableYes ItemStockable = "y"
        ItemStockableNo  ItemStockable = "n"
)

var ItemStockables = map[ItemStockable]string{
        ItemStockableYes: "是",
        ItemStockableNo:  "否",
}

type SearchForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        Name       string `form:"name"`
        Code       string `form:"code"`
        Status     string `form:"status"`
        CategoryID string `form:"category_id"`
        Tags       string `form:"tags"`
        MenuTags   string `form:"menu_tags"`
}

func Search(q SearchForm) ([]Item, int) {
        db := database.GetConn()
        var count int64
        var os []Item
        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()
        var statement = db.Model(&Item{})
        if q.Name != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("name LIKE ?", "%"+q.Name+"%")
        }
        if q.Code != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("code = ?", q.Code)
        }
        if q.CategoryID != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("category_id = ?", q.CategoryID)
        }
        if q.Tags != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("tags LIKE ?", "%"+q.Tags+"%")
        }
        if q.MenuTags != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("menu_tags LIKE ?", "%"+q.MenuTags+"%")
        }
        statement.Where(&q).Count(&count).Order(sort).Offset(start).Limit(pagesize).Find(&os)
        return os, int(count)
}

func Create(o *Item) (*Item, error) {
        db := database.GetConn()
        if err := db.Create(o).Error; err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return o, nil
}

func Update(id int, o *Item) error {
        db := database.GetConn()
        if err := db.Model(&o).Where("id = ?", id).Updates(o).Error; err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return nil
}

func GetById(id uint) (*Item, error) {
        var o Item
        db := database.GetConn()
        err := db.Where("id = ?", id).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}

func (o *Item) Delete() error {
        db := database.GetConn()
        if err := db.Delete(&Item{}, o.ID).Error; err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return nil
}

package item

import (
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/utils"

        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type ItemSKU struct {
        models.Model
        ItemID  uint          `json:"item_id"`
        Item    Item          `json:"item" gorm:"foreignKey:ItemID;references:ID"`
        Price   float64       `json:"price" gorm:"type:decimal(10,2)"`
        SkuAtt1 string        `gorm:"type:varchar(200);column:skuatt1" json:"skuatt1"`
        SkuAtt2 string        `gorm:"type:varchar(200);column:skuatt2" json:"skuatt2"`
        SkuAtt3 string        `gorm:"type:varchar(200);column:skuatt3" json:"skuatt3"`
        Status  models.Status `json:"status"`
}

func GetSKUByAtts(itemID string, att1 string, att2 string, att3 string) (*ItemSKU, error) {
        var o ItemSKU
        db := database.GetConn()
        err := db.Where("item_id = ? and skuatt1 = ? and skuatt2 = ? and skuatt3 = ? and status = ?", itemID, att1, att2, att3, models.StatusValid).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}

func GetSKUByItemID(itemID string) ([]ItemSKU, error) {
        var o []ItemSKU
        db := database.GetConn()
        err := db.Where("item_id = ? and status = ?", itemID, models.StatusValid).Find(&o).Error
        if err != nil {
                return []ItemSKU{}, err
        }
        return o, nil
}

func (o *ItemSKU) GetStocks() ([]ItemStock, error) {
        stocks, err := GetStockByItemSKUID(o.ID)
        if err != nil {
                return []ItemStock{}, err
        }
        return stocks, nil
}

func (o *ItemSKU) InitStock(qty int, unit string) (*[]ItemStock, error) {
        stocks, err := o.GetStocks()
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        if len(stocks) == 0 {
                stock := ItemStock{
                        ItemID:    o.ItemID,
                        ItemSKUID: o.ID,
                        Qty:       qty,
                        Unit:      unit,
                        Status:    models.StatusValid,
                }
                newstock, err := CreateStock(&stock)
                if err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
                stocks = append(stocks, *newstock)
        } else {
                for _, stock := range stocks {
                        stock.SetQty(qty)
                }
        }

        return &stocks, nil
}

type SearchSKUForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        ItemID  string        `form:"item_id"`
        Status  models.Status `form:"status"`
        SkuAtt1 string        `form:"skuatt1"`
        SkuAtt2 string        `form:"skuatt2"`
        SkuAtt3 string        `form:"skuatt3"`
        //模式2: 批量查询
        IDs     []string `form:"ids"`
        ItemIDs []string `form:"item_ids"`
}

func SearchSKU(q SearchSKUForm) ([]ItemSKU, int) {
        db := database.GetConn()
        var count int64
        var os []ItemSKU
        var statement = db.Model(&ItemSKU{})
        if q.ItemID != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("item_id = ?", q.ItemID)
        }
        if q.ItemIDs != nil {
                statement = statement.Where("item_id IN (?)", q.ItemIDs)
        }
        if q.IDs != nil {
                statement = statement.Where("id IN (?)", q.IDs)
        }
        if q.Status != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", q.Status)
        }
        if q.SkuAtt1 != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("skuatt1 = ?", q.SkuAtt1)
        }
        if q.SkuAtt2 != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("skuatt2 = ?", q.SkuAtt2)
        }
        if q.SkuAtt3 != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("skuatt3 = ?", q.SkuAtt3)
        }
        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()
        statement.Where(&q).Count(&count).Order(sort).Offset(start).Limit(pagesize).Find(&os)
        return os, int(count)
}

func CreateSKU(o *ItemSKU) (*ItemSKU, error) {
        db := database.GetConn()
        if err := db.Create(o).Error; err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return o, nil
}

func UpdateSKU(id int, o *ItemSKU) error {
        db := database.GetConn()
        if err := db.Model(&o).Where("id = ?", id).Updates(o).Error; err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return nil
}

func GetSKUById(id uint) (*ItemSKU, error) {
        var o ItemSKU
        db := database.GetConn()
        err := db.Where("id = ?", id).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}

func (o *ItemSKU) Delete() error {
        db := database.GetConn()
        if err := db.Delete(&ItemSKU{}, o.ID).Error; err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return nil
}

package item

import (
        "errors"
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/utils"

        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type ItemStock struct {
        models.Model
        ItemID    uint          `json:"item_id"`
        Item      Item          `json:"item" gorm:"foreignKey:ItemID;references:ID"`
        ItemSKUID uint          `json:"item_sku_id" gorm:"column:itemsku_id"`
        ItemSKU   ItemSKU       `json:"item_sku" gorm:"foreignKey:ItemSKUID;references:ID"`
        Qty       int           `json:"qty"`
        Unit      string        `json:"unit" gorm:"column:unit"`
        Status    models.Status `json:"status"`
}

func GetStockByItemID(itemID string) ([]ItemStock, error) {
        var o []ItemStock
        db := database.GetConn()
        err := db.Where("item_id = ? and status = ?", itemID, models.StatusValid).Find(&o).Error
        if err != nil {
                return []ItemStock{}, err
        }
        return o, nil
}

func GetStockByItemSKUID(itemSKUID uint) ([]ItemStock, error) {
        var o []ItemStock
        db := database.GetConn()
        err := db.Where("itemSKU_id = ? and status = ?", itemSKUID, models.StatusValid).Find(&o).Error
        if err != nil {
                return []ItemStock{}, err
        }
        return o, nil
}

type SearchStockForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        ItemID    string        `form:"item_id"`
        Status    models.Status `form:"status"`
        ItemSKUID string        `form:"itemSKU_id"`
}

func SearchStock(q SearchStockForm) ([]ItemStock, int) {
        db := database.GetConn()
        var count int64
        var os []ItemStock
        var statement = db.Model(&ItemStock{})
        if q.ItemID != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("item_id = ?", q.ItemID)
        }

        if q.ItemSKUID != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("itemSKU_id = ?", q.ItemSKUID)
        }
        if q.Status != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", q.Status)
        }

        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()
        statement.Where(&q).Count(&count).Order(sort).Offset(start).Limit(pagesize).Find(&os)
        return os, int(count)
}

func CreateStock(o *ItemStock) (*ItemStock, error) {
        db := database.GetConn()
        if err := db.Create(o).Error; err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return o, nil
}

func UpdateStock(id int, o *ItemStock) error {
        db := database.GetConn()
        if err := db.Model(&o).Where("id = ?", id).Updates(o).Error; err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return nil
}

func GetStockById(id uint) (*ItemStock, error) {
        var o ItemStock
        db := database.GetConn()
        err := db.Where("id = ?", id).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}

func (o *ItemStock) Delta(n int) int {
        db := database.GetConn()
        tx := db.Begin()
        err := tx.Model(&o).Where("id = ?", o.ID).Update("qty", o.Qty-n).Error
        if err != nil {
                tx.Rollback()
                return n
        }
        err = tx.Commit().Error
        if err != nil {
                tx.Rollback()
                return n
        }
        o.Qty = o.Qty - n
        return o.Qty
}
func (o *ItemStock) Incr(n int) (int, error) {
        if n < 0 {
                return 0, errors.New("n must be greater than 0")
        }
        return o.Delta(n), nil
}
func (o *ItemStock) Decr(n int) (int, error) {
        if n > 0 {
                return 0, errors.New("n must be greater than 0")
        }
        return o.Delta(n), nil
}
func (o *ItemStock) SetQty(n int) int {
        db := database.GetConn()
        tx := db.Begin()
        err := tx.Model(&o).Where("id = ?", o.ID).Update("qty", n).Error
        if err != nil {
                tx.Rollback()
                return n
        }
        err = tx.Commit().Error
        if err != nil {
                tx.Rollback()
                return n
        }
        o.Qty = n
        return o.Qty
}

func (o *ItemStock) Delete() error {
        db := database.GetConn()
        if err := db.Delete(&ItemStock{}, o.ID).Error; err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return nil
}

package models

import (
        "time"

        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type Status string

const (
        StatusValid   Status = "valid"
        StatusInvalid Status = "invalid"
        StatusDeleted Status = "deleted"
)

var CommonStatuses = map[Status]string{
        StatusValid:   "有效",
        StatusInvalid: "无效",
        StatusDeleted: "删除",
}

var Conn *gorm.DB

// Entity 表示任何包含基础Model字段的实体
type Entity interface {
        *Model | *LegacyModel
}

// Repository 通用CRUD仓储接口
type Repository[T Entity] interface {
        GetById(id uint) (T, error)
        Create(entity T) error
        Update(entity T) error
        Delete(id uint) error
        Search(query interface{}) ([]T, int)
}

// BaseRepository 基础实现
type BaseRepository[T Entity] struct {
        db *gorm.DB
}

func NewBaseRepository[T Entity](db *gorm.DB) *BaseRepository[T] {
        return &BaseRepository[T]{db: db}
}

func (r *BaseRepository[T]) GetById(id uint) (T, error) {
        var entity T
        err := r.db.First(&entity, id).Error
        return entity, err
}

type Model struct {
        // gorm.Model
        ID uint `gorm:"primaryKey;autoIncrement" json:"id" form:"id"`
        // ID database.UUID `gorm:"primary_key;;default:(UUID_TO_BIN(UUID()));"`ref：https://medium.com/wesionary-team/implementation-of-uuid-and-binary-16-in-gorm-v2-1c329c352c91

        CreatedAt time.Time      `gorm:"column:created_at" json:"created_at"`
        UpdatedAt time.Time      `gorm:"column:updated_at" json:"updated_at"`
        DeletedAt gorm.DeletedAt `gorm:"column:deleted_at" json:"-"`
        // Attribute string `gorm:"column:attribute;type:text" json:"attribute"`
}

func ConvertMapToArray[K ~string](m map[K]string) []any {
        var result []any
        for k, v := range m {
                result = append(result, map[string]string{"title": v, "value": string(k)})
        }
        return result
}
