type LegacyModel struct {
        ID           int `gorm:"primaryKey" json:"id"`
        CreatedTime  int `gorm:"column:createdTime;type:int;size:32" json:"createdtime"`
        ModifiedTime int `gorm:"column:modifiedTime;type:int;size:32" json:"modifiedtime"`
}

package order

import (
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/models/bean"
        "hahacking/models/shop"
        "hahacking/utils"
        "log"
        "strconv"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "github.com/mitchellh/mapstructure"
        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type Order struct {
        models.Model
        UserID         *uint       `json:"user_id"`
        SN             string      `json:"sn" gorm:"column:sn;unique"`
        Date           string      `json:"date"`
        TotalAmt       float64     `json:"totalamt" gorm:"type:decimal(10,2);column:totalamt"` // 总金额
        PayAmt         float64     `json:"payamt" gorm:"type:decimal(10,2);column:payamt"`     // 实付金额 - 或者是计入收入账户 - 暂时不用也可以 2025-4-9
        TotalQty       int         `json:"totalqty" gorm:"column:totalqty"`                    // 总数量
        Memo           string      `json:"memo"`
        Pics           string      `json:"pics"`
        Status         OrderStatus `json:"status" `
        OrderItems     []OrderItem `json:"items" gorm:"foreignKey:OrderID"`                            // Add foreignKey tag to establish the relationship
        OrderPaymentID uint        `json:"orderpayment_id" gorm:"column:orderpayment_id"`              // 关联的支付单ID
        Refunds        []Refund    `json:"refunds" gorm:"foreignKey:OrderID"`                          // 退款
        RefundAmount   float64     `json:"refundamount" gorm:"type:decimal(10,2);column:refundamount"` // 退款金额
}
type OrderStatus string

const (
        OrderStatusCreated      OrderStatus = "created"     // 已创建
        OrderStatusPlaced       OrderStatus = "placed"      // 已下单
        OrderStatusSettled      OrderStatus = "settled"     // 已结算
        OrderStatusInvalid      OrderStatus = "invalid"     // 无效
        OrderStatusSetttledUndo OrderStatus = "settledundo" //已结算撤销
)

var OrderStatuses = map[OrderStatus]string{
        OrderStatusCreated:      "已创建",
        OrderStatusPlaced:       "已下单",
        OrderStatusSettled:      "已结算",
        OrderStatusInvalid:      "无效",
        OrderStatusSetttledUndo: "结算撤销",
}

func Meta(name string) map[string]any {
        names := strings.Split(name, ",")
        result := make(map[string]any)
        for _, n := range names {
                if n == "OrderStatus" {
                        result[n] = models.ConvertMapToArray(OrderStatuses)
                }
        }
        return result
}

func GetById(id uint) (*Order, error) {
        var o Order
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("id = ?", id).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}

func GetBySn(sn string) (*Order, error) {
        var o Order
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("sn = ?", sn).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}

type SearchForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        UserID   uint    `form:"user_id"`
        SN       string  `form:"sn"`
        Date     string  `form:"date"`
        TotalAmt float64 `form:"totalamt"`
        TotalQty int     `form:"totalqty"`
        Status   string  `form:"status"`
}

func Search(q SearchForm) ([]Order, int) {
        db := database.Conn
        var os []Order
        var statement = db.Model(&Order{})
        if q.Status != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", q.Status)
        }
        if q.SN != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("sn = ?", q.SN)
        }
        if q.UserID != 0 {
                statement = statement.Where("user_id = ?", q.UserID)
        }
        if q.Date != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("date like ?", "%"+q.Date+"%")
        }
        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()
        var count int64
        statement.Where(&Order{}).Preload("OrderItems").
                Count(&count).
                Order(sort).
                Offset(start).
                Limit(pagesize).
                Find(&os)
        return os, int(count)
}

type CreateForm struct {
        Date     string      `json:"date"`
        TotalAmt float64     `json:"totalamt"`
        TotalQty int         `json:"totalqty"`
        Status   string      `json:"status"`
        Items    []OrderItem `json:"items"`
        Memo     string      `json:"memo"`
}

func (form *CreateForm) Validate() map[string]string {
        var result = make(map[string]string)
        if len(form.Items) == 0 {
                result["items"] = "订单明细不能为空"
        }
        if form.Date == "" {
                result["date"] = "日期不能为空"
        }
        if form.TotalAmt <= 0 {
                result["totalamt"] = "总金额不能为空"
        }
        if form.TotalQty <= 0 {
                result["totalqty"] = "总数量不能为空"
        }
        return nil
}

type UpdateForm struct {
        ID       uint        `json:"id" form:"id"`
        Date     string      `json:"date"`
        TotalAmt float64     `json:"totalamt"`
        TotalQty int         `json:"totalqty"`
        Status   string      `json:"status"`
        Items    []OrderItem `json:"items"`
        Memo     string      `json:"memo"`
}

func (form *UpdateForm) Validate() map[string]string {
        var result = make(map[string]string)
        if len(form.Items) == 0 {
                result["items"] = "订单明细不能为空"
        }
        if form.Date == "" {
                result["date"] = "日期不能为空"
        }
        if form.TotalAmt <= 0 {
                result["totalamt"] = "总金额不能为空"
        }
        if form.TotalQty <= 0 {
                result["totalqty"] = "总数量不能为空"
        }
        return nil
}

func Update(f *UpdateForm) (*Order, error) {
        db := database.Conn
        var o Order
        if err := mapstructure.Decode(f, &o); err != nil {
                log.Println("Decode order err", err)
                return nil, err
        }
        if f.ID != 0 { //修改
                if err := db.Model(&Order{}).Where("id = ?", f.ID).Updates(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        log.Println("Update order err", err)
                        return nil, err
                }
                var existingItems []OrderItem
                if err := db.Where("order_id = ?", f.ID).Find(&existingItems).Error; err != nil {
                        log.Println("查询订单明细失败", err)
                        return nil, err
                }
                // Delete items that are not in the new list
                var itemsToDelete []OrderItem
                for _, existingItem := range existingItems {
                        found := false
                        for _, item := range f.Items {
                                if existingItem.ID == item.ID {
                                        found = true
                                        break
                                }
                        }
                        if !found {
                                itemsToDelete = append(itemsToDelete, existingItem)
                        }
                }
                tx := db.Begin()
                for _, item := range itemsToDelete {
                        if err := tx.Where("id = ?", item.ID).Delete(&OrderItem{}).Error; err != nil {
                                log.Println("Delete order item err", err)
                                tx.Rollback()
                                return nil, err
                        }
                }
                for _, item := range f.Items {
                        if item.ID != 0 {
                                if err := tx.Model(&OrderItem{}).Where("id = ?", item.ID).Updates(&item).Error; err != nil {
                                        log.Println("Update order item err", err)
                                        tx.Rollback()
                                        return nil, err
                                }
                        } else {
                                var oitem OrderItem
                                if err := mapstructure.Decode(item, &oitem); err != nil {
                                        log.Println("Decode order item err", err)
                                        tx.Rollback()
                                        return nil, err
                                }
                                oitem.OrderID = f.ID
                                if err := tx.Create(&oitem).Error; err != nil {
                                        log.Println("Create order item err", err)
                                        tx.Rollback()
                                        return nil, err
                                }
                        }
                }
                tx.Commit()
        } else {
                tx := db.Begin()
                o.Status = "created"
                if err := tx.Create(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return nil, err
                }
                for _, item := range f.Items {
                        item.OrderID = o.ID
                        if err := tx.Create(&item).Error; err != nil {
                                tx.Rollback()
                                return nil, err
                        }
                }
                tx.Commit()
        }
        return &o, nil
}
func (o *Order) AfterCreate(tx *gorm.DB) (err error) {
        // 生成流水号：前缀 + 时间戳 + 自增ID
        prefix := ""
        timestamp := time.Now().Format("20060102")
        serialNumber := fmt.Sprintf("%s%s%06d", prefix, timestamp, o.ID)
        if err := tx.Model(&o).Update("SN", serialNumber).Error; err != nil {
                tx.Rollback()
                return err
        }
        return
}

type PlaceForm struct {
        UserID       uint               `json:"user_id"`
        OrderID      uint               `json:"order_id"`
        PaymentItems []OrderPaymentItem `json:"payitems,omitempty"`
}

func (form *PlaceForm) Validate() map[string]string {
        if len(form.PaymentItems) == 0 {
                return map[string]string{"payitems": "结算单明细不能为空"}
        }
        if form.OrderID == 0 {
                return map[string]string{"order_id": "订单ID不能为空"}
        }
        return nil
}

/**
* 订单支付项确认 - 计算实付金额
* 如果支付项校验通过：则创建并关联Order和OrderPayment，之后让用户确认后Settle
* 如果校验未通过：则给用户提示
 */
func (order *Order) Place(form PlaceForm) (*OrderPayment, error) {
        db := database.Conn
        var err error
        //保存payment并计算剩余应付金额
        var totalPayAmt float64
        orderPayment := OrderPayment{
                OrderID: form.OrderID,
        }
        if err := db.Create(&orderPayment).Error; err != nil {
                // tx.Rollback()
                return nil, err
        }
        for _, paymentItem := range form.PaymentItems {
                paymentItem.OrderPaymentID = orderPayment.ID
                if err = db.Create(&paymentItem).Error; err != nil {
                        // tx.Rollback()
                        return nil, err
                }
                totalPayAmt += paymentItem.Amt
        }
        order.PayAmt = order.TotalAmt - totalPayAmt
        order.Status = "placed"
        order.OrderPaymentID = orderPayment.ID
        if err := db.Save(&order).Error; err != nil {
                // tx.Rollback()
                return nil, err
        }
        // tx.Commit()
        // Attach payment items to the payment object
        var paymentItems []OrderPaymentItem
        if err := db.Where("orderpayment_id = ?", orderPayment.ID).Find(&paymentItems).Error; err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        orderPayment.Items = paymentItems
        return &orderPayment, nil
}

type SettleForm struct {
        UserID  uint `json:"user_id,omitempty"`
        OrderID uint `json:"order_id,omitempty"`
        //模式1:支付单ID - 和支付单明细同时存在时，优先选择订单支付单ID
        OrderPaymentID uint `json:"orderpayment_id,omitempty"`
        //模式2:支付单明细（按需创建支付单）
        PaymentItems []OrderPaymentItem `json:"payitems,omitempty"`
}

func (form *SettleForm) Validate() map[string]string {
        if form.OrderID == 0 {
                return map[string]string{"order_id": "订单ID不能为空"}
        }
        if form.OrderPaymentID == 0 {
                if len(form.PaymentItems) == 0 {
                        return map[string]string{"payitems": "结算单明细不能为空"}
                }
        }
        return nil
}

/*
*  结算订单
* case1: 传入OrderPaymentID，根据paymentID结算 （需要校验支付单是否还有效可用过期）
* case2: 传入payitems数组，此时需要重新调用place进行校验（可能通过可能不通过）
 */
func (order *Order) Settle(form SettleForm) (*Order, error) {
        db := database.GetConn()
        log.Println("开始结算订单", order.ID)
        log.Printf("结算单 %v", form)
        // tx := db.Begin()
        order.Status = OrderStatusSettled
        if err := db.Save(&order).Error; err != nil {
                // tx.Rollback()
                return nil, err
        }
        var payment *OrderPayment
        var err error
        //校验OrderPayment状态、合并的金额是否正确
        if form.OrderPaymentID == 0 { //如果没有paymentid，则检查payitems是否正确
                payment, err = order.Place(PlaceForm{
                        UserID:       form.UserID,
                        OrderID:      form.OrderID,
                        PaymentItems: form.PaymentItems,
                })
                if err != nil {
                        log.Println("支付单校验失败", err)
                        return nil, err
                }
        } else {
                payment, err = GetPaymentById(form.OrderPaymentID)
                if err != nil {
                        log.Println("获取支付单失败", err)
                        return nil, err
                }
        }

        payment.Status = models.StatusValid
        db.Save(payment)
        log.Printf("支付单 %v", payment)
        //修改payment的状态和items的状态
        pitems := payment.Items
        log.Printf("支付单明细 %v", pitems)
        for _, item := range pitems {
                // item.Status = models.StatusValid
                // if err := db.Model(&item).Updates(map[string]any{"status": item.Status}).Error; err != nil {
                //         log.Println("更新支付单明细状态失败", err)
                //         // tx.Rollback()
                //         return nil, err
                // }
                switch item.Type {
                case OrderPaymentTypeShopAccount: //账户结算（包括现金）
                        log.Println("账户结算", item.Ids, item.Amt)
                        accid, _ := strconv.Atoi(item.Ids)
                        acc, _ := shop.GetAccountById(accid)
                        acc.Balancing(-item.Amt, "OrderPayment", order.SN+"|"+strconv.Itoa(int(payment.ID)))
                case OrderPaymentTypeCash: //现金结算 - 暂时兼容，后续现金以账户处理
                        // accid, _ := strconv.Atoi(item.Ids)
                        // acc, _ := shop.GetAccountById(accid)
                        // acc.Balancing(-item.Amt, "OrderPayment", order.SN+"|"+strconv.Itoa(int(payment.ID)))
                case OrderPaymentTypeAlipay:
                        //TODO: 支付宝结算
                case OrderPaymentTypeWeChat:
                        //TODO: 微信结算
                case OrderPaymentTypeBank:
                        //TODO: 银行结算
                case OrderPaymentTypeCredit:
                        //TODO: 积分结算
                case OrderPaymentTypeGiftCard:
                        //TODO: 礼品卡结算
                case OrderPaymentTypeOther:
                        //TODO: 其他结算
                }

        }
        order.Status = OrderStatusSettled
        if err := db.Save(&order).Error; err != nil {
                // tx.Rollback()
                return nil, err
        }
        //熟豆库存更新（批次剩余量、stocklog，bean.roastqty）
        err = order.OnSettled()
        if err != nil {
                log.Println("结算后库存更新失败", err)
                return nil, err
        }

        return order, nil
}
func (order *Order) OnSettled() error {
        items := order.Items()
        var err error
        for _, item := range items {
                //批次日志需要一个一个写入，库存日志可以将相同的豆子（豆子id、生/熟）数量合并
                b, _ := bean.GetById(item.BeanID)
                _, err = b.Stocking(-item.Qty, bean.BeanTypeRoasted, bean.StockLogTypeSale, "order", order.SN+"|"+strconv.FormatUint(uint64(item.ID), 10),
                        "订单"+order.SN+"|"+strconv.FormatUint(uint64(item.ID), 10))
                if err != nil {
                        errmsg := "库存更新失败 -" + err.Error() + " 订单" + order.SN + " 豆子" + b.Name + " 数量" + strconv.Itoa(item.Qty) +
                                "refid:" + order.SN + "|" + strconv.FormatUint(uint64(item.ID), 10)
                        log.Println(errmsg)
                        err = errors.New(errmsg)
                        return err
                }
                batch, _ := bean.GetBatchById(item.BeanBatchID)
                // batch.CurrentQty -= item.Qty 移入log方法
                // database.Conn.Save(batch)
                err = batch.Log(-item.Qty, "order", order.SN+"|"+strconv.FormatUint(uint64(item.ID), 10),
                        "订单结算"+order.SN+"|"+strconv.FormatUint(uint64(item.ID), 10))
                if err != nil {
                        errmsg := "批次库存更新失败 - " + err.Error() + " 订单" + order.SN + " 批次" + strconv.Itoa(int(batch.ID)) + " 数量" + strconv.Itoa(item.Qty) +
                                "refid:" + order.SN + "|" + strconv.FormatUint(uint64(item.ID), 10)
                        log.Println(errmsg)
                        err = errors.New(errmsg)
                        return err
                }
        }
        return nil
}
func (order *Order) Items() []OrderItem {
        db := database.Conn
        var items []OrderItem
        db.Where("order_id = ?", order.ID).Find(&items)
        return items
}
func (order *Order) Payment() OrderPayment {
        db := database.Conn
        var payment OrderPayment
        db.Where("order_id = ? and status = ?", order.ID, models.StatusValid).Preload("Items").Last(&payment)
        return payment
}

func (order *Order) SettleUndo() error {
        db := database.Conn
        tx := db.Begin()

        if err := tx.Model(&order).Update("status", OrderStatusSetttledUndo).Error; err != nil {
                return err
        }
        //1. 撤销支付单 状态, 撤销账户记录
        payment, err := GetPaymentById(order.OrderPaymentID)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        payment.Status = models.StatusInvalid
        tx.Save(payment)
        //恢复结算（如结算账户扣款、退款、券等）

        pitems, _ := payment.GetItems()
        // fmt.Println("pitemsxxxxxxxxxxxx", pitems)
        for _, item := range pitems {
                if err := tx.Model(&item).Updates(map[string]any{"status": OrderStatusInvalid}).Error; err != nil {
                        log.Println("更新支付单明细状态失败", err)
                        // tx.Rollback()
                        return err
                }
                // fmt.Println("itemxxxxxxxxxxxx", item)
                if item.Type == OrderPaymentTypeShopAccount { //账户结算（包括现金）
                        accid, _ := strconv.Atoi(item.Ids)
                        acc, _ := shop.GetAccountById(accid)
                        acc.Balancing(item.Amt, "OrderPayment", strconv.Itoa(int(payment.ID)))
                }
                //如果是用券， 需要记录log Later
        }

        //恢复库存
        items := order.Items()
        for _, item := range items {
                b, _ := bean.GetById(item.BeanID)
                b.Stocking(item.Qty, bean.BeanTypeRoasted, bean.StockLogTypeUndo, "order", order.SN, "订单取消"+order.SN)
                batch, _ := bean.GetBatchById(item.BeanBatchID)
                // batch.CurrentQty += item.Qty 移入log
                // database.Conn.Save(batch)
                batch.Log(item.Qty, "order", strconv.FormatUint(uint64(order.ID), 10), "订单取消"+order.SN+"|"+strconv.FormatUint(uint64(item.ID), 10))
        }

        tx.Commit()

        return nil
}
func (order *Order) Remove() error {
        db := database.Conn
        if err := db.Model(&order).Update("status", OrderStatusInvalid).Error; err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return nil
}

// 获取订单的折扣金额(子项中每个bean都采用对应的等级价格的和)
func (order Order) GetPriceLevelDiscount(levelid uint) (float64, error) {
        db := database.Conn
        var orderitems []OrderItem
        err := db.Where("order_id = ?", order.ID).Find(&orderitems).Error
        if err != nil {
                return 0, err
        }
        var beanids []uint
        for _, item := range orderitems {
                beanids = append(beanids, item.BeanID)
        }
        prices, _ := bean.GetPrices(beanids, levelid)
        var totaldiscount float64
        for _, item := range orderitems {
                beanlevelprice := prices[item.BeanID]
                bean, _ := bean.GetById(item.BeanID)
                //一律以零售基础价为折扣基础
                totaldiscount += (bean.CPrice - beanlevelprice) * float64(item.Qty) / 1000

        }
        return totaldiscount, nil
}

type RefundForm struct {
        OrderID  uint         `json:"order_id"`
        Date     string       `json:"date"`
        TotalQty int          `json:"totalqty"`
        TotalAmt float64      `json:"totalamt"`
        Items    []RefundItem `json:"items"`
        Amts     []RefundAmt  `json:"amts"`
}

func (refundform *RefundForm) Validate() map[string]string {
        if refundform.OrderID == 0 {
                return map[string]string{"order_id": "订单ID不能为空"}
        }
        if refundform.Date == "" {
                return map[string]string{"date": "日期不能为空"}
        }
        if refundform.TotalQty <= 0 && refundform.TotalAmt <= 0 {
                return map[string]string{"totalqty": "总数量和总金额不能同时为空"}
        }
        if len(refundform.Items) == 0 && len(refundform.Amts) == 0 {
                return map[string]string{"items": "退货或者退款项目不能同时为空"}
        }
        for _, item := range refundform.Items {
                if item.OrderItemID == 0 {
                        return map[string]string{"items": "订单项目ID不能为空"}
                }
        }
        err := RefundRuleCheck(*refundform)
        if err != nil {
                return map[string]string{"items": err.Error()}
        }
        return nil
}

func (order *Order) Refund(form RefundForm) (*Refund, error) {
        db := database.Conn
        //先检查够不够退 -- 移入form.Validate()
        err := RefundRuleCheck(form)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        refund := Refund{
                OrderID:  form.OrderID,
                Date:     form.Date,
                TotalQty: form.TotalQty,
                TotalAmt: form.TotalAmt,
                Status:   models.StatusValid,
        }
        tx := db.Begin()
        if err := tx.Create(&refund).Error; err != nil {
                tx.Rollback()
                return nil, err
        }

        for _, item := range form.Items {
                if err := tx.Create(&RefundItem{
                        RefundID:    refund.ID,
                        OrderItemID: item.OrderItemID,
                        Qty:         item.Qty, //克
                        // Price:       item.Price, //价格是公斤价格
                        // Amt:         float64(item.Qty) * item.Price / 1000,
                }).Error; err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return nil, err
                }
        }
        for _, amt := range form.Amts {
                if err := tx.Create(&RefundAmt{
                        RefundID:           refund.ID,
                        OrderPaymentItemID: amt.OrderPaymentItemID,
                        Amt:                amt.Amt,
                }).Error; err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return nil, err
                }
        }

        tx.Commit()
        return &refund, nil
}

func (order *Order) GetRefunds() []Refund {
        db := database.Conn
        var refunds []Refund
        db.Where("order_id = ?", order.ID).Preload("Items").Preload("Amts").Find(&refunds)
        return refunds
}

package order

import (
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/models/bean"

        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type OrderItem struct {
        models.Model
        OrderID     uint            `json:"order_id"`
        BeanID      uint            `json:"bean_id"`
        Bean        *bean.Bean      `json:"bean" gorm:"foreignKey:BeanID;references:ID"`
        BeanName    string          `json:"bean_name"`
        BeanBatchID uint            `json:"beanbatch_id" gorm:"null"`
        BeanBatch   *bean.BeanBatch `json:"beanbatch" gorm:"foreignKey:BeanBatchID;references:ID"`
        Qty         int             `json:"qty"`
        Price       float64         `json:"price" gorm:"type:decimal(10,2)"`
        Amt         float64         `json:"amt" gorm:"type:decimal(10,2)"`
}

func GetItemById(id uint) (*OrderItem, error) {
        var o OrderItem
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("id = ?", id).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}

package order

import (
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/utils"

        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type OrderPayment struct {
        models.Model
        OrderID uint               `json:"order_id"`
        Status  models.Status      `json:"status" `
        Items   []OrderPaymentItem `json:"items" gorm:"foreignKey:OrderPaymentID;references:ID"`
}
type OrderPaymentItem struct {
        ID uint `json:"id"`
        models.Model
        OrderID        uint             `json:"order_id"`
        OrderPaymentID uint             `json:"orderpayment_id" gorm:"column:orderpayment_id"`
        Type           OrderPaymentType `json:"type"`
        Amt            float64          `json:"amt"`
        Qty            float64          `json:"qty"`
        Memo           string           `json:"memo"`
        Ids            string           `json:"ids"`
        Status         models.Status    `json:"status" `
}

type OrderPaymentType string

const (
        OrderPaymentTypeCash        OrderPaymentType = "cash"        // 现金
        OrderPaymentTypeAlipay      OrderPaymentType = "alipay"      // 支付宝
        OrderPaymentTypeWeChat      OrderPaymentType = "wechat"      // 微信
        OrderPaymentTypeBank        OrderPaymentType = "bank"        // 银行转账
        OrderPaymentTypeShopAccount OrderPaymentType = "shopaccount" // 挂账
        OrderPaymentTypeCredit      OrderPaymentType = "credit"      // 积分
        OrderPaymentTypeGiftCard    OrderPaymentType = "giftcard"
        OrderPaymentTypeOther       OrderPaymentType = "other" // 其他
)

func GetPaymentById(id uint) (*OrderPayment, error) {
        var o OrderPayment
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("id = ?", id).Preload("Items").First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}

type SearchPaymentForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        OrderID uint          `form:"order_id"`
        Status  models.Status `form:"status"`
}

func SearchPayment(q SearchPaymentForm) ([]OrderPayment, int) {
        db := database.Conn
        var os []OrderPayment
        var count int64
        var statement = db.Model(&OrderPayment{})
        if q.OrderID != 0 {
                statement = statement.Where("order_id = ?", q.OrderID)
        }
        if q.Status != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", q.Status)
        }
        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()
        statement.Where(&OrderPayment{}).Preload("Items").
                Count(&count).
                Order(sort).
                Offset(start).
                Limit(pagesize).
                Find(&os)
        return os, int(count)
}

func (p *OrderPayment) GetItems() ([]OrderPaymentItem, error) {
        var items []OrderPaymentItem
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("orderpayment_id = ?", p.ID).Find(&items).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return items, nil
}

func GetPaymentItemById(id uint) (*OrderPaymentItem, error) {
        var item OrderPaymentItem
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("id = ?", id).First(&item).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &item, nil
}

package order

import (
        "errors"
        "fmt"
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/models/bean"
        "hahacking/models/shop"
        "hahacking/utils"
        "log"
        "strconv"
        "time"

        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type Refund struct {
        models.Model
        SN       string        `json:"sn" gorm:"column:sn;unique"` //退款流水号
        Date     string        `json:"date"`                       //退款日期
        OrderID  uint          `json:"order_id"`
        Order    *Order        `json:"order" gorm:"foreignKey:OrderID;references:ID"`
        Status   models.Status `json:"status"`
        TotalAmt float64       `json:"totalprice" gorm:"column:totalamt;type:decimal(10,2)"`
        TotalQty int           `json:"totalqty" gorm:"column:totalqty;default:0;not null;type:int"`
        Items    []RefundItem  `json:"items" gorm:"foreignKey:RefundID"`
        Amts     []RefundAmt   `json:"amts" gorm:"foreignKey:RefundID"`
}

// 记录退款商品（豆子）
type RefundItem struct {
        models.Model
        RefundID    uint    `json:"refund_id" gorm:"column:refund_id"`
        Refund      *Refund `json:"refund" gorm:"foreignKey:RefundID;references:ID"`
        OrderItemID uint    `json:"order_item_id" gorm:"column:order_item_id"`
        // OrderItem   *OrderItem      `json:"order_item" gorm:"foreignKey:OrderItemID;references:ID"`
        //这些豆子信息似乎没用了
        // BeanID      uint            `json:"bean_id" gorm:"column:bean_id"`
        // BeanName    string          `json:"bean_name"`
        // BeanBatchID uint            `json:"beanbatch_id" gorm:"column:beanbatch_id"`
        // BeanBatch   *bean.BeanBatch `json:"beanbatch" gorm:"foreignKey:BeanBatchID;references:ID"`
        // Bean        *bean.Bean      `json:"bean" gorm:"foreignKey:BeanID;references:ID"`
        // BeanType    bean.BeanType   `json:"bean_type" gorm:"column:bean_type"`
        Qty int `json:"qty" gorm:"column:qty"`
        // Price       float64         `json:"price" gorm:"column:price;type:decimal(10,2)"`
        // Amt         float64         `json:"amt" gorm:"column:amt;type:decimal(10,2)"`
        Status models.Status `json:"status" gorm:"column:status"`
}

// 记录退款金额和渠道来源
type RefundAmt struct {
        models.Model
        RefundID uint    `json:"refund_id" gorm:"column:refund_id"`
        Refund   *Refund `json:"refund" gorm:"foreignKey:RefundID;references:ID"`
        //支付项ID
        OrderPaymentItemID uint          `json:"order_payment_item_id"`
        Amt                float64       `json:"amt"`
        Status             models.Status `json:"status" gorm:"column:status"`
}

func (refund *Refund) BeforeCreate(tx *gorm.DB) (err error) {
        // 生成流水号：前缀 + 时间戳 + 纳秒级时间戳
        prefix := "RF"
        timestamp := time.Now().Format("20060102")
        nanos := time.Now().UnixNano() % 1000000 // 取纳秒的后6位
        serialNumber := fmt.Sprintf("%s%s%06d", prefix, timestamp, nanos)

        // 如果SN已存在，重试生成
        for i := 0; i < 10; i++ {
                var count int64
                tx.Model(&Refund{}).Where("SN = ?", serialNumber).Count(&count)
                if count == 0 {
                        break
                }
                // 如果存在冲突，添加随机数
                nanos = time.Now().UnixNano() % 1000000
                serialNumber = fmt.Sprintf("%s%s%06d", prefix, timestamp, nanos)
        }

        refund.SN = serialNumber
        return nil
}

func RefundRuleCheck(form RefundForm) error {
        //1. 检查订单是否存在足够的退款金额
        order, _ := GetById(form.OrderID)
        refunds := order.GetRefunds()
        currentRefundAmount := 0.0
        for _, refund := range refunds {
                currentRefundAmount += refund.TotalAmt
        }
        if currentRefundAmount+form.TotalAmt > order.TotalAmt {
                return errors.New("订单退款金额超过订单总金额,已退金额:" + strconv.FormatFloat(currentRefundAmount, 'f', 2, 64) + ",可退金额:" + strconv.FormatFloat(order.TotalAmt-currentRefundAmount, 'f', 2, 64))
        }
        //2. 检查每个订单项目(batch)是否还有足够的退货数量
        //订单原始数量
        orderitems := order.Items()
        orderitemqty := make(map[uint]int)
        for _, item := range orderitems {
                orderitemqty[item.ID] += item.Qty
        }
        //已退总数量
        refunditemqty := make(map[uint]int)
        for _, refund := range refunds {
                refunditems := refund.GetItems()
                for _, item := range refunditems {
                        refunditemqty[item.OrderItemID] += item.Qty
                }
        }
        for _, item := range form.Items {
                if orderitemqty[item.OrderItemID]-refunditemqty[item.OrderItemID] < item.Qty {
                        return errors.New("订单项目" + strconv.FormatUint(uint64(item.OrderItemID), 10) +
                                "退款数量超过订单项目总数量(" + strconv.FormatUint(uint64(orderitemqty[item.OrderItemID]), 10) + "),已退数量:" + strconv.Itoa(refunditemqty[item.OrderItemID]) +
                                ",可退数量:" + strconv.Itoa(item.Qty))
                }
        }
        //3. 检查退款
        // 先获取各个支付子项的已退合计金额
        refundpaymentamts := make(map[uint]float64)
        for _, refund := range refunds {
                refunditems := refund.GetAmts()
                for _, item := range refunditems {
                        refundpaymentamts[item.OrderPaymentItemID] += item.Amt
                }
        }
        orderpayments, _ := SearchPayment(SearchPaymentForm{
                Pager: utils.Pager{
                        PageNo:   1, //这里应该只搜索出一个
                        PageSize: 1,
                },
                Status:  models.StatusValid,
                OrderID: form.OrderID,
        })
        if len(orderpayments) == 0 {
                return errors.New("支付项不存在")
        }
        // 再获取各个支付子项的合计金额
        totalpaymentamts := make(map[uint]float64)
        orderpayment := orderpayments[0]
        orderpaymentitems, _ := orderpayment.GetItems()
        for _, pitem := range orderpaymentitems {
                totalpaymentamts[pitem.ID] += pitem.Amt
        }
        // 检查各个支付项的已退合计金额是否超过合计金额
        for _, refundamt := range form.Amts {
                if totalpaymentamts[refundamt.OrderPaymentItemID]-refundpaymentamts[refundamt.OrderPaymentItemID] < refundamt.Amt {
                        return errors.New("支付项" + strconv.FormatUint(uint64(refundamt.OrderPaymentItemID), 10) +
                                "退款金额超过支付项总金额,已退金额:" + strconv.FormatFloat(refundpaymentamts[refundamt.OrderPaymentItemID], 'f', 2, 64) +
                                ",可退金额:" + strconv.FormatFloat(totalpaymentamts[refundamt.OrderPaymentItemID]-refundpaymentamts[refundamt.OrderPaymentItemID], 'f', 2, 64))
                }
        }

        return nil
}

func (refund *Refund) GetItems() []RefundItem {
        db := database.Conn
        var items []RefundItem
        db.Where("refund_id = ?", refund.ID).Find(&items)
        return items
}

func (refund *Refund) GetAmts() []RefundAmt {
        db := database.Conn
        var amts []RefundAmt
        db.Where("refund_id = ?", refund.ID).Find(&amts)
        return amts
}

func GetRefundBySn(sn string) (*Refund, error) {
        var refund Refund
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("sn = ?", sn).First(&refund).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &refund, nil
}

// 撤销 beanbatch、bean 库存、 account 账户记账记录
func (refund *Refund) StockUndo() error {
        db := database.Conn
        tx := db.Begin()
        refunditems := refund.GetItems()
        log.Printf("退款商品撤销 %v", refunditems)
        for _, item := range refunditems {
                log.Printf("退款商品撤销 %v, qty: %v", item.ID, item.Qty)
                orderitem, _ := GetItemById(item.OrderItemID)
                itembean, err := bean.GetById(orderitem.BeanID)
                if err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                itembatch, err := bean.GetBatchById(orderitem.BeanBatchID)
                if err != nil {
                        return err
                }
                itembean.Stocking(item.Qty, itembatch.BeanType, bean.StockLogTypeReturn,
                        "refund", refund.SN+"|"+strconv.FormatUint(uint64(item.ID), 10),
                        fmt.Sprintf("订单%v退款%v", refund.OrderID, refund.SN+"|"+strconv.FormatUint(uint64(item.ID), 10)))
                itembatch.Log(item.Qty, "refund", refund.SN+"|"+strconv.FormatUint(uint64(item.ID), 10),
                        "订单退货"+refund.SN+"|"+strconv.FormatUint(uint64(item.ID), 10))
                if err := db.Model(&item).Where("id = ?", item.ID).Update("status", models.StatusInvalid).Error; err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        tx.Commit()
        return nil
}

// 撤销退款金额
func (refund *Refund) AmtUndo() error {
        db := database.Conn
        tx := db.Begin()
        refundamts := refund.GetAmts()
        log.Printf("退款金额撤销 %v", refundamts)
        for _, ritem := range refundamts {
                opitem, _ := GetPaymentItemById(ritem.OrderPaymentItemID)
                switch opitem.Type {
                case OrderPaymentTypeShopAccount:
                        accid, _ := strconv.Atoi(opitem.Ids)
                        acc, _ := shop.GetAccountById(accid)
                        log.Printf("账户 %v 退款金额撤销 %v", acc.Name, ritem.Amt)
                        acc.Balancing(ritem.Amt, "refund", refund.SN)
                case OrderPaymentTypeCash:
                        //TODO: 现金退款 - 应该也用账户处理
                case OrderPaymentTypeAlipay:
                        //TODO: 支付宝退款
                case OrderPaymentTypeWeChat:
                        //TODO: 微信退款
                case OrderPaymentTypeBank:
                        //TODO: 银行退款
                case OrderPaymentTypeCredit:
                        //TODO: 积分退款
                case OrderPaymentTypeGiftCard:
                        //TODO: 礼品卡退款
                case OrderPaymentTypeOther:
                        //TODO: 其他退款
                }
                if err := db.Model(&ritem).Where("id = ?", ritem.ID).Update("status", models.StatusInvalid).Error; err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        tx.Commit()
        return nil

}

type SearchRefundForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        OrderID uint `form:"order_id"`
}

func SearchRefund(q SearchRefundForm) ([]Refund, int) {
        db := database.Conn
        var refunds []Refund
        var statement = db.Model(&Refund{})
        if q.OrderID != 0 {
                statement = statement.Where("order_id = ?", q.OrderID)
        }
        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()
        var count int64
        statement.Where(&Refund{}).Count(&count).Order(sort).Offset(start).Limit(pagesize).Find(&refunds)
        return refunds, len(refunds)
}

package page

import (
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/models/category"
        "hahacking/utils"
        "strings"

        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type Page struct {
        models.Model
        Code         string             `json:"code"`
        Title        string             `json:"title"`
        Subtitle     string             `json:"subtitle"`
        Summary      string             `json:"summary"` //tldr
        Author       string             `json:"author"`
        PublishDate  string             `json:"publish_date"`
        Type         PageType           `json:"type"`                     //page,post,recipe,cupping
        Content      string             `json:"content" gorm:"type:text"` //could be html
        Status       string             `json:"status"`
        CategoryID   *uint              `json:"category_id"`
        CategoryPath string             `json:"category_path"`
        Category     *category.Category `json:"category" gorm:"foreignKey:CategoryID;references:ID"`
}

type PageType string

const (
        PageTypePage     PageType = "page"
        PageTypeRedirect PageType = "redirect"
        PageTypeForward  PageType = "forward"
)

var PageTypes = map[PageType]string{
        PageTypePage:     "页面",
        PageTypeRedirect: "前端跳转",
        PageTypeForward:  "后端替换",
}

func (o *Page) AfterUpdate(tx *gorm.DB) (err error) {
        // o.CategoryPath = o.Category.Path()
        return nil
}

func Meta(name string) map[string]any {
        names := strings.Split(name, ",")
        result := make(map[string]any)
        for _, n := range names {
                if n == "PageStatus" {
                        result[n] = models.ConvertMapToArray(models.CommonStatuses)
                }
                if n == "PageType" {
                        result[n] = models.ConvertMapToArray(PageTypes)
                }
        }
        return result
}

func GetById(id uint) (*Page, error) {
        var o Page
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("id = ?", id).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}
func GetByCode(code string) (*Page, error) {
        var o Page
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("code = ?", code).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}

type SearchForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        Title      string `form:"title"`
        Code       string `form:"code"`
        CategoryID *uint  `form:"category_id"`
        Author     string `form:"author"`
        Status     string `form:"status"`
}

func Search(q SearchForm) ([]Page, int) {
        db := database.Conn
        var os []Page
        var statement = db.Model(&Page{}).Preload("Category")
        if q.Title != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("title LIKE ?", "%"+q.Title+"%")
        }
        if q.Code != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("code like ?", "%"+q.Code+"%")
        }
        if q.CategoryID != nil {
                statement = statement.Where("category_id = ?", *q.CategoryID)
        }
        if q.Status != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", q.Status)
        }
        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()
        var count int64
        statement.
                Count(&count).
                Order(sort).
                Offset(start).
                Limit(pagesize).
                Find(&os)
        return os, int(count)
}

type UpdateForm struct {
        ID           uint   `json:"id"`
        Title        string `json:"title"`
        Code         string `json:"code"`
        Status       string `json:"status"`
        Type         string `json:"type"`
        PublishDate  string `json:"publish_date"`
        Content      string `json:"content"`
        Author       string `json:"author"`
        CategoryID   *uint  `json:"category_id"`
        CategoryPath string `json:"category_path"`
        utils.Pager
}

func (form *UpdateForm) Validate() map[string]string {
        var result = make(map[string]string)
        if form.Title == "" {
                result["title"] = "请填写标题"
        }
        if form.Code == "" {
                result["code"] = "请填写编码"
        } else {
                existingBean, err := GetByCode(form.Code) // Changed from form["code"] to form.Code
                if err == nil && existingBean.ID != 0 && existingBean.ID != form.ID {
                        result["code"] = "编码已存在"
                }
        }

        return result
}

func Update(o *Page) (*Page, error) {
        db := database.Conn
        tx := db.Begin()

        if o.ID != 0 {
                if err := tx.Model(&Page{}).Where("id = ?", o.ID).Updates(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return nil, err
                }
        } else {
                if err := tx.Create(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return nil, err
                }
        }

        tx.Commit()
        return o, nil
}

func Delete(id int) error {
        db := database.GetConn()
        tx := db.Begin()

        if err := tx.Delete(&Page{}, id).Error; err != nil {
                tx.Rollback()
                return err
        }

        tx.Commit()
        return nil
}

package price

import (
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/utils"
        "strings"

        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type Price struct {
        ID uint `json:"id" form:"id" gorm:"primaryKey"`
        models.Model
        BeanID       uint       `json:"bean_id" gorm:"not null;uniqueIndex:idx_bean_id_pricelevel_id"`
        PriceLevelID uint       `json:"pricelevel_id" gorm:"column:pricelevel_id;not null;uniqueIndex:idx_bean_id_pricelevel_id"`
        PriceLevel   PriceLevel `json:"pricelevel" gorm:"foreignKey:PriceLevelID;references:ID"`
        Price        float64    `json:"price"`
        Status       string     `json:"status"`
}

type SearchForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        BeanID    uint   `form:"bean_id"`
        LevelType string `form:"level_type"`
        Status    string `form:"status"`
}

func Search(q SearchForm) ([]Price, int) {
        db := database.Conn

        var statement = db.Model(&Price{})
        if q.LevelType != "" {
                statement = statement.Joins("JOIN t_price_level ON t_price_level.id = t_price.pricelevel_id").Where("t_price_level.type = ?", q.LevelType)
        }
        if q.BeanID != 0 {
                statement = statement.Where("bean_id = ?", q.BeanID)
        }
        statement.Preload("PriceLevel")

        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()

        // if q.Pager.SortBy == "level_name" {
        //         sort = "t_price_level.name " + q.Pager.SortDesc
        // }
        var count int64
        var os []Price
        statement.
                Count(&count).
                Order(sort).
                Offset(start).
                Limit(pagesize).
                Find(&os)
        return os, int(count)
}
func Meta(name string) map[string]any {
        names := strings.Split(name, ",")
        result := make(map[string]any)
        for _, n := range names {
                if n == "LevelType" {
                        result[n] = models.ConvertMapToArray(LevelTypes)
                }
                if n == "Level" {
                        // 直接获取全部level的kv map
                        levels, _ := SearchLevel(SearchLevelForm{
                                Status: "valid",
                        })
                        result[n] = levels
                }
        }
        return result
}

func GetById(id uint) (*Price, error) {
        var o Price
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("id = ?", id).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}
func Update(o *Price) error {
        db := database.Conn

        if o.ID != 0 {
                if err := db.Model(&Price{}).Where("id = ?", o.ID).Updates(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        } else {
                if err := db.Create(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        return nil
}

func Delete(id int) error {
        db := database.Conn
        if err := db.Delete(&Price{}, id).Error; err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return nil
}

package price

import (
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/utils"

        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type PriceLevel struct {
        ID uint `json:"id" form:"id" gorm:"primaryKey"`
        models.Model
        Name        string    `json:"name"`
        Type        LevelType `json:"type"`
        Discount    float64   `json:"discount" gorm:"column:discount"`
        Status      string    `json:"status"`
        Description string    `json:"description"`
}
type LevelType string

const (
        LevelTypeB LevelType = "B" // 批发价
        LevelTypeC LevelType = "C" // 零售价
)

var LevelTypes = map[LevelType]string{
        LevelTypeB: "批发价",
        LevelTypeC: "零售价",
}

func GetLevelById(id uint) (*PriceLevel, error) {
        var o PriceLevel
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("id = ?", id).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}
func GetLevelByCode(code string) (*PriceLevel, error) {
        var o PriceLevel
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("code = ?", code).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}

type SearchLevelForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        Name   string `form:"name"`
        Code   string `form:"code"`
        Type   string `form:"type"`
        Status string `form:"status"`
}

func SearchLevel(q SearchLevelForm) ([]PriceLevel, int) { // Update return type to []PriceLevel
        db := database.Conn
        var os []PriceLevel                     // Update to []PriceLevel
        var statement = db.Model(&PriceLevel{}) // Update model to PriceLevel
        if q.Name != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("name LIKE ?", "%"+q.Name+"%")
        }
        if q.Type != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("type = ?", q.Type)
        }
        if q.Status != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", q.Status)
        }
        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()
        var count int64
        statement.Where(&PriceLevel{}).
                Count(&count).
                Order(sort).
                Offset(start).
                Limit(pagesize).
                Find(&os)
        return os, int(count)
}

func UpdateLevel(o *PriceLevel) error {
        db := database.Conn

        if o.ID != 0 {
                if err := db.Model(&PriceLevel{}).Where("id = ?", o.ID).Updates(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        } else {
                if err := db.Create(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        return err
                }
        }
        return nil
}

func DeleteLevel(id int) error {
        db := database.Conn
        if err := db.Delete(&PriceLevel{}, id).Error; err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return nil
}

package report

import (
        "encoding/json"
        "fmt"
        "hahacking/common/logger"
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/database/datatypes"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/utils"
        "strconv"

        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

/*
 */
type Chart struct {
        models.Model
        Code        string         `json:"code"`
        Name        string         `json:"name"`
        Type        ChartType      `json:"type"`
        Description string         `json:"description"`
        Tags        *uint          `json:"tags" `
        Size        *int           `json:"size"`
        Params      datatypes.JSON `json:"params" gorm:"type:varchar(2048);column:params"`
        ColNames    datatypes.JSON `json:"colnames" gorm:"type:varchar(2048);column:colnames"`
        ReportID    *uint          `json:"report_id"`
        Report      *Report        `json:"report" gorm:"foreignKey:ReportID;references:ID"`
        Status      models.Status
}

func (r *Chart) TableName() string {
        return "t_reportchart"
}

type ChartType string

const (
        ChartTypeBar     ChartType = "bar"
        ChartTypeLine    ChartType = "line"
        ChartTypePie     ChartType = "pie"
        ChartTypeTable   ChartType = "table"
        ChartTypeMap     ChartType = "map"
        ChartTypeScatter ChartType = "scatter"
        ChartTypeRadar   ChartType = "radar"
        ChartTypeCard    ChartType = "card"
        //以下later
        ChartTypeGauge     ChartType = "gauge"
        ChartTypeWordCloud ChartType = "wordcloud"
        ChartTypeHeatmap   ChartType = "heatmap"
)

var ChartTypes = map[ChartType]string{
        ChartTypeBar:       "柱状图",
        ChartTypeLine:      "折线图",
        ChartTypePie:       "饼图",
        ChartTypeTable:     "表格",
        ChartTypeMap:       "地图",
        ChartTypeScatter:   "散点图",
        ChartTypeRadar:     "雷达图",
        ChartTypeCard:      "卡片",
        ChartTypeGauge:     "仪表盘",
        ChartTypeWordCloud: "词云",
        ChartTypeHeatmap:   "热力图",
}

func (rc *Chart) Plot(reqParams map[string]any) map[string]any {
        //处理报表静态参数
        params := map[string]any{}
        json.Unmarshal(rc.Params, &params)
        //处理请求中包含的动态参数
        for key, value := range reqParams {
                params[key] = value
        }
        report := rc.GetReport()
        size := 1000
        if rc.Size != nil {
                size = *rc.Size
        }
        logger.Debug("********** plot params", params)
        results, err := report.Generate(params, size)
        if err != nil {
                return nil
        }

        cols := report.GetColumns()
        if len(cols) == 0 {
                for _, col := range results["cols"].([]string) {
                        cols = append(cols, Column{
                                Name:        col,
                                DisplayName: col,
                                Sort:        "1",
                                Status:      models.StatusValid,
                        })
                }
        }
        var rcCols []string
        if rc.ColNames != nil {
                json.Unmarshal(rc.ColNames, &rcCols)
        }
        trimedCols := make([]Column, 0)
        for _, col := range cols {
                if containsString(rcCols, col.Name) {
                        trimedCols = append(trimedCols, col)
                }
        }

        switch rc.Type {
        case ChartTypeBar:
                return buildBar(trimedCols, results)
        case ChartTypeLine:
                return buildBar(trimedCols, results)
        case ChartTypePie:
                return buildPie(trimedCols, results)
        case ChartTypeTable:
                return buildTable(results)
        // case ChartTypeMap:
        //         return buildMap(results)
        // case ChartTypeScatter:
        //         return buildScatter(results)
        case ChartTypeRadar:
                return buildRadar(trimedCols, results)
        case ChartTypeCard:
                return buildCard(results)
                // case ChartTypeGauge:
                //         return buildGauge(results)
                // case ChartTypeWordCloud:
                //         return buildWordCloud(results)
                // case ChartTypeHeatmap:
                //         return buildHeatmap(results)
        }

        return nil
}
func containsString(list []string, target string) bool {
        for _, s := range list {
                if s == target {
                        return true
                }
        }
        return false
}

// convertToString 将任意类型转换为字符串
func convertToString(value any) string {
        if value == nil {
                return ""
        }
        switch v := value.(type) {
        case string:
                return v
        case int64:
                return strconv.FormatInt(v, 10)
        case int:
                return strconv.Itoa(v)
        case float64:
                return strconv.FormatFloat(v, 'f', -1, 64)
        case float32:
                return strconv.FormatFloat(float64(v), 'f', -1, 32)
        case bool:
                return strconv.FormatBool(v)
        default:
                return fmt.Sprintf("%v", v)
        }
}

func buildCard(results map[string]any) map[string]any {
        return buildTable(results)
}

/*
         雷达图
                每一行是一个维度，
                第一列是维度名称 - 如日期
*/
func buildRadar(cols []Column, results map[string]any) map[string]any {
        var data = make([]map[string]any, 0)
        var dataKeys = make([]string, 0)
        var dataLabels = make(map[string]string)
        categoryCol := cols[0]
        categoryKey := categoryCol.Name
        valueCols := cols[1:]

        for _, valueCol := range valueCols {
                dataKeys = append(dataKeys, valueCol.Name)
                dataLabels[valueCol.Name] = valueCol.DisplayName
        }

        categories := make([]string, 0)       //维度名称数组
        maxValues := make(map[string]float64) //每个维度最大值
        var maxValue, minValue float64        //所有维度的最大值和最小值
        result := results["list"].([]map[string]any)
        for _, row := range result {
                categoryName := convertToString(row[categoryKey])
                categories = append(categories, categoryName)
                values := make([]string, 0)
                for _, dataKey := range dataKeys {
                        dbvalue := row[dataKey]
                        dataLabel := dataLabels[dataKey]
                        value := convertToFloat64(dbvalue)
                        if value > maxValues[dataLabel] {
                                maxValues[dataLabel] = value
                        }
                        if value < minValue {
                                minValue = value
                        }
                        if value > maxValue {
                                maxValue = value
                        }
                        values = append(values, strconv.FormatFloat(value, 'f', -1, 64))
                }
                data = append(data, map[string]any{
                        "value": values,
                        "name":  categoryName,
                })
        }
        var indicator = make([]any, 0)
        for maxName, maxValue := range maxValues {
                indicator = append(indicator, map[string]any{
                        "name": maxName,
                        "max":  strconv.FormatFloat(maxValue, 'f', -1, 64),
                })
        }
        return map[string]any{
                "legend":    categories,
                "indicator": indicator,
                "data":      data,
        }
}
func convertToFloat64(value any) float64 {
        if value == nil {
                return 0.0
        }
        tmp, _ := strconv.ParseFloat(value.(string), 64)
        return tmp
}

func buildTable(results map[string]any) map[string]any {
        return map[string]any{
                "data": results["list"],
                "cols": results["cols"],
        }
}

// 饼图 只支持一行结果集
func buildPie(cols []Column, results map[string]any) map[string]any {
        result := results["list"].([]map[string]any)
        var data = make([]map[string]any, 0)
        if len(result) > 0 {
                dataRow := result[0]
                for _, col := range cols {
                        colname := col.DisplayName
                        if colname == "" {
                                colname = col.Name
                        }
                        data = append(data, map[string]any{
                                "value": dataRow[col.Name],
                                "name":  colname,
                        })
                }
        }
        return map[string]any{
                "data": data,
        }
}

func buildBar(cols []Column, results map[string]any) map[string]any {
        categoryCol := cols[0]          // 第一个是分类列(x轴)
        categoryKey := categoryCol.Name //x轴 列名
        valueCols := cols[1:]           // 剩余的是值列(y轴)

        var dataKeys []string
        var dataLabels []string
        var dataLabelMap = make(map[string]string)
        var data = make(map[string]any, 0)

        for _, valueCol := range valueCols {
                dataKeys = append(dataKeys, valueCol.Name)
                dataLabels = append(dataLabels, valueCol.DisplayName)
                dataLabelMap[valueCol.Name] = valueCol.DisplayName
                data[valueCol.DisplayName] = make([]any, 0)
        }

        result := results["list"].([]map[string]any)
        categories := make([]string, 0)
        for _, row := range result {
                categories = append(categories, convertToString(row[categoryKey]))
                for _, dataKey := range dataKeys {
                        data[dataLabelMap[dataKey]] = append(data[dataLabelMap[dataKey]].([]any),
                                convertToString(row[dataKey]))
                }
        }

        return map[string]any{
                "data":         data,
                "categories":   categories,
                "datalabels":   dataLabels,
                "datalabelmap": dataLabelMap,
                "datakeys":     dataKeys,
                "cols":         cols,
        }
}
func (rc *Chart) GetReport() *Report {
        if rc.Report == nil {
                report, err := GetById(*rc.ReportID)
                if err != nil {
                        return nil
                }
                rc.Report = report
        }
        return rc.Report
}
func GetChart(idorcode string) (*Chart, error) {
        var chart *Chart
        if id, err := strconv.Atoi(idorcode); err == nil {
                chart, err = GetChartById(uint(id))
                if err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
        } else {
                chart, err = GetChartByCode(idorcode)
                if err != nil {
                        return nil, err
                }
        }
        return chart, nil
}
func GetChartById(id uint) (*Chart, error) {
        var o Chart
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("id = ?", id).Preload("Report").First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}

type SearchChartForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        Name     string `form:"name"`
        Code     string
        Status   string `form:"status"`
        Type     string `form:"type"`
        ReportID string `form:"report_id"`
}

func SearchChart(q SearchChartForm) ([]Chart, int) {
        db := database.Conn
        var os []Chart
        var statement = db.Model(&Chart{}).Preload("Report")
        if q.Name != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("name LIKE ?", "%"+q.Name+"%")
        }
        if q.Code != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("code = ?", q.Code)
        }
        if q.Type != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("type = ?", q.Type)
        }
        if q.Status != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", q.Status)
        } else {
                statement = statement.Where("status = ?", models.StatusValid)
        }
        if q.ReportID != "" {
                statement = statement.Where("report_id = ?", q.ReportID)
        }
        _, pagesize, start, sort := q.Pager.Paging()
        var count int64
        statement.
                Count(&count).
                Order(sort).
                Offset(start).
                Limit(pagesize).
                Find(&os)
        return os, int(count)
}

type UpdateChartForm struct {
        ID          uint          `json:"id"`
        ReportID    uint          `json:"report_id"`
        Name        string        `json:"name"`
        Code        string        `json:"code"`
        Status      models.Status `json:"status"`
        Type        string        `json:"type"`
        Size        string        `json:"size"`
        ParamSet    string        `json:"paramset" gorm:"column:paramset;type:text"`
        Description string        `json:"description" gorm:"column:description;type:text"`
        Params      string        `json:"params" gorm:"column:params;type:text"`
        ColNames    string        `json:"colnames" gorm:"column:colnames;type:text"`
}

func (form *Chart) ValidateForm() map[string]string {
        var result = make(map[string]string)
        if form.Name == "" {
                result["name"] = "请填写名称"
        }
        if form.Code == "" {
                result["code"] = "请填写编码"
        } else {
                exist, err := GetChartByCode(form.Code)
                if err == nil && exist != nil {
                        if exist.ID != form.ID {
                                result["code"] = "编码已存在"
                        }
                } else {
                        result["code"] = "编码已存在"
                }
        }
        if form.Status == "" {
                result["status"] = "请选择状态"
        }
        if len(form.ColNames) == 0 {
                result["colnames"] = "至少要选择一列"
        }

        return result
}
func GetChartByCode(code string) (*Chart, error) {
        db := database.Conn
        var o Chart
        err := db.Where("code = ? and status = ?", code, models.StatusValid).Preload("Report").First(&o).Error
        if err != nil || err == gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, err
}

func UpdateChart(o *Chart) error {
        db := database.Conn
        tx := db.Begin()

        if o.ID != 0 {
                // 更新现有记录
                if err := tx.Model(&Chart{}).Where("id = ?", o.ID).Updates(o).Error; err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return err
                }
        } else {
                // 创建新记录
                if err := tx.Create(&o).Error; err != nil {
                        tx.Rollback()
                        return err
                }
        }

        tx.Commit()
        return nil
}

func DeleteChart(id int) error {
        db := database.GetConn()
        tx := db.Begin()

        // Delete the cupping record
        if err := tx.Delete(&Chart{}, id).Error; err != nil {
                tx.Rollback()
                return err
        }

        tx.Commit()
        return nil
}

func StatusChart(id int, to string) error {
        db := database.Conn
        if err := db.Model(&Chart{}).Where("id = ?", id).Update("status", to).Error; err != nil {
                return err
        }
        return nil
}

package report

import (
        "hahacking/database"
        "hahacking/models"
        "hahacking/utils"
        "regexp"
        "strings"
        "time"

        "gorm.io/gorm"
)

type Parameter struct {
        models.Model
        Name         string        `gorm:"column:name" json:"name"`
        Code         string        `gorm:"column:code" json:"code"`
        Label        string        `gorm:"column:label" json:"label"`
        Sort         string        `gorm:"column:sort" json:"sort"`
        Status       models.Status `gorm:"column:status" json:"status"`
        Description  string        `gorm:"type:text;column:description" json:"description"`
        DefaultValue string        `gorm:"type:text;column:defaultvalue" json:"defaultvalue"`
        ReportID     uint          `gorm:"column:report_id" json:"report_id"`
        Report       Report        `gorm:"foreignKey:ReportID;references:ID"`
        Type         ParameterType `json:"type"`
}

func (p *Parameter) TableName() string {
        return "t_reportparameter"
}

type ParameterType string

const (
        ParameterTypeText        ParameterType = "text"
        ParameterTypeDate        ParameterType = "date"
        ParameterTypeTime        ParameterType = "time"
        ParameterTypeSelect      ParameterType = "select"
        ParameterTypeMultiSelect ParameterType = "multiselect"
        ParameterTypeCheckbox    ParameterType = "checkbox"
        ParameterTypeRadio       ParameterType = "radio"
        ParameterTypeDateRange   ParameterType = "daterange"
        ParameterTypeTimeRange   ParameterType = "timerange"
)

var ParameterTypes = map[ParameterType]string{
        ParameterTypeText:        "文本",
        ParameterTypeDate:        "日期",
        ParameterTypeTime:        "时间",
        ParameterTypeSelect:      "单选",
        ParameterTypeMultiSelect: "多选",
        ParameterTypeCheckbox:    "复选框",
        ParameterTypeRadio:       "单选框",
        ParameterTypeDateRange:   "日期范围",
        ParameterTypeTimeRange:   "时间范围",
}

func (p *Parameter) ValidateForm() map[string]string {
        errs := make(map[string]string)
        if p.ReportID == 0 {
                errs["report_id"] = "报表ID不能为空"
        }
        if p.Name == "" {
                errs["name"] = "名称不能为空"
        }
        return errs
}
func (p *Parameter) GetDefaultValue() string {
        return p.Macro(p.DefaultValue, "").(string)
}
func (p *Parameter) Macro(macro string, defaultValue interface{}) interface{} {
        switch macro {
        case "timestamp()":
                return time.Now().Unix()
        case "time()":
                return time.Now().Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05")
        case "date()":
                return time.Now().Format("2006-01-02")
        case "datetime()":
                return time.Now().Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05")
        case "earliest()":
                return int64(0)
        case "earliesttime()":
                return time.Unix(0, 0).Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05")
        case "earliestdate()":
                return time.Unix(0, 0).Format("2006-01-02")
        case "CURRENTMONTH_D1":
                now := time.Now()
                return time.Date(now.Year(), now.Month(), 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, now.Location()).Format("2006-01-02")
        case "CURRENTMONTH":
                return time.Now().Format("2006-01")
        default:
                // 处理 strtotime() 函数
                if strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(macro), "strtotime") {
                        re := regexp.MustCompile(`(?i)strtotime\((.*)\)`)
                        matches := re.FindStringSubmatch(macro)
                        if len(matches) > 1 {
                                // 移除引号和括号
                                timeStr := strings.Trim(strings.Trim(matches[1], "'"), "()")
                                if t, err := time.Parse("2006-01-02 15:04:05", timeStr); err == nil {
                                        return t.Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05")
                                }
                                // 尝试解析相对时间
                                if t, err := utils.ParseRelativeTime(timeStr); err == nil {
                                        return t.Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05")
                                }
                        }
                }

                // 处理 strtodate() 函数
                if strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(macro), "strtodate") {
                        re := regexp.MustCompile(`(?i)strtodate\((.*)\)`)
                        matches := re.FindStringSubmatch(macro)
                        if len(matches) > 1 {
                                // 移除引号和括号
                                timeStr := strings.Trim(strings.Trim(matches[1], "'"), "()")
                                if t, err := utils.ParseRelativeTime(timeStr); err == nil {
                                        return t.Format("2006-01-02")
                                }
                        }
                }

                // 处理 date() 函数
                if strings.Contains(macro, "date(") {
                        re := regexp.MustCompile(`date\((.*),(.*)\)`)
                        matches := re.FindStringSubmatch(macro)
                        if len(matches) > 2 {
                                tmp, _ := utils.PHPTimeFormatRelativeFixed(strings.Trim(matches[1], "'"), strings.Trim(matches[2], "'"))
                                return tmp
                        }
                }

                return macro
        }
}

func GetParameterById(id uint) (*Parameter, error) {
        var o Parameter
        db := database.Conn
        err := db.Where("id = ?", id).First(&o).Error
        if err != nil && err != gorm.ErrRecordNotFound {
                return nil, err
        }
        return &o, nil
}
