        "log"
        "strconv"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/mitchellh/mapstructure"
)

func SearchStockLog(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var f models.SearchStockLogForm

        if c.ShouldBindQuery(&f) == nil {
                log.Println(f)
        }
        result, count := models.SearchStockLog(f)
        appG.Success("", utils.Response{
                "objects": result,
                "total":   count,
        })
}

type UpdateStockLogForm struct {
        ID          uint                `json:"id" form:"id"`
        TotalPrice  float64             `json:"totalprice"`
        TotalQty    int                 `json:"totalqty"`
        Type        models.StockLogType `json:"type"`
        BeanID      uint                `json:"bean_id"`
        BeanBatchID uint                `json:"beanbatch_id"`
        BeanType    models.BeanType     `json:"bean_type"`
}

func (form *UpdateStockLogForm) Validate() map[string]string {
        var result = make(map[string]string)
        if form.BeanID == 0 {
                result["bean_id"] = "请选择豆"
        }
        if form.BeanType == "" {
                result["bean_type"] = "请选择豆类型"
        }
        if form.TotalPrice == 0.00 {
                result["totalprice"] = "请填写总价值"
        }
        if form.TotalQty == 0 {
                result["totalqty"] = "请填写总数量"
        }
        if form.Type == "" {
                result["type"] = "请选择库存类型"
        }
        return result
}
func UpdateStockLog(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var form UpdateStockLogForm
        if err := c.ShouldBind(&form); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "绑定请求中的json到form失败，可能是格式问题", err)
                return
        }
        err := form.Validate()
        if len(err) > 0 {
                appG.Response(200, 1000, err)
                return
        }

        var o models.BeanStockLog

        if err := mapstructure.Decode(form, &o); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "映射form内容到对象失败", err)
                return
        }

        bean, _ := models.GetById(form.BeanID)
        if bean == nil {
                appG.Error(400, "未找到相关生豆信息", nil)
                return
        }
        _, userid, ok := middleware.GetCurrentUser(c)
        if !ok {
                appG.Error(400, "未登录", nil)
                return
        }
        delta := form.TotalQty
        if contains(models.StockOutTypes(), string(form.Type)) {
                delta = -delta
        }
        _, err2 := bean.Stocking(delta, form.BeanType, form.Type, "user", strconv.Itoa(int(userid)),
                "手动修改库存"+strconv.Itoa(int(form.ID)))
        if err2 != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "库存修改失败", err2)
                return
        }
        if form.BeanBatchID > 0 {
                beanbatch, _ := models.GetBatchById(form.BeanBatchID)
                if beanbatch == nil {
                        appG.Error(400, "未找到相关批次信息", nil)
                        return
                }
                beanbatch.Log(delta, "user", strconv.Itoa(int(userid)),
                        "手动修改库存"+strconv.Itoa(int(form.ID)))
        }

        appG.Success("", delta)
}
func contains(slice []string, item string) bool {
        for _, v := range slice {
                if v == item {
                        return true
                }
        }
        return false
}

package common

import (
        "crypto/md5"
        "encoding/base64"
        "encoding/hex"
        "encoding/json"
        "hahacking/common/app"
        "hahacking/config"
        "strconv"
        "time"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

func RegisterRoutes(r *gin.RouterGroup) {
        APIs := r.Group("/common")
        {
                APIs.GET("upconfig/:name", UpConfig)
        }
}

func UpConfig(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        name := c.Param("name")
        cfg := config.Get("upyun")[name]

        if cfg == nil {
                appG.Error(404, "配置不存在", nil)
                return
        }
        cfg2 := make(map[string]interface{})
        cfgMap := cfg.(map[string]interface{})
        cfg2["bucket"] = cfgMap["bucket"]
        expiration, _ := strconv.Atoi(cfgMap["formexpiration"].(string))
        cfg2["expiration"] = time.Now().Unix() + int64(expiration)
        cfg2["save-key"] = cfgMap["save-key"]
        cfg2["allow-file-type"] = cfgMap["allow-file-type"]
        cfg2["content-length-range"] = cfgMap["content-length-range"]

        policy, _ := json.Marshal(cfg2)
        policyStr := base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(policy)
        formapisecret := cfgMap["formapisecret"].(string)
        signature := md5.Sum([]byte(policyStr + "&" + formapisecret))
        signatureStr := hex.EncodeToString(signature[:])
        cfg2["policy"] = policyStr
        cfg2["signature"] = signatureStr
        cfg2["cdn"] = cfgMap["cdn"]
        appG.Success("", cfg2)
}

package cupping

import (
        "hahacking/common/app"
        "hahacking/middleware"
        models "hahacking/models/cupping"

)

func RegisterRoutes(r *gin.RouterGroup) {
        APIs := r.Group("/cupping")
        {
                APIs.GET("meta/:ids", Meta)
        }
        userAPI := APIs.Group("")
        userAPI.Use(middleware.JWTAuth())
        userAPI.Use(middleware.BaseAuth("user"))
        {
                userAPI.GET("", Search)
                userAPI.GET(":id", GetById)
                userAPI.POST("", Update)
                userAPI.DELETE(":id", Delete)

                userAPI.GET("sample", SearchSample)
                userAPI.GET("sample/:id", GetSampleById)
                userAPI.POST("sample", UpdateSample)
                userAPI.GET("sample/:id/scoresummary", GetSampleScoreSummary)

                userAPI.POST("score", UpdateScore)
                userAPI.GET("score", SearchScore)
                userAPI.GET("score/bean/:beanid", GetBeanScoreSummary) // 必须在 score/:id 之前注册，避免路由冲突
                userAPI.GET("score/:id", GetScoreById)
                userAPI.POST("score/useradd", CreateScoreByUser) //用户添加杯测评分 - 仅包含bean和评分项，不包含cupping的相关信息
        }
}

type UpdateForm struct {
        ID          uint                   `json:"id"`
        Location    string                 `json:"location"`
        Status      string                 `json:"status"`
        Method      string                 `json:"method"`
        Description string                 `json:"description"`
        Samples     []models.CuppingSample `json:"samples" `
}

func (form *UpdateForm) Validate() map[string]string {
        var result = make(map[string]string)
        log.Println("form", form)
        if form.Location == "" {
                result["location"] = "请填写地点"
        }
        if form.Status == "" {
                result["status"] = "请填写状态"
        }
        if form.Method == "" {
                result["method"] = "请填写杯测方法"
        }
        if len(form.Samples) == 0 {
                result["samples"] = "请填写杯测样本"
        }
        return result
}
func Update(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var form UpdateForm
        var o models.Cupping
        formErrors := api.Bind(&form, &o, c)
        // log.Println("form errors", formErrors)
        // log.Println("form", form.ID, form)
        // log.Println("model", o.ID, o)
        o.ID = form.ID //注意：需要手动设置一下，因为model中的id是嵌入的models.Model，在绑定的时候不会自动设置
        if len(formErrors) > 0 {
                appG.Error(400, "表单验证失败", formErrors)
                return
        }

        err := models.Update(&o)
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "更新失败", err)
                return
        }

        appG.Success("", o)
}

// Delete deletes a cupping by ID
func Delete(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id, err := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "无效的ID", err)
                return
        }

        err = models.Delete((id))
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "删除失败", err)
                return
        }

        appG.Success("", nil)
}

// CreateSample creates a new sample
func CreateSample(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var sample models.CuppingSample
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&sample); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "参数错误", err)
                return
        }

        if err := models.UpdateSample(&sample); err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "创建样本失败", err)
                return
        }

        appG.Success("", sample)
}

// UpdateSample updates an existing sample
func UpdateSample(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var sample models.CuppingSample
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&sample); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "参数错误", err)
                return
        }

        if err := models.UpdateSample(&sample); err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "更新样本失败", err)
                return
        }

        appG.Success("", sample)
}

// DeleteSample deletes a sample by ID
func DeleteSample(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id, err := strconv.ParseUint(c.Param("id"), 10, 32)
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "无效的ID", err)
                return
        }

        sample, err := models.GetSampleById(uint(id))
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "获取样本失败", err)
                return
        }
        if err := sample.Delete(); err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "删除样本失败", err)
                return
        }

        appG.Success("", nil)
}

// 在杯测页面中的smaple上添加评分

                appG.Error(400, "参数错误", err)
                return

                appG.Error(500, "创建评分失败", err)

        }

type CreateScoreByUserForm struct {
        //评分项
        models.CuppingScoreItem
        //cupping信息
        Location string `json:"location"`
        Method   string `json:"method"` //制作方法
        //sample信息
        UserID    uint    `json:"user_id"`
        BeanID    uint    `json:"bean_id"`
        BeanQty   uint    `json:"bean_qty"`
        GrindSize string  `json:"grind_size"`
        WaterTemp float64 `json:"water_temp"`
}

// 用户直接根据豆子添加（自动创建cupping、sample、score）
func CreateScoreByUser(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var form CreateScoreByUserForm
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&form); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "参数错误", err)
                return
        }
        _, cuid, ok := middleware.GetCurrentUser(c)
        if !ok {
                appG.Error(401, "请先登录", nil)
                return
        }
        cupping := models.Cupping{
                Location: form.Location,
                Method:   models.CuppingMethod(form.Method),
                UserID:   &cuid,
                Status:   models.CuppingStatusValid,
        }
        models.Update(&cupping)
        cuppingSample := models.CuppingSample{
                CuppingID: cupping.ID,
                BeanID:    form.BeanID,
                BeanQty:   form.BeanQty,
                UserID:    &cuid,
                GrindSize: form.GrindSize,
                WaterTemp: form.WaterTemp,
                Method:    models.CuppingMethod(form.Method),
        }
        models.UpdateSample(&cuppingSample)

        score := models.CuppingScore{
                CuppingID:        cupping.ID,
                CuppingSampleID:  cuppingSample.ID,
                UserID:           cuid,
                BeanID:           &form.BeanID,
                CuppingScoreItem: form.CuppingScoreItem,
                Status:           commonmodels.StatusValid,
        }
        models.UpdateScore(&score)
        appG.Success("", score)
}

// UpdateScore updates an existing score
func UpdateScore(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}

        var score models.CuppingScore
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&score); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "参数错误", err)
                return
        }
        if score.Status == "" {
                score.Status = commonmodels.StatusValid
        }

        if err := models.UpdateScore(&score); err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "更新评分失败", err)
                return
        }

        appG.Success("", score)
}

// DeleteScore deletes a score by ID
func DeleteScore(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id, err := strconv.ParseUint(c.Param("id"), 10, 32)
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "无效的ID", err)
                return
        }

        if err := models.DeleteScore(uint(id)); err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "删除评分失败", err)
                return
        }

        appG.Success("", nil)
}

// GetSampleScoreSummary gets the score summary for a sample
func GetSampleScoreSummary(c *gin.Context) {

package favorite

import (
        "hahacking/common/app"
        "hahacking/middleware"
        rootmodels "hahacking/models"
        models "hahacking/models/favorite"
        "log"
        "strconv"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

func RegisterRoutes(r *gin.RouterGroup) {
        APIs := r.Group("/favorite")
        {
                // APIs.GET("meta/:ids", Meta)

                userAPI := APIs.Group("")
                userAPI.Use(middleware.JWTAuth())
                userAPI.Use(middleware.BaseAuth("user"))
                {
                        userAPI.GET("", Search)
                        userAPI.GET(":id", GetById)
                        userAPI.POST("", Create)

                        userAPI.GET("add/:item_id/:type", UserAdd)
                        userAPI.GET("remove/:item_id/:type", UserRemove)
                }
                adminAPI := APIs.Group("")
                adminAPI.Use(middleware.JWTAuth())
                adminAPI.Use(middleware.BaseAuth("admin"))
                {
                        adminAPI.DELETE(":id", Delete)
                }
        }
}

func UserAdd(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        typ := c.Params.ByName("type")
        itemID := c.Params.ByName("item_id")

        // 使用GetCurrentUser函数获取用户信息
        username, uid, ok := middleware.GetCurrentUser(c)
        if !ok {
                appG.Error(401, "无法获取用户信息", nil)
                return
        }

        log.Printf("用户操作: username=%s, uid=%d", username, uid)

        f := models.UserAdd(typ, itemID, uid)
        appG.Success("", f)
}
func UserRemove(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        typ := c.Params.ByName("type")
        itemID := c.Params.ByName("item_id")

        // 使用GetCurrentUser函数获取用户信息
        username, uid, ok := middleware.GetCurrentUser(c)
        if !ok {
                appG.Error(401, "无法获取用户信息", nil)
                return
        }

        log.Printf("用户操作: username=%s, uid=%d", username, uid)

        f := models.UserRemove(typ, itemID, uid)
        appG.Success("", f)
}

// func Meta(c *gin.Context) {
//         appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
//         name := c.Params.ByName("ids")
//         result := models.Meta(name)
//         appG.Success("", result)
// }

type CreateForm struct {
        Name   string `json:"name" form:"name"`
        Code   string `json:"code" form:"code"`
        Type   string `json:"type" form:"type"`
        Status string `json:"status" form:"status"`
        ItemID string `json:"item_id" form:"item_id"`
}
type UpdateForm struct {
        CreateForm
        ID int `json:"id" form:"id" uri:"id" binding:"required"`
}

func Search(c *gin.Context) {
        var f models.SearchForm
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}

        if c.ShouldBindQuery(&f) == nil {
                log.Println(f)
        }

        result, count := models.Search(f)
        appG.Success("", map[string]any{
                "objects": result,
                "total":   count,
        })
}

func Create(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var f CreateForm
        if err := c.ShouldBind(&f); err != nil {
                appG.Failed(400, "参数绑定失败: "+err.Error())
                return
        }

        // 验证必要字段
        if f.Name == "" {
                appG.Failed(400, "name不能为空")
                return
        }

        var o models.Favorite
        o.ItemID = f.ItemID
        o.Type = f.Type
        o.Status = rootmodels.Status(f.Status)

        // 设置状态
        if f.Status == "" {
                o.Status = rootmodels.StatusValid
        } else {
                o.Status = rootmodels.Status(f.Status)
        }

        // 处理时间字段

        result := models.Update(&o)
        if result != nil {
                appG.Failed(500, "创建banner失败: "+result.Error())
                return
        }

        appG.Success("banner创建成功", o)
}
func GetById(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id, err := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        result, err := models.GetById(uint(id))

        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                return
        }

        appG.Success("", result)
}
func Delete(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id, err := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                return
        }
        err = models.Delete(id)

        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                return
        }

        appG.Success("", nil)
}

package order

import (
        "hahacking/common/app"
        "hahacking/middleware"

        "hahacking/models/order"
        models "hahacking/models/order"
        "hahacking/utils"
        "log"
        "strconv"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

func RegisterRoutes(r *gin.RouterGroup) {
        APIs := r.Group("/order")
        {
                APIs.GET("meta/:ids", Meta)
        }
        userAPI := APIs.Group("")
        userAPI.Use(middleware.JWTAuth())
        userAPI.Use(middleware.BaseAuth("user"))
        {
                userAPI.GET("", Search)
                userAPI.GET(":id", GetById)
                userAPI.GET(":id/items", GetItems)
                userAPI.GET(":id/payment", GetPayment)
                userAPI.GET(":id/refunds", GetRefunds)
                userAPI.DELETE(":id", Remove)
                userAPI.POST("update", Update)
                userAPI.POST("create", Create)
                userAPI.POST("place", Place)
                userAPI.POST("settle", Settle)
                userAPI.POST("refund", Refund)
                // userAPI.POST("refundUndo/:id", RefundUndo) //TODO 需要退款撤销吗？
                userAPI.POST("afterSettle/:id", AfterSettle) //结算后更新库存 - 临时
                userAPI.POST("settleUndo/:id", SettleUndo)
                userAPI.GET("getPriceLevelDiscount", GetPriceLevelDiscount)
        }
}

func Meta(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        name := c.Params.ByName("ids")
        result := models.Meta(name)
        appG.Success("", result)
}

func GetById(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        idparam := c.Params.ByName("id")
        id, err := strconv.Atoi(idparam)
        var o *models.Order
        if err != nil {
                o, err = models.GetBySn(idparam)
        } else {
                o, err = models.GetById(uint(id))
        }

        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到订单")
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", o)
}

func GetItems(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        orderid, _ := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        order, _ := models.GetById(uint(orderid))
        items := order.Items()
        appG.Success("", items)
}

func GetPayment(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        orderid, _ := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        order, _ := models.GetById(uint(orderid))
        payment := order.Payment()
        appG.Success("", payment)
}

func Search(c *gin.Context) {
        var f models.SearchForm
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}

        if c.ShouldBindQuery(&f) == nil {
                log.Println(f)
        }
        result, count := models.Search(f)
        appG.Success("", utils.Response{
                "objects": result,
                "total":   count,
        })
}

/*
* 主要是对订单Item的确认
 */
func Create(c *gin.Context) {
        Update(c)
}

func Update(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var f models.UpdateForm
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&f); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "绑定请求中的json到form失败，可能是格式问题", err)
                return
        }
        if err := f.Validate(); err != nil {
                appG.Response(200, 1000, err)
                return
        }

        o, err := models.Update(&f)
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "更新失败", err)
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", o)
}

/*
* 主要是订单支付方式（组合）的确认
 */
func Place(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var form models.PlaceForm
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&form); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "绑定请求中的json到form失败，可能是格式问题", err)
                return
        }
        err := form.Validate()
        if err != nil {
                appG.Response(200, 1000, err)
                return
        }
        order, _ := models.GetById(form.OrderID)
        if order == nil {
                appG.Error(400, "订单不存在", nil)
                return
        }

        orderPayment, err2 := order.Place(form)
        if err2 != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "订单确认失败", err2)
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", orderPayment)
}

func Settle(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}

        var form models.SettleForm
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&form); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "绑定请求中的json到form失败，可能是格式问题", err)
                return
        }
        err := form.Validate()
        if err != nil {
                appG.Response(200, 1000, err)
                return
        }
        order, _ := models.GetById(form.OrderID)

        o, err2 := order.Settle(form)
        if err2 != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "订单结算失败", err2)
                return
        }

        // err3 := o.OnSettled()
        // if err3 != nil {
        //         appG.Error(400, "结算后库存更新失败，需要修复", err)
        //         return
        // }
        appG.Success("", o)
}
func SettleUndo(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        orderid, _ := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        order, _ := models.GetById(uint(orderid))

        err2 := order.SettleUndo()
        if err2 != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "订单结算撤销失败", err2)
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", nil)
}

func AfterSettle(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        orderid, _ := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        order, _ := models.GetById(uint(orderid))
        err := order.OnSettled()
        if err != nil {
                log.Println("结算后更新库存失败", err)
                appG.Error(400, err.Error(), nil)
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", "success")
}

func GetRefunds(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        orderid, _ := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        order, _ := models.GetById(uint(orderid))
        refunds := order.GetRefunds()
        appG.Success("", refunds)
}

func Refund(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var form models.RefundForm
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&form); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "绑定请求中的json到form失败，可能是格式问题", err)
                return
        }
        err := form.Validate()
        if err != nil {
                appG.Response(200, 1000, err)
                return
        }
        order, _ := models.GetById(form.OrderID)
        if order == nil {
                appG.Error(400, "订单不存在", nil)
                return
        }
        refund, err2 := order.Refund(form)
        if err2 != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "订单退款失败", err2)
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", refund)
}
func Remove(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        orderid, _ := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        o, _ := models.GetById(uint(orderid))
        if o.Status != order.OrderStatusCreated {
                appG.Error(400, "订单状态不正确", nil)
                return
        }
        err2 := o.Remove()
        if err2 != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "订单删除失败", err2)
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", nil)
}

func GetPriceLevelDiscount(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        orderid, _ := strconv.Atoi(c.Query("orderid"))
        levelid, _ := strconv.Atoi(c.Query("levelid"))
        order, err := models.GetById(uint(orderid))
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "获取订单失败", err)
                return
        }

        priceLevelDiscount, err := order.GetPriceLevelDiscount(uint(levelid))
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "获取价格等级折扣失败", err)
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", priceLevelDiscount)
}

package page

import (
        "hahacking/common/app"
        "hahacking/middleware"
        models "hahacking/models/page"
        "hahacking/utils"
        "log"
        "strconv"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/mitchellh/mapstructure"
)

func RegisterRoutes(r *gin.RouterGroup) {
        APIs := r.Group("/page")
        {
                APIs.GET("meta/:ids", Meta)
                userAPI := APIs.Group("")
                userAPI.Use(middleware.JWTAuth())
                userAPI.Use(middleware.BaseAuth("user"))
                {
                        userAPI.GET("", Search)
                        userAPI.GET(":id", GetById)

                }
                adminAPI := APIs.Group("")
                adminAPI.Use(middleware.JWTAuth())
                adminAPI.Use(middleware.BaseAuth("admin"))
                {
                        adminAPI.POST("", Update)
                        adminAPI.DELETE(":id", Remove)
                }
        }
}

func Update(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var form models.UpdateForm
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&form); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "绑定请求中的json到form失败，可能是格式问题", err)
                return
        }
        err := form.Validate()
        if len(err) > 0 {
                appG.Response(200, 1000, err)
                return
        }
        var o models.Page
        if err := mapstructure.Decode(form, &o); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "映射form内容到对象失败", err)
                return
        }
        o.ID = uint(form.ID)
        ro, err2 := models.Update(&o)

        if err2 != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "更新失败", err2)
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", ro)
}
func GetById(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id, err := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        o, err := models.GetById(uint(id))

        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", o)
}

func Remove(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id, err := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        err = models.Delete(id)
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "删除失败", err)
                return
        }
        appG.Success("删除成功", "")
}
func Search(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var f models.SearchForm
        if c.ShouldBindQuery(&f) == nil {
                log.Println(f)
        }
        result, count := models.Search(f)
        appG.Success("", utils.Response{
                "objects": result,
                "total":   count,
        })
}

func Meta(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        name := c.Params.ByName("ids")
        result := models.Meta(name)
        appG.Success("", result)
}

package price

import (
        "fmt"
        "hahacking/common/app"
        "hahacking/middleware"
        models "hahacking/models/price"
        "hahacking/utils"
        "log"
        "strconv"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/mitchellh/mapstructure"
)

func RegisterRoutes(r *gin.RouterGroup) {
        APIs := r.Group("/price")
        {
        }
        userAPI := APIs.Group("")
        userAPI.Use(middleware.JWTAuth())
        userAPI.Use(middleware.BaseAuth("user"))
        {
                userAPI.GET("", Search)
                userAPI.POST("", Update)
                userAPI.GET(":id", GetById)
                userAPI.GET("level", SearchLevel)
                userAPI.POST("level", UpdateLevel)
                userAPI.GET("level/:id", GetLevelById)

        }
}

type UpdateForm struct {
        ID           uint    `json:"id" form:"id"`
        Price        float64 `json:"price" form:"price"`
        PriceLevelID uint    `json:"pricelevel_id" form:"pricelevel_id"`
}

func (form *UpdateForm) Validate() map[string]string {
        var result = make(map[string]string)
        if form.Price == 0.00 {
                result["price"] = "请填写价格"
        }
        return result
}

func GetById(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id, err := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        o, err := models.GetById(uint(id))

        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", o)
}

func Search(c *gin.Context) {
        var f models.SearchForm
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}

        if c.ShouldBindQuery(&f) == nil {
                log.Println(f)
        }

        result, count := models.Search(f)
        appG.Success("", utils.Response{
                "objects": result,
                "total":   count,
        })
}

func Update(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var form UpdateForm
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&form); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "绑定请求中的json到form失败，可能是格式问题", err)
                return
        }
        err := form.Validate()
        fmt.Errorf("form validation error: %v", err)
        if len(err) > 0 {
                appG.Response(200, 1000, err)
                return
        }

        var o models.Price

        if err := mapstructure.Decode(form, &o); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "映射form内容到对象失败", err)
                return
        }
        o.ID = form.ID
        result := models.Update(&o)
        appG.Success("", result)
}
func Delete(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id, err := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        err = models.Delete(id)

        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                return
        }

        appG.Success("", nil)
}

        Name        string  `json:"name"`
        Group       string  `json:"group"`
        Discount    float64 `json:"discount"`
        Status      string  `json:"status"`
        Type        string  `json:"type"`
        LowerBound  string  `json:"lowerbound"`
        UpperBound  string  `json:"upperbound"`
        Description string  `json:"description" form:"description"`
}

func (form *UpdateLevelForm) Validate() map[string]string {
        var result = make(map[string]string)
        if form.Name == "" {
                result["name"] = "请填写名称"

        }

package report

import (
        "fmt"
        "hahacking/common/app"
        "hahacking/common/logger"
        "hahacking/common/util"
        "hahacking/middleware"
        models "hahacking/models/report"
        "log"
        "net/url"
        "strconv"
        "strings"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/xuri/excelize/v2"
)

func RegisterRoutes(r *gin.RouterGroup) {
        APIs := r.Group("/report")
        {
                // APIs.GET("meta/:ids", Meta)
                userAPI := APIs.Group("")
                userAPI.Use(middleware.JWTAuth())
                userAPI.Use(middleware.BaseAuth("user"))
                {
                        userAPI.GET("", Search)
                        userAPI.GET("meta/:ids", Meta)
                        userAPI.GET(":id", GetById)
                        userAPI.GET(":id/status/:to", Status)
                        userAPI.GET(":id/columns", GetColumns)
                        userAPI.GET(":id/parameters", GetParameters)
                        userAPI.GET(":id/sql", GetSQL)
                        userAPI.GET(":id/generate", Generate)
                        userAPI.POST("", Update)

                        userAPI.POST("parameter", UpdateParameter)
                        userAPI.DELETE("parameter/:id", DeleteParameter)

                        userAPI.POST("column", UpdateColumn)
                        userAPI.DELETE("column/:id", DeleteColumn)

                        userAPI.GET("chart", SearchChart)
                        userAPI.GET("chart/:id", GetChartById)
                        userAPI.GET("chart/:id/status/:to", StatusChart)
                        userAPI.GET("chart/:id/data", GetChartData)
                        userAPI.POST("chart", UpdateChart)

                }
                adminAPI := APIs.Group("")
                adminAPI.Use(middleware.JWTAuth())
                adminAPI.Use(middleware.BaseAuth("admin"))
                {
                        adminAPI.DELETE("/:id", Delete)
                        adminAPI.DELETE("chart/:id", DeleteChart)
                }
        }
}

func Update(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var f models.Report
        c.ShouldBind(&f)
        errs := f.ValidateForm()
        if len(errs) > 0 {
                appG.Error(500, "表单报错", errs)
                return
        }
        updatedReport, err := models.Update(&f)
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "抱歉未找到相关信息", err.Error())
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", updatedReport)
}
func Generate(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id, err := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                return
        }
        report, err := models.GetById(uint(id))
        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                return
        }

        reqParams := util.GetQueryParams(c)
        //需要检查参数是否合法
        errs := map[string]any{}
        for _, param := range report.Parameters {
                if reqParams[param.Name] == "" {
                        theDefaultValue := param.GetDefaultValue()
                        if theDefaultValue != "" {
                                reqParams[param.Name] = theDefaultValue
                        } else {
                                err := fmt.Sprintf("缺少参数：%s (%s)", param.Label, param.Name)
                                errs[param.Name] = err
                                return
                        }
                }
        }
        if len(errs) > 0 {
                appG.Error(400, "参数错误", errs)
                return
        }
        results, err := report.Generate(reqParams, 1000)
        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                return
        }
        if c.Query("export") != "" {
                if c.Query("export") == "xlsx" {
                        exportXls(report, results, c)
                        return
                } else {
                        appG.Failed(400, "不支持的导出格式")
                        return
                }

        } else {
                appG.Success("", results)
        }
}
func exportXls(report *models.Report, results map[string]any, c *gin.Context) {
        logger.Debug("exportXls", report.Name+"("+strconv.Itoa(int(report.ID))+")")
        logger.Debug("exportXls cols", results["cols"])
        f := excelize.NewFile()
        defer f.Close()

        // 设置为活动工作表
        f.SetActiveSheet(0)
        cols := results["cols"].([]models.Column)
        activeSheetIndex := f.GetActiveSheetIndex()
        sheetName := f.GetSheetName(activeSheetIndex)

        logger.Debug("exportXls", "列数:", len(cols), "工作表名:", sheetName)

        // 设置表头
        for j, col := range cols {
                cellName := fmt.Sprintf("%c1", 'A'+j)
                err := f.SetCellValue(sheetName, cellName, col.DisplayName)
                if err != nil {
                        logger.Error("设置表头失败", cellName, col.DisplayName, err)
                }
        }

        // 设置单元格格式为常规，避免在excel里认为是文本而不能进行函数公式运算
        styleID, _ := f.NewStyle(&excelize.Style{
                NumFmt: 0, // 0 = 常规
        })
        // 检查数据
        if list, ok := results["list"].([]map[string]any); ok {
                logger.Debug("exportXls", "数据行数:", len(list))
                if len(list) > 0 {
                        // 写入数据
                        for i, row := range list {
                                for j, col := range cols {
                                        cellName := fmt.Sprintf("%c%d", 'A'+j, i+2)
                                        value := row[col.Name]
                                        err := f.SetCellValue(sheetName, cellName, value)
                                        if err != nil {
                                                logger.Error("设置单元格失败", cellName, value, err)
                                        }
                                        f.SetCellStyle(sheetName, cellName, cellName, styleID)
                                }
                        }
                        logger.Debug("exportXls", "写入数据完成", len(list))
                } else {
                        logger.Warn("exportXls", "数据列表为空")
                }
        } else {
                logger.Error("exportXls", "无法获取数据列表，results类型:", fmt.Sprintf("%T", results["list"]))
                if results["list"] != nil {
                        logger.Debug("exportXls", "list内容:", results["list"])
                }
        }

        // 设置响应头
        c.Header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*")
        c.Header("Access-Control-Expose-Headers", "Content-Disposition")
        c.Header("Content-Type", "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet")

        // 处理中文文件名编码
        filename := fmt.Sprintf("%s.xlsx", report.Name)
        encodedFilename := url.QueryEscape(filename)
        c.Header("Content-Disposition", fmt.Sprintf("attachment; filename=\"%s\"; filename*=UTF-8''%s", encodedFilename, encodedFilename))
        c.Header("Cache-Control", "no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate")
        c.Header("Pragma", "no-cache")
        c.Header("Expires", "0")

        // 写入响应
        if err := f.Write(c.Writer); err != nil {
                appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
                appG.Error(500, "导出失败", err.Error())
                logger.Error("导出失败", err.Error())
        } else {
                logger.Debug("导出成功")
        }
}

type CreateForm struct {
        Name        string `form:"name"`
        Code        string `form:"code"`
        Type        string `form:"type"`
        Status      string `form:"status"`
        Description string `form:"description"`
        ParentID    uint   `form:"parent_id"`
}
type UpdateForm struct {
        CreateForm
        ID int `form:"id" uri:"id" binding:"required"`
}

func Meta(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        name := c.Params.ByName("ids")
        result := models.Meta(name)
        appG.Success("", result)
}

func Search(c *gin.Context) {
        var f models.SearchForm
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}

        if c.ShouldBindQuery(&f) == nil {
                log.Println(f)
        }

        result, count := models.Search(f)
        for i, report := range result {
                fmt.Printf("report %d: ID=%d, UserID=%d , Name=%s\n", i, report.ID, report.UserID, report.Name)
        }
        appG.Success("", map[string]any{
                "objects": result,
                "total":   count,
        })
}

func Create(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var f CreateForm
        c.ShouldBind(&f)
        //todo 直接自动映射的方法
        var o models.Report
        o.Name = f.Name

        updatedReport, err := models.Update(&o)
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "抱歉未找到相关信息", err.Error())
                return
        }

        appG.Success("", updatedReport)
}
func GetById(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        idParam := c.Params.ByName("id")

        // 检测是否是逗号间隔的多个ID
        if strings.Contains(idParam, ",") {
                // 处理多个ID的情况
                ids := strings.Split(idParam, ",")
                var results []interface{}

                for _, idStr := range ids {
                        idStr = strings.TrimSpace(idStr) // 去除空格
                        if idStr == "" {
                                continue
                        }

                        result, err := models.GetReport(idStr)
                        if err != nil {
                                // 记录错误但继续处理其他ID
                                log.Printf("获取ID %s 失败: %v", idStr, err)
                                continue
                        }

                        results = append(results, result)
                }

                if len(results) == 0 {
                        appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                        return
                }

                appG.Success("", results)
                return
        }

        // 处理单个ID的情况
        result, err := models.GetReport(idParam)
        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                return
        }

        appG.Success("", result)
}

func Delete(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id := c.Params.ByName("id")
        report, err := models.GetReport(id)
        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                return
        }
        err = report.Delete()

        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("删除失败")
                return
        }

        appG.Success("", nil)
}

package roast

import (
        "fmt"
        "hahacking/common/app"
        models "hahacking/models/roast"
        "hahacking/utils"
        "log"
        "strconv"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

// 将烘焙参数保存为配方
func SaveRecipe(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        roastid, _ := strconv.Atoi(c.Param("id"))
        roast, err := models.GetById(uint(roastid))
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "获取烘焙信息失败,id="+strconv.Itoa(roastid), err)
                return
        }
        if roast.Status != models.RoastStatusComplete {
                appG.Error(500, fmt.Sprintf("烘焙状态[%s]未完成，不能保存配方", roast.Status), nil)
                return
        }
        recipe, err := models.SaveRecipeByRoastId(uint(roastid))
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "保存失败"+err.Error(), err)
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", recipe)
}
func SearchRecipe(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        o := map[string]any{}
        var f models.SearchRecipeForm

        if c.ShouldBindQuery(&f) == nil {
                log.Println(f)
        }

        if f.Name != "" {
                o["name"] = f.Name
        }
        if f.Status != "" {
                o["status"] = f.Status
        }
        o["pageNo"] = f.PageNo
        o["pageSize"] = f.PageSize

        result, count := models.SearchRecipe(f)
        appG.Success("", utils.Response{
                "objects": result,
                "total":   count,
        })
}
func UpdateRecipe(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var o models.RoastRecipe
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&o); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "绑定请求中的json到form失败，可能是格式问题", err)
                return
        }
        err := models.UpdateRecipe(&o)
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "更新失败", err)
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", o)
}

func RegisterRoutes(r *gin.RouterGroup) {
        APIs := r.Group("/roast")
        {
                APIs.GET("meta/:ids", Meta)
        }
        userAPI := APIs.Group("")
        userAPI.Use(middleware.JWTAuth())
        userAPI.Use(middleware.BaseAuth("user"))
        {
                userAPI.GET("", Search)
                userAPI.GET(":id", GetById)
                userAPI.GET(":id/profile", GetProfileByRoastId)
                userAPI.POST(":id/saverecipe", SaveRecipe)
                userAPI.POST("start", Start)
                userAPI.POST("complete", Complete)
                userAPI.GET(":id/cancel", Cancel)
                userAPI.GET(":id/delete", Delete)

                userAPI.GET("profile/:id", GetProfileById)
                userAPI.POST("profile", UpdateProfile)

                userAPI.GET("recipe", SearchRecipe)
                userAPI.GET("recipe/:id", GetRecipeById)
                userAPI.POST("recipe", UpdateRecipe)
        }

        adminAPI := APIs.Group("")
        adminAPI.Use(middleware.JWTAuth())
        adminAPI.Use(middleware.BaseAuth("admin"))
        {

        }
}

                appG.Response(200, 1000, formErrs)
                return
        }
        roast, _ := models.GetById(form.ID)
        err := roast.Complete(form)
        if err != nil {
                log.Println("complete error", err)
                appG.Error(500, "完成失败", err)
                return
        }
        roast, _ = models.GetById(form.ID)
        appG.Success("", roast)
}

package routers

import (
        "hahacking/handler/websocket"
        "hahacking/i18n"
        "hahacking/middleware"
        "hahacking/routers/auth"
        "hahacking/routers/baidu"
        "hahacking/routers/bean"
        "hahacking/routers/category"
        "hahacking/routers/common"
        "hahacking/routers/cupping"
        "hahacking/routers/favorite"
        "hahacking/routers/order"
        "hahacking/routers/page"
        "hahacking/routers/price"
        "hahacking/routers/report"
        "hahacking/routers/roast"
        "hahacking/routers/shop"
        "hahacking/routers/tag"
        "hahacking/routers/task"
        "hahacking/routers/user"
        "hahacking/version"
        "time"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

func InitRouter() *gin.Engine {
        r := gin.Default()
        r.NoRoute(func(c *gin.Context) {
                c.JSON(404, gin.H{"code": 404, "message": "Page not found"})
        })
        r.Use(middleware.CustomLogger())
        // r.Use(gin.Logger()) //gin.Default()已经包含了gin.Logger()
        // r.Use(gin.Recovery()) //gin.Default中已经注册了gin.Recovery()
        r.Use(middleware.CORS())
        r.Use(middleware.RateLimit(time.Second, 100, 100))
        r.Use(ErrorHandler())
        r.Use(i18n.Middleware())
        r.Use(middleware.QueryArrayCompatibility())
        RegisterAPI(r)
        RegisterWebSocket(r)
        return r
}
func RegisterWebSocket(app *gin.Engine) error {
        ws := app.Group("/ws")
        ws.GET("", func(c *gin.Context) {
                websocket.HandleConnections(c)
        })
        return nil
}
func ErrorHandler() gin.HandlerFunc {
        return func(c *gin.Context) {
                c.Next()
                if len(c.Errors) > 0 {
                        c.JSON(400, gin.H{
                                "error": c.Errors.Last().Error(),
                        })
                }
        }
}
func RegisterAPI(app *gin.Engine) error {
        apiV1 := app.Group("/")
        // apiV1.Use(middleware.JWTAuth(
        //         "/auth/register",
        //         "/auth/login",
        //         "/auth/refresh", // 添加refresh路径到公开路径列表
        // ))
        baiduAPI := apiV1.Group("/baidu")
        {
                {
                        baiduAPI.GET("text2audio/:text", baidu.Text2Audio)
                }
        }

        apiV1.GET("ping", func(c *gin.Context) {
                c.JSON(200, gin.H{"message": "pong@ api.noso.cafe", "time": time.Now().Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05"),
                        "build_info": version.GetBuildInfo()})
        })

        common.RegisterRoutes(apiV1)
        cupping.RegisterRoutes(apiV1)
        auth.RegisterRoutes(apiV1)
        task.RegisterRoutes(apiV1)
        bean.RegisterRoutes(apiV1)
        roast.RegisterRoutes(apiV1)
        page.RegisterRoutes(apiV1)
        category.RegisterRoutes(apiV1)
        order.RegisterRoutes(apiV1)
        shop.RegisterRoutes(apiV1)
        user.RegisterRoutes(apiV1)
        tag.RegisterRoutes(apiV1)
        price.RegisterRoutes(apiV1)
        report.RegisterRoutes(apiV1)
        favorite.RegisterRoutes(apiV1)
        {
                supplierAPI := apiV1.Group("supplier")
                {
                        {
                                supplierAPI.GET("", bean.SearchSupplier)
                        }
                }

        }
        return nil

}

package shop

import (

)

func RegisterRoutes(r *gin.RouterGroup) {
        APIs := r.Group("/shop")
        {
                APIs.GET("meta/:ids", Meta)
        }
        userAPI := APIs.Group("")
        userAPI.Use(middleware.JWTAuth())
        userAPI.Use(middleware.BaseAuth("user"))
        {
                userAPI.GET("account", SearchAccount)
                userAPI.GET("account/:id", GetAccountById)
                userAPI.POST("account", UpdateAccount)
                userAPI.GET("accountlog", SearchAccountLog)
                userAPI.GET("accountlog/:id", GetAccountLogById)
        }
}

func Meta(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        name := c.Params.ByName("ids")
        result := models.Meta(name)
        appG.Success("", result)
}

type UpdateForm struct {
        ID            uint   `json:"id" form:"id"`
        Name          string `json:"name"`
        Code          string `json:"code"`
        Status        string `json:"status"`
        Type          string `json:"type"`
        Processor     string `json:"processor"`
        Variety       string `json:"variety"`
        Process       string `json:"process"`
        OtherProcess  string `json:"otherprocess"`
        Country       string `json:"country"`
        PurchasePrice string `json:"purchaseprice"`
        BPrice        string `json:"bprice"`
        CPrice        string `json:"cprice"`
        Owner         string `json:"owner"`
}

func (form *UpdateForm) Validate() map[string]string {
        var result = make(map[string]string)
        if form.Name == "" {
                result["name"] = "请填写名称"
        }
        if form.Code == "" {
                result["code"] = "请填写编码"
        } else {

        }

        return result
}

type SearchForm struct {
        utils.Pager
        Name   string `form:"name"`
        Code   string `form:"code"`
        Status string `form:"status"`
}

func GetById(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id, _ := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        o, err := models.GetById(id)

        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", o)
}

func Search(c *gin.Context) {
        var f SearchForm
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}

        if c.ShouldBindQuery(&f) == nil {
                log.Println(f)
        }

        o := map[string]interface{}{}
        if f.Name != "" {
                o["name"] = f.Name
        }
        if f.Status != "" {
                o["status"] = f.Status
        }
        o["pageNo"] = f.PageNo
        o["pageSize"] = f.PageSize
        result, count := models.Search(o)
        appG.Success("", utils.Response{
                "objects": result,
                "total":   count,
        })
}

func Update(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var form UpdateForm
        if err := c.ShouldBind(&form); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "绑定请求中的json到form失败，可能是格式问题", err)
                return
        }
        err := form.Validate()
        if len(err) > 0 {
                appG.Response(200, 1000, err)
                return
        }

        var o models.Shop

        if err := mapstructure.Decode(form, &o); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "映射form内容到对象失败", err)
                return
        }
        o.ID = form.ID
        result := models.Update(&o)
        appG.Success("", result)
}
func Delete(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id, _ := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        err := models.Delete(id)

        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                return
        }

        appG.Success("", nil)
}

import (

type UpdateAccountForm struct {
        ID      uint    `json:"id" form:"id"`
        Name    string  `json:"name"`
        Type    string  `json:"type"`
        Code    string  `json:"code"`
        Balance float64 `json:"balance" gorm:"type:decimal(10,2)"`
        Status  string  `json:"status" `
        ShopID  uint    `json:"shop_id" `
}

func (form *UpdateAccountForm) Validate() map[string]string {
        var result = make(map[string]string)
        if form.Name == "" {
                result["name"] = "请填写名称"
        }
        if form.Code == "" {
                result["code"] = "请填写编码"
        }
        if form.Type == "" {
                result["type"] = "请选择类型"
        }
        if form.Status == "" {
                result["status"] = "请选择状态"
        }

        return result
}

        result, count := models.SearchAccount(f)
        appG.Success("", utils.Response{
                "objects": result,
                "total":   count,
        })
}

func UpdateAccount(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var form UpdateAccountForm
        if err := c.ShouldBind(&form); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "绑定请求中的json到form失败，可能是格式问题", err)
                return
        }
        err := form.Validate()
        if len(err) > 0 {
                appG.Response(200, 1000, err)

package tag

import (
        "hahacking/common/app"
        "hahacking/middleware"
        models "hahacking/models/tag"
        "hahacking/utils"
        "log"
        "strconv"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

func RegisterRoutes(r *gin.RouterGroup) {
        APIs := r.Group("/tag")
        {
        }
        userAPI := APIs.Group("")
        userAPI.Use(middleware.JWTAuth())
        userAPI.Use(middleware.BaseAuth("user"))
        {
                userAPI.POST("add", Add)
                userAPI.GET("remove", Remove)                 //从某个target删除标签
                userAPI.GET("getGroups", GetGroups)           //根据target获取全部相关标签组
                userAPI.GET("getTags", GetTags)               //根据target获取全部相关标签
                userAPI.GET("search", Search)                 //比如某个target的全部标签
                userAPI.GET("searchGroup", SearchGroup)       //搜索所有group
                userAPI.GET("getGroupById/:id", GetGroupById) //搜索所有group
        }
}

func Add(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var form models.AddForm
        if err := c.ShouldBind(&form); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "绑定请求中的json到form失败，可能是格式问题", err)
                return
        }
        formerror := form.Validate()
        if len(formerror) > 0 {
                appG.Response(200, 1000, formerror)
                return
        }
        rr, err := models.Add(form)
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "创建标签失败", err)
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", rr)
}

func Search(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var f models.SearchForm
        if c.ShouldBindQuery(&f) == nil {
                log.Println(f)
        }
        result, count := models.Search(f)
        appG.Success("", utils.Response{
                "objects": result,
                "total":   count,
        })
}

func GetGroups(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id, _ := c.GetQuery("id")
        typ, _ := c.GetQuery("type")
        groups, err := models.GetGroupByTarget(id, typ)
        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", groups)
}
func GetTags(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id, _ := c.GetQuery("id")
        typ, _ := c.GetQuery("type")
        tags, err := models.GetByTarget(id, typ)

        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", tags)
}

func Remove(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id, _ := c.GetQuery("target_id")
        typ, _ := c.GetQuery("target_type")
        tagid, _ := c.GetQuery("tag_id")
        err := models.RemoveTag(tagid, id, typ)
        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("删除标签失败")
                return
        }

        appG.Success("", nil)
}

func Delete(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id, _ := strconv.Atoi(c.Params.ByName("id"))
        err := models.Delete(id)

        if err != nil {
                appG.NotFound("抱歉未找到相关信息")
                return
        }

        appG.Success("", nil)
}

package task

import (
        "hahacking/middleware"
        "hahacking/scheduler"
        "net/http"

        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
)

func RegisterRoutes(r *gin.RouterGroup) {
        APIs := r.Group("/task")
        {
        }
        userAPI := APIs.Group("")
        userAPI.Use(middleware.JWTAuth())
        userAPI.Use(middleware.BaseAuth("user"))
        {
                userAPI.POST("add", Add)
                userAPI.POST("runNow/:id", RunNow)
                userAPI.POST("pause/:id", Pause)
                userAPI.POST("resume/:id", Resume)
        }
}

func Add(c *gin.Context) {
        var req struct {
                ID       string                 `json:"id"`
                Name     string                 `json:"name"`
                Schedule string                 `json:"schedule"`
                Type     string                 `json:"type"`
                Params   map[string]interface{} `json:"params"`
        }
        if err := c.BindJSON(&req); err != nil {
                c.JSON(http.StatusBadRequest, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
                return
        }
        task := &scheduler.Task{
                ID:       req.ID,
                Name:     req.Name,
                Schedule: req.Schedule,
                Type:     req.Type,
                Params:   req.Params,
        }
        err := scheduler.S.AddTask(task)
        if err != nil {
                c.JSON(http.StatusInternalServerError, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
                return
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"message": "task added"})
}

func RunNow(c *gin.Context) {
        id := c.Param("id")
        scheduler.S.RunNow(id)
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"message": "task executed now"})
}

func Pause(c *gin.Context) {
        id := c.Param("id")
        scheduler.S.PauseTask(id)
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"message": "task paused"})
}

func Resume(c *gin.Context) {
        id := c.Param("id")
        err := scheduler.S.ResumeTask(id)
        if err != nil {
                c.JSON(http.StatusInternalServerError, gin.H{"error": err.Error()})
                return
        }
        c.JSON(http.StatusOK, gin.H{"message": "task resumed"})
}

type UpdateForm struct {
        ID           uint    `json:"id"`
        Nickname     string  `json:"nickname"`
        Email        *string `json:"email"`
        Mobile       *string `json:"mobile"`
        Type         string  `json:"type"`
        Status       string  `json:"status"`
        PriceLevelID uint    `json:"pricelevel_id" form:"pricelevel_id"`
        Password     string  `json:"password"`
}

func (form *UpdateForm) Validate() map[string]string {
        var result = make(map[string]string)
        if form.Nickname == "" {
                result["nickname"] = "请填写昵称"
        }
        if form.Email == nil && form.Mobile == nil {
                result["email"] = "请填写邮箱或手机号"
        } else {
                if form.Email != nil {
                        if !utils.IsEmail(*form.Email) {
                                result["email"] = "请填写正确的邮箱"
                        }
                }
                if form.Mobile != nil {
                        if !utils.IsMobile(*form.Mobile) {
                                result["mobile"] = "请填写正确的手机号"
                        }
                }
        }

        return result
}

func Update(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var form UpdateForm
        var o models.User
        formErrors := api.Bind(&form, &o, c)
        if len(formErrors) > 0 {
                appG.Error(400, "表单验证失败", formErrors)
                return
        }
        if form.PriceLevelID == 0 {
                o.PriceLevelID = nil
        }
        o.ID = form.ID //注意：需要手动设置一下，因为model中的id是嵌入的models.Model，在绑定的时候不会自动设置
        err := models.Update(&o)
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "更新失败", err)
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", o)
}

func Remove(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        id := c.Params.ByName("id")
        idInt, err := strconv.Atoi(id)
        user, err := models.GetById(uint(idInt))
        err = user.Remove()
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "删除失败", err)
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", nil)
}

type ModifyPasswordForm struct {
        UserID uint `json:"user_id"`
        //前端输入+sha256后的串
        OldPassword     string `json:"oldpassword"`
        NewPassword     string `json:"newpassword"`
        ConfirmPassword string `json:"passwordconfirm"`
}

func (form *ModifyPasswordForm) Validate() map[string]string {
        var result = make(map[string]string)
        if form.UserID == 0 {
                result["user_id"] = "用户ID不能为空"
                return result
        }
        if form.OldPassword == "" {
                result["oldpassword"] = "请填写旧密码"
        }
        if form.NewPassword == "" {
                result["newpassword"] = "请填写新密码"
        }
        if form.ConfirmPassword == "" {
                result["passwordconfirm"] = "请填写确认密码"
        }
        if form.NewPassword != form.ConfirmPassword {
                result["passwordconfirm"] = "新密码和确认密码不一致"
        }
        //检查密码是否正确
        user, err := models.GetById(form.UserID)
        if err != nil {
                result["user_id"] = "用户不存在"
                return result
        }
        if user.Password != models.CreatePassword(form.OldPassword) {
                result["oldpassword"] = "旧密码不正确"
        }
        return result
}
func ModifyPassword(c *gin.Context) {
        appG := app.Gin{Cxt: c}
        var form ModifyPasswordForm

        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(&form); err != nil {
                appG.Error(400, "绑定请求中的json到form失败，可能是格式问题", err)
                return
        }
        formErrors := form.Validate()
        if len(formErrors) > 0 {
                appG.Error(400, "表单验证失败", formErrors)
                return
        }

        u, err := models.GetById(uint(form.UserID))
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "修改密码失败", err)
                return
        }
        u.Password = models.CreatePassword(form.NewPassword)
        err = models.Update(u)
        if err != nil {
                appG.Error(500, "修改密码失败", err)
                return
        }
        appG.Success("", nil)
}

package scheduler

import (
        "log"
        "time"
)

type Executor interface {
        Execute(task *Task) error
}

type DefaultTaskExecutor struct {
}

func (e *DefaultTaskExecutor) Execute(task *Task) error {
        log.Println("Executing task ID:", task.ID)
        log.Println("Executing task Name:", task.Name)
        log.Println("Executing task Schedule:", task.Schedule)
        log.Println("Executing task Type:", task.Type)
        log.Println("Executing task Params:", task.Params)
        return nil
}

type TestTaskExecutor struct {
}

func (e *TestTaskExecutor) Execute(task *Task) error {
        log.Printf("Archiving logs for task: %s, Params: %+v", task.Name, task.Params)
        time.Sleep(1 * time.Second)
        log.Printf("Log archival completed for task: %s", task.Name)

        return nil
}

package scheduler

import (
        "fmt"
        "log"
        "sync"

        "github.com/robfig/cron/v3"
)

var S *Scheduler

func Init() {
        S = NewScheduler()
        RegisterExecutors()
        S.Start()
        // defer S.Stop()
}

func RegisterExecutors() {
        S.RegisterExecutor("default", &DefaultTaskExecutor{})
        S.RegisterExecutor("test", &TestTaskExecutor{})
}

type Scheduler struct {
        c         *cron.Cron
        mu        sync.Mutex
        taskIDs   map[string]cron.EntryID
        tasks     map[string]*Task
        status    map[string]string
        executors map[string]Executor
}

func (s *Scheduler) RegisterExecutor(taskType string, executor Executor) {
        s.executors[taskType] = executor
}

func NewScheduler() *Scheduler {
        return &Scheduler{
                c:         cron.New(cron.WithSeconds()),
                taskIDs:   make(map[string]cron.EntryID),
                tasks:     make(map[string]*Task),
                status:    make(map[string]string),
                executors: make(map[string]Executor),
        }
}

func (s *Scheduler) Start() {
        log.Println("Scheduler starting...")
        s.c.Start()
        log.Println("Scheduler started")
}

func (s *Scheduler) Stop() {
        s.c.Stop()
}
func (s *Scheduler) GetTasks() map[string]*Task {
        s.mu.Lock()
        defer s.mu.Unlock()
        return s.tasks
}

func (s *Scheduler) AddTask(t *Task) error {
        // s.mu.Lock()
        // defer s.mu.Unlock()

        executor, exists := s.executors[t.Type]
        if !exists {
                return fmt.Errorf("unknown task type: %s", t.Type)
        }
        id, err := s.c.AddFunc(t.Schedule, func() {
                log.Printf("Running task: %s", t.Name)
                if err := executor.Execute(t); err != nil {
                        log.Printf("Failed to execute task %s: %v", t.Name, err)
                } else {
                        log.Printf("Task %s executed successfully", t.Name)
                        s.status[t.ID] = "success"
                        s.RemoveTask(t.ID)
                }
        })
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }
        // 再加锁，操作map
        s.mu.Lock()
        defer s.mu.Unlock()
        s.taskIDs[t.ID] = id
        s.tasks[t.ID] = t
        s.status[t.ID] = "running"
        return nil
}
func (s *Scheduler) RemoveTask(taskID string) {
        s.mu.Lock()
        defer s.mu.Unlock()
        if id, ok := s.taskIDs[taskID]; ok {
                s.c.Remove(id)
                delete(s.taskIDs, taskID)
        }
        delete(s.tasks, taskID)
        delete(s.status, taskID)
}

func (s *Scheduler) RunNow(taskID string) {
        s.mu.Lock()
        // defer s.mu.Unlock()
        t := s.tasks[taskID]
        executor, exists := s.executors[t.Type]
        s.mu.Unlock()
        if !exists {
                fmt.Errorf("unknown task type: %s", t.Type)
        }
        if err := executor.Execute(t); err != nil {
                log.Printf("Failed to execute task %s: %v", t.Name, err)
        } else {
                log.Printf("Task %s executed successfully", t.Name)
                s.status[t.ID] = "success"
                s.RemoveTask(t.ID)
        }
}

func (s *Scheduler) PauseTask(taskID string) {
        s.mu.Lock()
        defer s.mu.Unlock()

        if id, ok := s.taskIDs[taskID]; ok {
                s.c.Remove(id)
                s.status[taskID] = "paused"
        }
}

func (s *Scheduler) ResumeTask(taskID string) error {
        if t, ok := s.tasks[taskID]; ok {
                return s.AddTask(t)
        }
        return nil
}

func (s *Scheduler) RetryTask(taskID string) {
        s.RunNow(taskID) // 简单实现，等同于立即执行
}

func (s *Scheduler) Status(taskID string) string {
        s.mu.Lock()
        defer s.mu.Unlock()
        return s.status[taskID]
}

package acl

import (
        "fmt"
        "hahacking/database"
        "log"
        "strings"

        "github.com/casbin/casbin/v2"
        gormadapter "github.com/casbin/gorm-adapter/v3"
)

var Enforcer *casbin.Enforcer
var MenuEnforcer *casbin.Enforcer

func Setup() {
        db := database.GetConn()
        adapter, _ := gormadapter.NewAdapterByDB(db)
        // 通过mysql适配器新建一个enforcer
        // 注意：可能出现数据库中有脏数据导致这里初始化失败的情况
        // Enforcer, err := casbin.NewEnforcer("config/rbac_model.conf", adapter)
        Enforcer, err := casbin.NewEnforcer("config/rbac_model_with_domain.conf", adapter)
        if err != nil {
                log.Println("create enforcer err", err)
        }
        // 日志记录
        Enforcer.EnableLog(true)

        Enforcer.AddFunction("isSuper", func(arguments ...interface{}) (i interface{}, e error) {
                if len(arguments) == 1 {
                        user := arguments[0].(string)
                        return IsSuperAdmin(user), nil
                }
                return nil, fmt.Errorf("superMatch error")
        })
        Enforcer.AddFunction("keyMatch", KeyMatchFunc) //支持通配符 *

        MenuEnforcer, _ = casbin.NewEnforcer("config/rbac_menu_model.conf", adapter)
        MenuEnforcer.EnableLog(true)
}
func KeyMatch(key1 string, key2 string) bool { //https://www.cnblogs.com/yjf512/p/12200206.html
        i := strings.Index(key2, "*")
        if i == -1 {
                return key1 == key2
        }

        if len(key1) > i {
                return key1[:i] == key2[:i]
        }
        return key1 == key2[:i]
}

func KeyMatchFunc(args ...interface{}) (interface{}, error) {
        name1 := args[0].(string)
        name2 := args[1].(string)

        return (bool)(KeyMatch(name1, name2)), nil
}

func IsSuperAdmin(userName string) bool { //只是为了演示添加方法
        var admins = []string{"root", "admin"}
        for _, user := range admins {
                if user == userName {
                        return true
                }
        }
        return false
}

package api

import (
        "github.com/gin-gonic/gin"
        "github.com/mitchellh/mapstructure"
)

type Form interface {
        Validate() map[string]string
}

func Bind(form Form, model any, c *gin.Context) map[string]string {
        var result = make(map[string]string)
        if err := c.ShouldBindJSON(form); err != nil {
                result["form"] = "字段绑定格式错误 - " + err.Error()
                return result
        }
        result = form.Validate()
        if len(result) > 0 {
                return result
        }

        if err := mapstructure.Decode(form, &model); err != nil {
                result["form"] = "映射form内容到对象失败"
                return result
        }

        return nil
}

package baidu

import (
        "bytes"
        "encoding/json"
        "hahacking/common/logger"
        "io"
        "net/http"
        neturl "net/url"
        "os"
        "path/filepath"
        "time"
)

const (
        cacheFile = "/tmp/baidu_token.json"
)

type TokenResponse struct {
        AccessToken string `json:"access_token"`
        ExpiresIn   int    `json:"expires_in"`
}

type CachedToken struct {
        Token     string `json:"token"`
        ExpiresAt int64  `json:"expires_at"`
}

/*
参考：短文本在线合成 https://cloud.baidu.com/doc/SPEECH/s/mlbxh7xie
*/

func Text2Audio(text string) ([]byte, error) {
        token, err := GetToken(false)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        url := "https://tsn.baidu.com/text2audio?tex=" + text + "&tok=" + token + "&cuid=1234567890&ctp=1&lan=zh&aue=3"
        // 创建HTTP客户端
        client := &http.Client{
                Timeout: 30 * time.Second,
        }
        req, err := http.NewRequest("GET", url, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded")
        resp, err := client.Do(req)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer resp.Body.Close()
        body, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        return body, nil
}

type RecognizeResponse struct {
        // 根据百度API的实际返回结构定义字段
        // 这里只是一个示例，需要根据实际API响应调整
        Result []struct {
                Name  string  `json:"name"`
                Score float64 `json:"score"`
        } `json:"result"`
}

func Recognize(imageBase64 string) (*RecognizeResponse, error) {
        // 获取token
        token, err := GetToken(true)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }

        // 构建请求URL
        url := "https://aip.baidubce.com/rest/2.0/image-classify/v1/classify/ingredient?access_token=" + token
        logger.Sugar.Info("url", url)
        formData := neturl.Values{}
        formData.Set("image", imageBase64)

        // 创建HTTP客户端
        client := &http.Client{
                Timeout: 30 * time.Second,
        }

        // 创建请求
        req, err := http.NewRequest("POST", url, bytes.NewBufferString(formData.Encode()))
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }

        // 设置请求头
        req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded")

        // logger.Sugar.Info("req", req.URL.String())
        // 发送请求
        resp, err := client.Do(req)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer resp.Body.Close()
        logger.Sugar.Info("resp", resp)
        logger.Sugar.Info("resp.body", resp.Body)
        // 读取响应
        body, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
        if err != nil {
                logger.Sugar.Error("io.ReadAll", err)
                return nil, err
        }
        logger.Sugar.Info("body", string(body))
        // 解析响应
        var recognizeResp RecognizeResponse
        err = json.Unmarshal(body, &recognizeResp)
        if err != nil {
                logger.Sugar.Error("json.Unmarshal", err)
                return nil, err
        }
        logger.Sugar.Info("recognizeResp", recognizeResp)

        return &recognizeResp, nil
}

func GetToken(cache bool) (string, error) {
        // 尝试从缓存获取token
        if cache {
                if token := getCachedToken(); token != "" {
                        return token, nil
                }
        }

        // 如果没有缓存或已过期，获取新token
        tokenResp, err := fetchToken()

        if err != nil {
                return "", err
        }

        // 缓存新token
        if cache {
                err = cacheToken(tokenResp.AccessToken, tokenResp.ExpiresIn)
                if err != nil {
                        return "", err
                }
        }

        return tokenResp.AccessToken, nil
}

func getCachedToken() string {
        // 检查缓存文件是否存在
        if _, err := os.Stat(cacheFile); os.IsNotExist(err) {
                return ""
        }

        // 读取缓存文件
        data, err := os.ReadFile(cacheFile)
        if err != nil {
                return ""
        }

        var cached CachedToken
        if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &cached); err != nil {
                return ""
        }

        // 检查token是否过期
        if cached.ExpiresAt <= time.Now().Unix() {
                return ""
        }

        return cached.Token
}

func cacheToken(token string, expiresIn int) error {
        cached := CachedToken{
                Token:     token,
                ExpiresAt: time.Now().Unix() + int64(expiresIn) - 60, // 提前60秒过期
        }

        data, err := json.Marshal(cached)
        if err != nil {
                return err
        }

        // 确保目录存在
        dir := filepath.Dir(cacheFile)
        if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0755); err != nil {
                return err
        }

        return os.WriteFile(cacheFile, data, 0644)
}

func fetchToken() (*TokenResponse, error) {
        //hardcode:
        //佳泰电子秤
        //116157830
        apiKey := "TWSV3mFtv3TMTpULqBM12Jwb"
        secretKey := "l7BMRXdQEWYZ3vNQxCKp4SvXnw4lAdRp"

        client := &http.Client{
                Timeout: 30 * time.Second,
        }

        url := "https://aip.baidubce.com/oauth/2.0/token?client_id=" + apiKey + "&client_secret=" + secretKey + "&grant_type=client_credentials"

        req, err := http.NewRequest("POST", url, nil)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }

        req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
        req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/json")

        resp, err := client.Do(req)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }
        defer resp.Body.Close()

        body, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }

        var tokenResp TokenResponse
        err = json.Unmarshal(body, &tokenResp)
        if err != nil {
                return nil, err
        }

        return &tokenResp, nil
}

package cache

import (
        "time"

        "github.com/allegro/bigcache/v3"
)

var GlobalCache *bigcache.BigCache
